In manipulation with ordered phases, router entropy from a phase-supervised soft mixture-of-experts (MoE) is insufficient: the same value can reflect either a normal adjacent-phase handoff or support for nonadjacent or earlier phases. We propose MoE-DAgger, which uses causal router history to distinguish these cases without providing the policy with a phase label, progress variable, or hard phase command during execution. The acquisition rule allows an adjacent transition to settle, but requests correction when high entropy persists outside such a transition, the transition times out, or routing persistently favors an earlier phase. Under matched active human-control budgets, MoE-DAgger achieves 84.0% task success on Green and 78.0% on Red, exceeding phase-unaware filtered entropy by 22 percentage points on Green and 26 points on Red, with no observed collisions in 50 rollouts per task. On 50 shared intervention-free traces per task, 83.3% and 80.0% of its first proposals fall on failed rollouts within predefined failure windows when 15 and 25 traces are selected, compared with 70.0% and 72.0% for temporal action-chunk disagreement and 63.3% and 60.0% for ensemble disagreement. Shadow replay and an active no-veto ablation indicate that suppressing queries at normal adjacent handoffs is a key contributor to the improved allocation.

</details>


### [37] [DreamVLA: From Generated Human Motions to Humanoid Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10872)
*Kevin Park,Izat Rasyad,Dvij Kalaria,Shankar Sastry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamVLA 利用生成的运动轨迹引导仿真中的强化学习，产生可靠的任务执行轨迹来训练 VLA 模型，并用参考条件化的 DAgger 蒸馏缓解分布偏移，从而绕开昂贵的全身人形遥操作数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略通常依赖大量机器人示教数据，人类遥操作成本高且繁琐，全身人形交互尤为突出；仿真强化学习虽可扩展数据，但设计任务专属奖励函数困难。

Method: 用生成的运动轨迹作为参考引导仿真强化学习，将其转化为可靠的任务执行轨迹以在仿真中训练 VLA；核心是参考条件化 DAgger 蒸馏：学习器 rollout 失败后，从采样的学习器状态出发、沿用相同参考后缀重启 RL 专家，收集成功的纠正轨迹并聚合。

Result: 在从平台拾取、开抽屉、按键、行走并从平台拾取等任务上评估，并做了 DAgger 迭代次数、残差 RL 与生成数据集规模的消融实验。

Conclusion: 以生成轨迹引导仿真 RL 并结合参考条件化 DAgger 蒸馏，可在无需新任务专属遥操作数据的情况下训练 VLA 模型，并缓解离线模仿带来的分布偏移。

Abstract: Training vision-language-action (VLA) policies typically depends on substantial robot demonstration data, making human teleoperation a costly and cumbersome bottleneck, particularly for whole-body humanoid interaction. Reinforcement learning in simulation offers an alternative for scaling this data, but designing task-specific rewards remains challenging. To address this, we propose DreamVLA, which uses generated motion trajectories to guide reinforcement learning (RL) in simulation, converting them into reliable task execution trajectories. These trajectories train VLA models in simulation without new task-specific teleoperation data. Our central contribution is a reference-conditioned DAgger distillation procedure: after a failed learner rollout, we restart the RL expert from a sampled learner state with the same reference suffix and aggregate successful corrective trajectories, addressing the distribution shift left by offline imitation. We evaluate picking from a platform, opening a drawer, pressing a button, and walking and picking from a platform, with ablations of DAgger iterations, residual RL, and generated dataset scale. Project website: ember-center-berkeley.github.io/DreamVLA.

</details>


### [38] [Sampling-based Model Predictive Control as Sequential Monte Carlo Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10877)
*Austin Garrett,Muyang Yan,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文证明预测采样、MPPI、交叉熵方法及其偏置、有色噪声、退火等变体都可统一为同一个序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）推断算法，由提议分布、更新规则和重采样ESS阈值三个独立轴共同决定，并在多个机器人控制任务上验证了重采样与可编程提议分布的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样型MPC目前被视为一族彼此独立的算法（predictive sampling、MPPI、交叉熵方法及各类变体），它们都通过采样控制序列并按代价重加权来工作，但缺乏统一理解，导致难以分析其共性缺陷（如粒子退化）与系统性改进方向。

Method: 将采样型MPC表述为SMC推断算法，由三个正交维度参数化：(1) 提议分布(proposal)；(2) 将代价加权粒子归约为名义计划的更新规则；(3) 触发重采样的有效样本量(ESS)阈值。在MuJoCo非线性控制、障碍场开环导航、7自由度运动规划、非抓取式push-T任务上评估，涵盖仿真与Franka FR3真机实验。

Result: 重采样可缓解长时域下的粒子退化：在导航域28-knot时域下，碰撞率相比无重采样基线降低30%。可编程提议分布显著提升push-T成功率，相比扰动式基线，仿真中从1%提升至100%，真机上从10%提升至90%。

Conclusion: 采样型MPC的各算法族可在SMC框架下统一，三轴设计空间为算法选择与改进提供了清晰指导；重采样与可编程提议分布是应对粒子退化和提升任务成功率的关键机制，且在真机环境中有效。

Abstract: Sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) is treated as a family of distinct algorithms: predictive sampling, MPPI, the cross-entropy method, and their biased, colored-noise, and annealed variants all sample control sequences and reweight them by cost. We show a single Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) inference algorithm implements them all, specified by three independent axes: the proposal, the update rule that reduces cost-weighted particles to a nominal plan, and the effective-sample-size threshold for resampling. We evaluate SMC-based planning on MuJoCo non-linear control, open-loop navigation through obstacle fields, 7-DoF motion planning, and non-prehensile push-T, in simulation and on a Franka FR3. Resampling combats particle degeneracy at long horizons. At a 28-knot horizon in our navigation domain it cut the collision rate by 30% over non-resampling baselines. Programmable proposals improve push-T success over perturbative baselines, improving from 1% to 100% in simulation and 10% to 90% on hardware.

</details>


### [39] [Zero-Shot VLA Adaptation with Retrieved Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10894)
*Subin Kim,Anh-Quan Pham,Dinesh Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAGu 将检索增强生成（RAG）思想引入视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型：通过几何标定把域不匹配的野外示教轨迹变换到目标机器人设置，再用由粗到细的检索挑选最兼容的轨迹，并在流匹配推理过程中用其引导动作生成，从而显著提升跨域操作成功率（真实世界 20%→80%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模示教数据预训练的 VLA 模型在通用操作上表现不俗，但对相机视角、背景、物体身份或机器人形态等较小的域偏移仍然脆弱。受语言模型中 RAG 成功经验启发，作者希望利用来自域不匹配的野外示教数据（如 DROID）的检索结果，来指导 VLA 在新目标域中的行为，从而在无需重新训练基础模型的情况下提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 RAGu，针对机器人场景下 RAG 的特有挑战给出三点设计：(1) 引入简单的几何标定，将源域示教变换到目标机器人设置，缩小跨域差异；(2) 采用由粗到细的检索策略，在预先变换（pre-warped）的候选轨迹大池中选出与基础 VLA 最兼容的一条；(3) 用检索到的轨迹引导 VLA 的流匹配（flow-matching）推理过程，而不是重训基础模型或扩展其有限的上下文窗口。

Result: 在真实世界、仿真、仿真到现实迁移以及跨形态人形机器人操作上进行了评测：真实世界 VLA 成功率由 20% 提升到 80%，在仿真中优于最接近的对比基线。

Conclusion: 检索增强是提升 VLA 跨域泛化的一条有效且轻量路径：通过几何对齐解决机器人域的物理差异、通过由粗到细检索控制候选规模与兼容性、通过推理期引导避免昂贵重训，即可在多样场景中显著提升操作成功率。

Abstract: Pre-training large parametric "Vision-Language-Action" (VLA) models on large demonstration datasets has recently yielded promising results for general-purpose manipulation. However, these models still struggle under even small domain shifts relative to the training data, such as changes in camera viewpoint, background, object identity, or robot morphology. Motivated by the success of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) in language models, we pursue retrieval to boost VLA performance. Specifically, we seek to leverage retrieval from domain-mismatched, "in-the-wild" demonstration datasets like DROID to guide a VLA's behavior in a new target domain. To achieve this, we introduce RAGu, which overcomes robotics-specific challenges for RAG: (1) It incorporates simple geometric calibration to transform source demonstrations into the target setup and reduce cross-domain differences. (2) It uses a coarse-to-fine retrieval strategy that selects, from a large pool of pre-warped candidates, the trajectory most compatible with the base VLA. (3) It guides the VLA's flow-matching inference procedure rather than seeking to retrain the base model or expand its limited context window. We evaluate RAGu across real-world, simulation, sim-to-real transfer, and cross-embodiment humanoid manipulation. We find it improves real-world VLA success rates from 20% to 80% and outperforms the closest-related baselines in sim. Project website: raguvla.github.io

</details>


### [40] [Beating the Pool, Not the Game: A White-Box Equilibrium Agent for Language-Based Economic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10932)
*Sudhanshu Srivastava,Faisal Aqlan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出规则型智能体 sudsho，在 GLEE 竞赛中以零推理成本完成议价、谈判与说服，基于同池历史对局分位数设定出价目标，共 117,280 局取得 2056 总评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大模型的智能体在推理阶段需频繁调用模型，成本高且行为难以核验；本文希望在议价、谈判、说服这类游戏上以确定性规则实现低成本的强表现，并保证行为可审计。

Method: 游戏按相对同一配置历史对局池的收益分位数计分；据此用池分位数设定需求目标，并用受约束的均衡份额上下界限制出价与接受阈值；除说服环节的混合策略外，每步动作都是可见状态的确定性函数，因此可将冻结后的动作日志重放以验证一致性。另通过与对手池的三个冻结快照重放，做消融以评估池感知约束项的贡献。

Result: 117,280 局整体评分 2056，推理成本为零；以同策略复制的账号计，在 71 个上榜账号中排第 26。重放实验显示池感知约束项带来每局议价评分平均 274 至 330 分的提升，且在记录对手决策可固定重放的情境下依然成立。

Conclusion: 规则型、零推理的智能体在议价谈判类游戏中可与 LLM 方案竞争，且确定性设计便于对齐核验；对手池感知的约束机制是其性能提升的主要来源。

Abstract: We describe sudsho, a rule-based agent for the GLEE Competition that plays bargaining, negotiation, and persuasion with zero inference-time LLM calls. Games are scored as payoff percentiles against an empirical pool of prior play on the same configuration, so pool percentiles set our demand targets while clamped equilibrium shares bound demands and acceptance. Every move is a deterministic function of the visible state (persuasion mixing aside), so alignment is verified by replaying the post-freeze move log through the policy. Across 117,280 games the agent reached an overall rating of 2056 at $0 inference cost, and the account, ranked by its best agent (a same-policy replica), placed 26th of 71 ranked accounts. A replay against three frozen snapshots of the opponent pool credits the pool-aware clamp with 274 to 330 points of mean per-game bargaining rating, an effect that holds where logged opponent decisions fix the replay.

</details>


### [41] [BRACE: Belief-guided Residual Adaptive Control for Safe Navigation under Unknown Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10938)
*Jaeyoun Choi,Niklas Schmid,Oswin So,Augustinos Saravanos,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BRACE是一个分层强化学习框架，用于未知、有界、空间变化的扰动下的到达-规避导航：从模型预测误差推断扰动信念，并据此调节高层策略以自适应修正参考轨迹，性能优于MPC与RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主导航中扰动常随空间变化且不连续（障碍物改变风向、地形改变附着）。跟踪控制器通常假设有足够控制权限以抑制扰动并保证安全，而该假设失效的情形研究不足，本文针对这一区间展开。

Method: 提出Belief-guided Residual Adaptive Control（BRACE）分层RL框架：由模型预测误差推断扰动信念（belief），并用其调节高层策略以调整参考轨迹；策略由名义项与信念引导的残差项组成，联合训练并采用似然比优势重加权启发式。

Result: 在三个具有突变风场的四旋翼仿真环境中评估，并在Crazyflie实机上用风扇产生扰动验证；各设置下BRACE在到达-规避性能上优于MPC和RL基线。

Conclusion: BRACE表明将扰动信念注入分层策略并对残差进行信念引导调整，是在控制权限受限、时空变化扰动下提升到达-规避导航的有效途径，并具实机可行性。

Abstract: Disturbances encountered during autonomous navigation generally vary across space, often discontinuously, as obstacles redirect wind and changing terrain alters traction. Tracking controllers typically assume sufficient control authority to mitigate disturbances and maintain safety; the regime where this assumption fails remains underexplored. We present Belief-guided Residual Adaptive Control (BRACE), a hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL) framework for reach-avoid navigation under unknown, bounded, spatially varying disturbances. BRACE infers a disturbance belief from model prediction errors and uses that to condition a high-level policy that adapts the reference trajectory. The policy combines a nominal and a belief-guided residual term, trained jointly with a likelihood-ratio advantage reweighting heuristic. We evaluate BRACE in three simulated quadcopter environments with abruptly switching wind fields, and validate it on Crazyflie hardware with fan-generated disturbances. Across these settings, BRACE improves reach-avoid performance over model predictive control and RL baselines.

</details>


### [42] [Explicitly capturing demonstrator multi-modality for distillation into diffusion policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10949)
*Shao Yuan Chew Chia,Rebecca H. Jiang,Abhinav Agarwal,Andreas Hofmann,Leslie Pack Kaelbling,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将vine sampling引入行为克隆数据采集：对同一观测重复查询示教者$k$次，可蒸馏出更好保留条件动作分布多模态性与多样性的扩散策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏扩散策略的输出多样性本身即有价值——测试时搜索与策略改进都需要探索，因此希望把多模态示教者蒸馏成能复现其条件动作分布的扩散策略。但滑动窗口式行为克隆数据会把唯一观测历史与动作配对，这类数据既可由多模态策略拟合，也可由高Lipschitz常数的单模态策略拟合；已有工作发现稀疏数据上训练的扩散策略倾向后者，对每个观测只记忆一个动作。

Method: 将vine sampling迁移到行为克隆的数据采集流程：在示教者访问的部分状态上，从完全相同的观测出发查询示教者$k$次，从而在数据中显式提供同一观测下的多个动作，消除上述歧义。并在合成任务上系统研究$k$（每观测动作数）对学习策略的影响。

Result: 合成任务上，更大的$k$使学到的策略更好地匹配示教者的条件分布，包括其多模态性与多样性；在2D卡车卸货任务上，vine sampling得到的策略更接近示教者，并显著提升best-of-$N$与专家迭代（expert iteration）的表现。

Conclusion: 数据中同一观测的动作样本数量是扩散策略能否蒸馏出多模态、多样化行为的关键因素；采用vine sampling采集行为克隆数据是一种简单有效的做法，可直接惠及测试时搜索与策略改进等下游应用。

Abstract: The diversity of a distilled diffusion policy's outputs matters beyond how it impacts the policy's success rate. In particular, downstream applications like test-time search and policy improvement require exploration. Thus, we want to distill multi-modal demonstrators into diffusion policies which reproduce their conditional action distributions. Standard data collection for behavior cloning applies a sliding window to demonstration episodes, which pairs unique observation histories with actions. These datasets can be fit by a policy that is either multi-modal, or unimodal with high Lipschitz constant to capture a diversity of actions seen at nearby observation samples. Prior work finds that diffusion policies trained on sparse data learn the latter, memorizing one action per observation. We adapt vine sampling to behavior cloning data collection to resolve this ambiguity: at some states the demonstrator visits, query it $k$ times from exactly the same observation. On a synthetic task, we study how the number of actions per observation, $k$, affects the learned policy, and find that larger $k$ yields a policy that better matches the demonstrator's conditional distribution, including its multi-modality and diversity. On a 2D truck unloading task, vine sampling likewise leads to a policy closer to the demonstrator and substantially improves both best-of-$N$ and expert iteration performance.

</details>


### [43] [BAT-WAM: Bridging Action and Touch in World-Action Models for Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10967)
*Dong Fang,Feixuan Huang,Yuanxin Zhong,Yuxiang Gao,Qi Song,Yunlong Wang,Xiaosong Jia,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BAT-WAM 通过视觉条件连续隐桥接关节状态与触觉两路码本，实现缺失模态下的触觉重建与预测，并在真机灵巧操作中优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）的灵巧操作依赖手部构型与接触信息，但关节状态与全手触觉观测可能部分或完全缺失；RGB 图像虽有场景上下文与手物几何，却不足以可靠恢复这些缺失模态。作者进一步观察到手部状态与触觉信息之间存在相关性，由此提出跨模态桥接的思路。

Method: BAT-WAM 为触觉流与动作流分别学习独立的码本与解码器；用一个由 RGB 条件驱动的连续隐桥连接两流，实现双向学习；视觉信息作为连续上下文提供约束，从而在缺失模态时互为指导。

Result: 在 HTBench 协议下，完全掩码时触觉重建 RMSE 相对图像条件化的 HandTouch 检查点降低 34.73%，部分掩码时降低 38.84%；以目标时刻 RGB 与历史动作为条件的未来触觉预测，平均 RMSE 相对 OpenWAM with HandTouch 降低 85.56%。真机实验与未见物体/场景的泛化实验中均稳定优于相关 WAM 基线。

Conclusion: 结果支持将视觉上下文、手部状态与触觉联合建模，作为多模态世界动作模型的一种有效表示方法。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with world action models (WAMs) relies on hand configuration and contact information, yet joint-state and whole-hand tactile observations may be partially or entirely missing. RGB images provide scene context and hand-object geometry but are insufficient to reliably recover these missing modalities. We further observe a correlation between hand state and tactile information. Motivated by this observation, we introduce BAT-WAM, a framework that bridges visual, joint-state, and tactile representations for WAMs. BAT-WAM learns separate codebooks and decoders for the tactile and action streams. An RGB-conditioned continuous latent bridge connects the two streams for bidirectional learning, while visual information serves as continuous context. Under the HTBench evaluation protocol, BAT-WAM reduces the root mean squared error (RMSE) of tactile reconstruction by 34.73% under fully masked conditions and 38.84% under partially masked conditions, relative to an image-conditioned HandTouch checkpoint. For future tactile prediction conditioned on target-time RGB and historical actions, it reduces mean RMSE by 85.56% relative to OpenWAM with HandTouch. Real-robot experiments further show that BAT-WAM consistently outperforms related WAM baselines in dexterous manipulation. Generalization experiments on unseen objects and scenes also demonstrate substantial improvements over baseline models. These findings support joint modeling of visual context, hand state, and touch as a representation approach for multimodal WAMs.

</details>


### [44] [RoboMoCap: A Large-Scale Whole-Body Motion Capture Dataset with Egocentric Vision for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10994)
*Mayur Deshmukh,Aaron Tung,Junyi Li,Nikash Bhardwaj,Ademi Adeniji,Vincent Liu,Michael Cho,Jiafei Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboMoCap：面向人形机器人学习的、以第一人称视觉+光学动捕同步的大规模多模态数据集，覆盖下肢运动与全身操作行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需协调移动与操作，从人类示范中学习该能力需要精确全身运动与第一人称（egocentric）视觉配对，尤其下肢动作和落脚点常在第一人称视角中不可见；而现有第一人称数据集多缺乏高精度光学动捕或对多样移动-操作行为的系统覆盖，故需构建新数据集填补空白。

Method: 构建 RoboMoCap 数据集：14 名受试者、超过 200 小时记录，含 6 个头戴相机、腕部视觉与惯性传感、120 Hz 标记点式全身光学动捕；系统设计采集内容，覆盖多种移动原语、动态全身运动与移动-操作任务（变速行走、转向、受限导航、地形穿越、物体搬运、移动中操作）；提供时间对齐的多模态观测与度量尺度全身运动表示；并在三项基准任务上系统评测现有方法：第一人称全身运动重建、足部接触与落脚点估计、人到人形机器人的运动重定向。

Result: 提供时间对齐的多模态数据与度量全身运动表示，可研究不可直接观测身体区域的运动；在三项基准任务上给出对现有方法的系统评测基线；数据在受试者规模、时长、模态精度与行为覆盖度上具备优势。

Conclusion: 将第一人称视觉与高保真人体运动在多样移动与交互场景中配对，RoboMoCap 为第一人称运动理解及从人类示范学习人形机器人行为提供了基础数据与评测支撑。

Abstract: Humanoid robots must coordinate locomotion and manipulation to navigate complex environments and perform everyday tasks. Learning such behaviours from human demonstrations requires accurate whole-body motion paired with egocentric vision, particularly for lower-body movements and foot placements that are frequently invisible from a first-person perspective. However, existing egocentric datasets often lack high-fidelity optical motion capture or systematic coverage of diverse loco-manipulation behaviours. To address this gap, we introduce RoboMoCap, a large-scale multimodal dataset combining egocentric vision with marker-based optical motion capture, specifically designed for locomotion-centric humanoid learning. RoboMoCap comprises over 200 hours of recordings from 14 participants, featuring six head-mounted cameras, wrist-mounted visual and inertial sensing, and optical full-body motion capture at 120 Hz. The dataset systematically covers diverse locomotion primitives, dynamic whole-body movements, and loco-manipulation tasks, including variable-speed walking, turning, constrained navigation, terrain traversal, object transportation, and manipulation during locomotion. It provides temporally aligned multimodal observations and metric whole-body motion representations, enabling the study of human movement beyond directly observable body regions. We systematically evaluate existing methods on three benchmark tasks: egocentric full-body motion reconstruction, foot contact and placement estimation, and human-to-humanoid motion retargeting. By pairing egocentric vision with high-fidelity human motion across diverse locomotion and interaction scenarios, RoboMoCap provides a foundation for egocentric motion understanding and humanoid learning from human demonstrations.

</details>


### [45] [Floquet-Parameterized Koopman-MPC for Tracking Periodic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11050)
*Kartik A. Pant,Vishnu Vijay,Yufei Zhu,Xintong Deng,Taewoo Nam,Inseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息（physics-informed）的Koopman学习方法，将周期轨道稳定性（基于Floquet理论的单值矩阵约束）直接嵌入学习的线性预测器中，并结合MPC实现非线性系统的周期轨道跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性、强耦合系统（如系留风筝）的实时周期轨道跟踪控制困难，且真实模型参数难以获取；纯数据驱动方法在Koopman学习中缺乏物理一致性，泛化与外推能力受限。

Method: 利用周期轨道的周期性结构及周期状态-输入轨迹数据，在提升（可观）空间中学习线性预测器；借助Floquet理论，用单值矩阵（整周期的状态转移矩阵）刻画给定轨道的周期稳定性信息；在最小二乘EDMD框架下施加开环单值矩阵约束，得到Floquet参数化的线性预测器；再基于该预测器设计模型预测控制（MPC）律以跟踪周期参考。

Result: 在周期性驱动力作用下的cartpole系统上评估该框架，并与纯数据驱动基线方法进行对比。

Conclusion: 将Floquet周期稳定性先验嵌入EDMD可约束搜索空间、改善Koopman线性预测器的学习效果，进而支撑用于周期参考跟踪的MPC设计。

Abstract: Designing real-time control for nonlinear systems to track periodic orbits can be challenging, especially for complex and coupled dynamical systems (e.g., tethered kites), where the true model parameters are hard to obtain. To address this, we propose a physics-informed Koopman learning approach that incorporates periodic-orbit stability directly into the learned linear predictor. The approach combines the underlying periodic structure of the dynamics along with periodic state-input trajectory data to learn physically consistent linear predictors (in lifted/observable space). We leverage Floquet theory, which studies the stability of periodic orbits in nonlinear systems, to obtain the periodic stability information of a given orbit via a monodromy matrix (i.e., the state transition matrix over an entire period). Subsequently, we impose open-loop monodromy constraints to learn Floquet-parameterized linear predictors using a least-squares Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) formulation, thereby constraining the search space and aiding in Koopman learning. We then utilize these linear predictors to design a model predictive control (MPC) law for tracking periodic references. Finally, we evaluate the designed framework on a cartpole system under periodic forcing and compare it against a purely data-driven baseline.

</details>


### [46] [ObjectWorld: Scaling Object-Centric World Models with A Large-Scale 3D Full-Object Flow Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11085)
*Haonan Chen,Sicheng Isabella Lu,Haitian Li,Jiajin Wu,Hexiang Wei,Jiawei Zhang,Xin Fei,Yiwen Hou,Chenrui Tie,Chongkai Gao,Sheng Wang,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"3D全物体流"(3D full-object flow)作为面向机器人操作的中间表示，将稠密3D轨迹赋予目标物体完整表面（含遮挡区域），并构建1210小时数据集训练可预测全物体运动的生成式世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从大规模视觉数据中学习生成式世界模型是实现可扩展机器人操作的可行路径，但这需要一个既能大规模监督、又能直接用于下游控制的中间表示。已有的可见表面物体流无法刻画遮挡区域，导致物体运动信息不完整，也难以统一处理刚性与可变形物体。

Method: 将可见表面物体流扩展为覆盖完整物体几何的稠密3D轨迹表示，对刚性/可变形物体统一适用；构建1210小时全物体流数据集，融合真实视频与物理仿真，并提出可扩展的标注流程从真实观测中恢复全物体运动，以及用于系统评估标注质量的质量度量。随后训练一个世界模型，以图像和任务描述为输入，在重建的物体几何上预测稠密全物体流。

Result: 实验显示标注质量可靠、流预测具备数据可扩展性，并在新场景与新机器人本体上实现有效的下游操作。

Conclusion: 全物体流是一种可大规模监督且可直接用于控制的物体中心运动表示，配合大规模数据集与可扩展标注流程，能够支撑数据驱动的世界模型并泛化到未见场景与不同机器人本体。

Abstract: Generative world modeling from large-scale visual data offers a promising path toward scalable robot manipulation. This calls for an intermediate representation that can be supervised at scale and remains actionable for downstream control. We propose 3D full-object flow, an object-centric motion representation that assigns dense 3D trajectories to points across the complete surface of a target object, including its occluded regions. By extending visible-surface object flow to the full object geometry, this representation makes complete object motion explicit and applies uniformly to both rigid and deformable objects. To realize the potential of this representation at scale, we introduce a 1210-hour full-object flow dataset that addresses the scarcity of large-scale supervision for complete 3D object motion. The dataset integrates diverse real-world videos and physical simulations, with a scalable annotation pipeline for recovering full-object motion from real-world observations and quality metrics for systematically assessing annotation quality. We then train a world model that takes an image and a task description as input and predicts dense full-object flow over the reconstructed object geometry. Experiments show reliable annotation quality, data-scalable flow prediction, and effective downstream manipulation across novel scenes and robot embodiments. Project website with additional results and videos: https://nus-lins-lab.github.io/ObjectWorld/.

</details>

*Takayuki Osa,Kazuki Ota*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种异步推理与控制框架，在不修改底层策略学习过程的前提下加速视觉控制策略（含VLA模型）的任务执行，通过将不连续模型预测生成连续目标位置建模为跟踪控制问题，并结合基于预测位置方差的自适应动作跳过来进一步提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉控制策略（如视觉-语言-动作模型）动作推理计算开销大，导致机器人执行速度受限。作者希望在控制层面（不修改策略学习过程）解决这一问题。

Method: 1）提出异步推理与控制框架，使推理与执行并行以加快任务执行，同时保持运动平滑稳定；2）将从不连续模型预测生成连续目标位置建模为跟踪控制问题，使异步推理在真实机器人上可行；3）基于预测位置的方差进行自适应动作跳过，进一步加速执行。实验采用Action Chunking Transformer和OpenVLA-OFT在双臂机器人上验证。

Result: 在双臂机器人上，使用Action Chunking Transformer与OpenVLA-OFT的实验显示执行速度获得显著提升，同时保持较高的任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-based control policies, including vision-language-action models, have enabled robots to perform increasingly complex tasks. However, their execution speed is often limited by the high computational cost of action inference. In this work, we investigate what can be achieved at the control level without modifying the underlying policy learning process. We introduce an asynchronous inference and control framework that enables fast task execution while maintaining smooth and stable robot motions. By formulating the generation of continuous target positions from discontinuous model predictions as a tracking control problem, our method makes asynchronous inference practical for real robots. We further accelerate execution through adaptive action skipping based on the variance of predicted positions. Experiments with Action Chunking Transformer and OpenVLA-OFT on a bimanual robot demonstrate substantial speedups while preserving high task success rates.

</details>


### [48] [Large-Scale Hierarchical Decision-Making in Robot Control via ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11090)
*Kai Pfeiffer,Jia Wang,Gordon Owusu Boateng,Yuquan Wang,Vincent Bonnet,Abderrahmane Kheddar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对基于ℓ0范数的稀疏分层非线性规划(SH-NLP)问题，提出一种ADMM方法求解序贯稀疏分层二次规划(S-SHQP)子问题，在低至中等精度下相比结构利用的内点法计算时间最多降低23.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S-SHQP配合基于内点法的SHQP子问题求解器虽能利用分层稀疏结构，但难以在前后连贯的控制步之间进行有效热启动，限制了机器人运动学/动力学控制中的实时性与效率。

Method: 构建交替方向乘子法(ADMM)求解SHQP子问题；为表示ℓ0范数代理的辅助变量引入高效替换；通过按合适顺序计算线性代数表达式，高效处理可分离决策函数。

Result: 在大规模运动学与动力学控制实验中，于低至中等精度区间，计算时间相较结构利用的内点法最多减少23.5%；并将分层决策扩展到人形机器人行走的全身动力学控制，指出该场景对高精度解的需求。

Conclusion: ADMM方案在低至中等精度下效率更优且利于热启动，但高精度需求场景(如人形机器人行走的全身动力学控制)仍具挑战，需进一步关注求解精度。

Abstract: Sparse hierarchical nonlinear programming (SH-NLP) based on the $\ell_0$-norm provides a powerful framework for integrated, efficient hierarchical decision-making in robot kinematic control, for example in the presence of a large set of possible end-effector candidate locations. While Sequential Sparse Hierarchical Quadratic Programming (S-SHQP) with interior-point method based SHQP sub-problem solvers exploit the hierarchical, sparse structure of such problems, they are less amenable to effective warm-starting between subsequent, coherent control steps. In this work, we formulate an Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) to efficiently solve related SHQP sub-problems. We introduce efficient substitutions for auxiliary variables representing $\ell_0$-norm surrogates. The solver efficiently handles separable decision functions by evaluating linear algebra expressions in the appropriate order. Results in large-scale kinematic and dynamic control show reduced computation times of up to 23.5% compared with a structure-exploiting interior-point method in low- to moderate-accuracy regimes. Furthermore, hierarchical decision-making is extended to whole-body dynamic control for humanoid walking, where the need for high-accuracy solutions is highlighted.

</details>


### [49] [HARP-Bench: Benchmarking Robot Manipulation Around Active Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11123)
*Qinhong Zhou,Riley Zong,Chunru Lin,Diyi Hu,Shivraj Singh Bhatti,Hongxin Zhang,Lin Qiu,Hanqing Zeng,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HARP-Bench，一个把"人类活跃参与"视为操作问题本身而非背景变化的机器人操作基准，涵盖 Cueing、Contribution、Interruption、Co-presence 四种人机共享工作空间情形，并评估 VLA、模仿学习与启发式策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准普遍假设机器人是唯一主动智能体、任务语义仅由指令和物体状态决定，忽略了真实世界中人类在同一工作空间活动这一常见变量（隐式目标提示、部分完成任务、临时改变物体可用性、限制安全运动）。

Method: 构建 HARP-Bench：将人类在场的操作归为四类条件（Cueing 提示、Contribution 贡献、Interruption 打断、Co-presence 共处），在日常操作任务中实例化，并提供与标准操作配对的人在场变体以做受控比较；同时给出面向动态人类在场场景的演示生成流水线。

Result: 在 HARP-Bench 上评估 VLA 模型、模仿学习方法与启发式策略：人类在场变体对现有策略构成明显挑战，且更高任务成功率并不必然带来更好的安全性；定量失败分析显示操作与恢复是主要剩余难点。使用 HARP-Bench 演示训练可提升仿真中的人在场表现；单任务族真实世界研究显示在 Contribution 下性能提升，但在 Interruption 下未见提升。

Conclusion: 人类在场应被作为操作问题的一部分来建模与评测；该基准揭示了现有策略在安全性与恢复能力上的不足，验证了演示数据的初步效用，同时表明向真实场景迁移仍存在局限。

Abstract: Recent robot manipulation benchmarks have enabled systematic evaluation of general-purpose robot policies across diverse objects, scenes, instructions, and embodiments. However, most existing benchmarks still assume that the robot is the only active agent and that task semantics are determined primarily by the instruction and object state. This assumption overlooks a common source of real-world variation: humans actively sharing the workspace. In such settings, humans may reveal an implicit goal, complete part of the task, temporarily change object availability, or constrain safe robot motion while working nearby. We introduce HARP-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating robot manipulation policies in workspaces shared with active humans. HARP-Bench treats human activity as part of the manipulation problem, rather than as background scene variation. It organizes human-present manipulation into four conditions: Cueing, Contribution, Interruption, and Co-presence. These conditions are instantiated through everyday manipulation tasks, including paired human-present variants for controlled comparison with standard manipulation. HARP-Bench also provides a demonstration-generation pipeline for dynamic human-present settings. We evaluate VLA models, imitation-learning methods, and heuristic policies on HARP-Bench. Human-present variations challenge current policies, and higher task success does not consistently improve safety. Quantitative failure analysis identifies manipulation and recovery as the dominant remaining challenges. Training with HARP-Bench demonstrations improves human-present performance in simulation. A real-world study on one task family shows improved performance under human contribution, but not interruption, providing initial evidence of the data's utility and identifying limits of transfer. Code is available at https://github.com/UMass-Embodied-AGI/harp.

</details>


### [50] [From Festival Video to the Stage: A Sim-to-Real Ladder for Long-Horizon Expressive Whole-Body Dance on a Commercial Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11127)
*Hesham Shehata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套从单目视频学习传统日本阿波舞并部署到 Unitree G1 EDU 人形机器人的完整流水线，结合人体网格恢复、运动学重定向、强化学习运动跟踪与 ONNX 板载部署，并通过十一级硬件部署阶梯验证三项关键干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是让商业人形机器人掌握具有文化特征的传统舞蹈动作，同时填补从单目视频采集到真实硬件稳定执行之间的工程与可靠性空白，尤其是训练—部署链路上的隐性故障与状态估计器漂移问题。

Method: 流水线包括：单目视频的人体网格恢复（HMR）、运动学重定向到 G1 机器人、仿真中基于强化学习的运动跟踪训练（单张 RTX 5070 GPU）、以及通过 ONNX 进行板载部署。构建十一版本部署阶梯在硬件上评估三项干预：检测并修复部署产物的静默损坏、建模训练期间状态估计器漂移、编辑参考运动以实现稳定执行与受控收尾。

Result: 最终策略实现：无绳连续三次完成 43 秒舞蹈；带自动交接的 46 秒表演；由采集动作组合的连续 9.7 分钟无绳表演。现场交接中单次远程请求即可让机器人从厂商行走控制器启动并返回，测得无力矩窗口为 45–140 ms。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了可复现的低成本训练与部署流程及训练动力学与硬件表现分析，并指出失效边界：柔性地面对执行的影响以及单目采集中的遮挡问题。

Abstract: We present a pipeline for transferring Awa Odori, a traditional Japanese festival dance, from monocular video to a commercial Unitree G1 EDU humanoid. The pipeline combines human mesh recovery, kinematic retargeting, reinforcement learning for motion tracking in simulation, and onboard deployment via ONNX. Training ran on a single RTX 5070 GPU. An eleven-version deployment ladder evaluated on hardware examines three interventions. We detect and repair silent corruption of deployment artifacts, model state-estimator drift during training, and edit the reference motion to support stable execution and a controlled ending. The final policies achieved three consecutive rope-free completions of a 43-second routine, a 46-second performance with automatic handoff, and a continuous 9.7-minute rope-free performance composed from the captured motion. In a live handover, a single remote request let the robot start from and return to the manufacturer's walking controller on stage, with a measured torque-free window of 45-140 ms. We report the training dynamics and hardware outcomes and discuss failure boundaries involving compliant flooring and occlusion in monocular capture.

</details>


### [51] [MOSU2: Long-range Navigation System with Multi-modal Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11148)
*Jing Liang,Zhenyu Hou,Senthurbavan Kirubaharan,Md Safwan Mondal,Ashok Gudivada,Xuesu Xiao,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MOSU2 是一个面向公共户外长距离导航的两阶段轨迹选择系统：第一阶段做上下文推理判断是否需要更多证据，仅在交互无法解析时才触发第二阶段检索几何与动态信息并与场景上下文对齐，从而在实时性与准确性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外长距离移动机器人不能只避障，还需判断地形适配性以及在行人、自行车、车辆、交通信号和临时管制面前决定前进、让行或绕行。这些决策依赖场景演化线索（如车是否减速、骑行者是否会横穿），而 VLM 虽能解读这类线索，却只能基于缺乏度量几何与运动的图像推理；每步都补充这类证据对实时导航太慢，手工设计的触发规则也无法覆盖户外情境的开放性多样性。

Method: 提出 MOSU2，采用两阶段轨迹选择：在 MOSU 基础上，沿 GPS 路线规划行进，并通过多模态可通行性分析筛选候选轨迹后选定轨迹驱动机器人。第一阶段进行上下文推理，判断何时需要额外证据、需要检索什么；仅当某交互仍未解析时才触发第二阶段，检索几何与动态信息，并与场景上下文对齐以做出导航决策。

Result: 在涵盖停止手势、施工场地、人行横道以及与车辆、自行车、行人共享道路的真实世界实验中，相比其单图像前身，MOSU2 将平均导航成功率从 61.8% 提升到 94.2%，条件检测准确率从 72.2% 提升到 91.4%，平均决策延迟约为前身的 2.2 倍。

Conclusion: 按需检索几何与动态证据的两阶段设计可显著提升户外长距离导航的成功率与情境条件检测准确率，并以可控的延迟代价换取更强的开放式户外场景适应能力。

Abstract: Robots that travel long distances through public outdoor spaces must do more than avoid obstacles: they must remain on terrain suited to their mobility and decide when to proceed, yield, or detour around pedestrians, cyclists, vehicles, traffic signals, and temporary restrictions. These decisions are difficult because their correctness often depends on how a scene evolves, such as whether an approaching car is slowing or a cyclist will cross the robot's path. Vision-language models (VLMs) can interpret such cues, but they reason over images that lack metric geometry and motion. Supplying this evidence at every step is too slow for real-time navigation, and hand-crafted triggers cannot anticipate the open-ended variety of outdoor situations. We present MOSU2, a long-range navigation system with two-stage trajectory selection, in which first-stage contextual reasoning determines when additional evidence is needed and what to retrieve. Only when an interaction remains unresolved does the first stage trigger the second stage to retrieve geometric and dynamic information and align it with scene context to make navigation decisions. Building on MOSU, MOSU2 follows routes from GPS-based route planning and filters candidate trajectories through multi-modal traversability analysis before selecting a trajectory to move the robot. In real-world experiments spanning stop gestures, construction sites, crosswalks, and roads shared with vehicles, cyclists, and pedestrians, MOSU2 raises mean navigation success from 61.8% to 94.2% and condition-detection accuracy from 72.2% to 91.4% over its single-image predecessor, at about 2.2 times its mean decision latency. Project website: https://jinggm.github.io/mosu2/

</details>


### [52] [Tutorial: Vision-Based Formation Control with Differential-Drive Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11164)
*Christian Marowski,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇教学教程，介绍如何用差速驱动机器人、俯视相机和Python实现多机器人编队控制，覆盖硬件软件搭建、位姿估计、编队几何、反馈控制、系统集成与误差评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人教育中理论理解与实际实现之间存在鸿沟，需要一份从数学概念到实体机器人协同行为的完整实践指南。

Method: 使用俯视相机与ArUco标记估计机器人位置与朝向；以期望的成对位移向量定义圆形编队；比较实测与期望相对位置，控制器计算运动向量并转换为差速驱动轮速指令，同时引入基于接近传感器的简单避障；系统性地串联硬件软件搭建、位姿估计、编队几何、反馈控制与系统集成。

Result: 提供一套可运行的完整实现流程与编队误差评估方法，并配以开源GitHub仓库（含Python代码、搭建说明与演示），支撑动手学习。

Conclusion: 尽管实现基于特定机器人平台，其感知与编队控制原理可迁移至其他差速驱动机器人与仿真环境，有助于弥合理论教学与实践之间的差距。

Abstract: This tutorial presents a step-by-step introduction to multi-robot formation control using differential- drive robots, an overhead camera, and Python. ArUco markers are used to estimate robot positions and orientations, while desired pairwise displacement vectors define a circular formation. By comparing measured and desired relative positions, the controller computes movement vectors that are converted into differential-drive wheel commands, with basic proximity-sensor-based obstacle avoidance. The tutorial guides readers through hardware and software setup, robot pose estimation, formation geometry, feedback control, system integration, and formation-error evaluation. Motivated by the need to bridge the gap between theoretical understanding and practical implementation in robotics education, we emphasize how mathematical concepts are translated into coordinated behaviors on physical robots. Although the implementation uses a specific robotic platform, the underlying perception and formation-control principles can be adapted to other differential-drive robots and simulation environments. The accompanying open-source GitHub repository provides the Python implementation, setup instructions, and demonstrations to support hands-on learning and further exploration of multi-robot systems.

</details>


### [53] [DexForesight: Grounding Dexterous Policies with Implicit Spatial Foresight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11166)
*Zhongxi Chen,Yi Yang,Xinyi Chen,Mingzhe Ye,Yaping Li,Zhihong Liu,Congsheng Xu,Haonan Wang,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexForesight 通过在灵巧操作策略的潜空间中注入动力学感知的世界表征，隐式地学习空间前瞻能力；部署时仅保留策略，无需深度输入或显式未来预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作需要对周围场景与机器人手部具备丰富的空间前瞻能力，而现有 world-action 模型通常依赖显式的未来预测来获取这类预测性知识，成本高且在部署时负担重。

Method: 训练阶段：从离线场景深度、手部运动学几何与示教轨迹中学习一个以动作为条件的三维空间世界模型，预测三维手部点运动与视觉特征变化（涵盖铰接手部动力学、场景变化及被遮挡手指的运动）。随后冻结该世界模型，将策略查询与其预测性世界查询对齐，把学到的空间动力学迁移到策略潜变量中，用于条件化动作生成。部署阶段仅保留策略，输入为 RGB 观测、语言指令与机器人状态。

Result: 在 11 个 DexJoCo 仿真任务上，整体成功率由基线策略的 49.4% 提升至 60.5%；在三个真实机器人灵巧操作任务上成功率达 58.3%，最强基线为 50.0%。

Conclusion: 空间前瞻可以通过表征对齐被隐式注入策略，而非依赖部署时的显式未来预测；该方法在仿真与真机上均带来稳定提升，且不增加部署时的传感器与推理开销。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires rich spatial foresight over both the surrounding scene and the robot hand. Existing world-action models often acquire such predictive knowledge through explicit future prediction. We introduce DexForesight, a framework that learns spatial foresight implicitly by injecting dynamics-aware world representations into the latent space of a dexterous policy. During training, DexForesight learns an action-conditioned spatial world model from offline scene depth, kinematic hand geometry, and demonstration trajectories. The world model predicts 3D hand-point motion and visual feature changes, capturing both articulated hand dynamics and scene changes under demonstrated actions, including the motion of fingers occluded from the cameras. We then freeze the world model and align policy queries with its predictive world queries, transferring the learned spatial dynamics into policy latents and using them to condition action generation. At deployment, only the policy is retained, operating on RGB observations, language, and robot state without depth input or explicit future prediction. On 11 DexJoCo simulation tasks, DexForesight improves the overall success rate of the base policy from 49.4% to 60.5%. On three real-robot dexterous manipulation tasks, DexForesight achieves 58.3% success, compared with 50.0% for the strongest baseline.Project page https://dexforesight.github.io/

</details>


### [54] [DeepSoMo: Deformable Object Motion Estimation under Proprioceptive Partial Observability via Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11242)
*Hanwen Zhao,Weicheng Dai,Luoyao Chen,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeepSoMo，从潜在动力学视角解决本体感知部分可观测下的软体变形运动估计问题，通过自监督潜在预测约束增强潜在时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人中变形体的运动估计面临部分可观测性挑战：内部本体感知观测只能间接反映全局运动、接触与形状演化，单帧观测信息不足，需要时序一致的潜在表示才能实现准确估计。

Method: 将变形运动估计建模为潜在动力学学习问题，提出DeepSoMo框架，通过自监督潜在预测约束在潜在动力学中施加预测结构，使潜在状态保持对未来观测的可预测性；以高保真有限元仿真作为可控真值来源，在典型变形动力学设定下评估。

Result: 与架构匹配的循环基线相比，DeepSoMo在轨迹估计、姿态恢复、形状重建和接触推断方面均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 潜在时序一致性（潜在状态对未来观测的预测能力）是长时程变形运动估计的关键要求，显式施加预测性约束能有效改善本体感知下的软体运动估计性能。

Abstract: Estimating the motion of deformable bodies in soft robotics from proprioceptive sensing is challenging due to partial observability, as internal observations provide only indirect access to global motion, contact, and shape evolution. In such settings, instantaneous observations are insufficient, and accurate estimation requires temporally consistent latent representations. This paper studies deformable motion estimation under proprioceptive partial observability from a latent dynamics perspective. We argue that a key requirement for long-horizon estimation is latent temporal consistency, where latent states remain predictive of future observations. To this end, we present DeepSoMo, a learning-based framework that enforces predictive structure in latent dynamics through a self-supervised latent prediction constraint. We evaluate the approach in a canonical deformable dynamics setting using high-fidelity finite-element simulation as a controlled source of ground truth. Results demonstrate consistent improvements in trajectory estimation, orientation recovery, shape reconstruction, and contact inference over architecture-matched recurrent baselines.

</details>


### [55] [Single-Radar Bilateral 3D Near-Field Imaging for Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11296)
*Yingqi Wang,Tingwei Chen,Zhongqin Wang,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种运动辅助的毫米波雷达框架，利用单个前向安装雷达在机器人单次前向行驶中，重建轨迹两侧的三维结构，并通过多层高度提取获得较高的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家用移动机器人需要可靠的近场三维感知，但仅靠单个前向毫米波雷达同时恢复路径两侧信息十分困难，原因包括近场波前曲率、目标可见性随视角变化以及俯仰维角度多样性不足。

Method: 建立近场模型，用收发端到散射体的球面路径将场景反射率与跨频率、跨位置的步进频率MIMO观测关联；采用双侧发射组合实现宽带方向加权；利用滑动孔径反投影按近场传播距离进行相干聚焦；以Top-K功率融合整合不同可见性的局部图像；在重建结果上进行多层高度提取，并用原始MIMO信道做带保护的航向自适应高度精化。

Result: 在受控环境、办公室/书房和走廊三类场景中，横向-前向平均定位误差分别为4.65、5.63和5.98厘米，峰值背景比均超过13 dB。

Conclusion: 该框架仅使用单个雷达、在单次前向通过中即可恢复双侧三维散射结构，且不依赖场景的学习式表征。

Abstract: Mobile robots operating in homes need reliable 3D perception of nearby furniture and surrounding structures, yet recovering this information on both sides of the robot's path with a single forward-mounted millimeter-wave radar remains challenging because of near-field wavefront curvature, viewpoint-dependent target visibility, and limited elevation diversity. This paper presents a motion-assisted framework for reconstructing structures on both sides of a robot's trajectory. Our near-field model relates scene reflectivity to stepped-frequency multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) observations across frequencies and robot positions using spherical paths from transmitter (Tx) to scatterer to receiver (Rx). Bilateral Tx combining provides wideband directional weighting, while sliding-aperture back-projection coherently focuses the measurements using near-field propagation distances. Top-K power fusion combines local images with different visibility. The reconstruction provides lateral-forward support for multi-layer height extraction, followed by guarded adaptive height refinement using the original MIMO channels. Experiments in controlled, office/study, and corridor environments produce mean lateral-forward localization errors of 4.65, 5.63, and 5.98 cm, respectively, with peak-to-background ratios (PBRs) exceeding 13 dB. The framework recovers bilateral 3D scattering structure from one forward pass using a single radar, without relying on learned representations of the scene.

</details>


### [56] [When Coding Agents Get Arms: Safety Risks Across the Digital-Physical Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11438)
*Yiming Sun,Mi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 ArmedAgent，首个系统评估"前沿模型作为编码智能体零样本端到端控制真实机械臂"所带来的双重安全风险（能力滥用与间接提示注入跨域传导）的基准，覆盖 36 项任务、5 个场景，并在仿真与真实机械臂上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间理解能力的进步使前沿模型可驱动编码智能体零样本、端到端操控真实机械臂，无需任务示范或再训练。这带来两类新风险：一是模型广泛知识使智能体可完成早期 VLA/WAM 范式无法完成的任务，从而产生新的能力滥用途径；二是编码智能体可访问网页、邮件等数字资源，其中的间接提示注入等威胁会在其控制机器人时传导至物理世界。现有工作缺乏对这两类风险的系统评测。

Method: 构建 ArmedAgent 基准：覆盖鲁棒性、恶意使用、间接注入三个维度，包含 5 个场景下的 36 项任务；在仿真环境中评测，并在真实机械臂上做验证；其中相当一部分任务专门针对早期 VLA 范式无法实现的危害性行为。在多种主流智能体框架下测试多个前沿模型（含 GPT-6-Astra）。

Result: 在当前系统上观察到显著的安全风险：前沿模型驱动的编码智能体在鲁棒性、恶意使用与间接注入三类评测中均暴露出明显漏洞，且数字资源中的注入威胁可成功传导到物理世界行为。

Conclusion: 零样本机器人控制智能体开辟了新的安全评估空间，需要同时考虑"能力越强带来的滥用面扩大"与"数字—物理跨域注入传导"两类风险；ArmedAgent 为该方向提供了首个系统化基准，并表明现有前沿系统安全性不足。

Abstract: Advances in spatial understanding have enabled frontier models to power coding agents that control a real robot arm zero-shot and end-to-end, without task-specific demonstrations or retraining. This brings two new safety challenges. First, the extensive knowledge of frontier models lets these agents perform tasks that earlier VLA and WAM paradigms could not, opening the door to new forms of capability misuse. Second, coding agents can access digital resources such as web pages and emails. Threats in these resources, such as indirect prompt injection, can carry over into the physical world when the agent controls a robot. We therefore introduce ArmedAgent, the first systematic benchmark to evaluate both risks for coding agents performing zero-shot robot control. It covers robustness, malicious use, and indirect injection across 36 tasks in 5 scenarios, evaluated in simulation and validated on a physical robot arm. A distinctive portion of the tasks target harmful behaviors that earlier VLA paradigms could not perform. Testing several frontier models, including GPT-6-Astra, under widely used agent frameworks, we find substantial safety risks in current systems.

</details>


### [57] [Off-Policy RL with Online Inference Tuning: My Solution to the CoRL 2026 RoboEval Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11464)
*Ilia Larchenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个面向CoRL 2026 RoboEval挑战的双臂任务策略方案：通用部分为基于π_0.5的"自价值"off-policy强化学习（AWR+RECAP，直接优化官方评分优势，并在rollout中同步进行约20个推理参数的Thompson采样），任务特定部分补充局部探索无法获得的数据（满分生成演示与从自身失败出发的恢复episode）。最终在留出种子上达到0.990 RDS，在官方种子上达到0.987。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoboEval挑战不仅评估是否成功，还按过程评分：每个阶段需安全、高效、双臂协调，并与人类演示对比，因此策略必须优化完整的分阶段评分而非二元成功信号。作者希望把此前LeHome方案中的通用RL方法迁移到该任务上，并解决纯局部探索无法发现关键行为（如满分轨迹、失败恢复）的问题。

Method: 方法分为两部分。(1) 通用部分：在π_0.5策略上做off-policy RL，使该策略同时充当自身价值函数；用AWR和RECAP以"精确官方评分"的优势为信号训练；训练循环中每次rollout同时充当对约20个推理参数的Thompson采样试验。(2) 任务特定部分：额外注入数据，包括能达到完全奖励的生成演示，以及从策略自身失败状态起始的恢复episode。

Result: 通用部分开箱即用即在留出种子上两天内达到0.85 RDS，且远未收敛；叠加任务特定数据后，提交策略在留出种子上达到0.990 RDS，在官方种子上达到0.987 RDS。

Conclusion: 将策略与价值函数合一的off-policy RL、以官方评分为优势信号、结合推理参数Thompson采样，是一种高效的通用框架；但要达到接近满分，还需用生成演示与失败恢复数据补足局部探索的盲区。

Abstract: The CoRL 2026 RoboEval challenge has eight bimanual tasks, and it ranks a policy not only on whether it succeeds but on how: every stage of the task, done safely, efficiently and with both arms in coordination, scored against human demonstrations. My solution has two parts. The generic part, carried over from my LeHome challenge solution, is off-policy RL on a $π_{0.5}$-based policy that is also its own value function. It is trained with AWR and RECAP on advantages of the exact official score, in a loop where every rollout is at the same time a Thompson-sampling trial of about 20 inference parameters. Out of the box, it took the policy to 0.85 RDS on held-out seeds in two days (and it was still very far from convergence). The competition-specific part adds data that local exploration cannot find: generated demonstrations that reach the full reward, and recovery episodes that start from the policy's own failures. The submitted policy reaches 0.990 RDS on held-out seeds and 0.987 on the official seeds.

</details>


### [58] [Beyond Memory Limits: A Hierarchical Spatio-Temporal Map Representation for Large-Scale 3D Autonomous Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11489)
*Yizhe Zhang,Jianping Li,Yilun Gao,Liangliang Yin,Pengfei Wan,Bisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLAVOR 是一种层次化时空框架，将稠密几何限制在机器人局部地图内，用轻量观测记录与紧凑全局任务上下文替代全局可查询地图，实现内存有界的长时域无人机自主探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 UAV 探索系统大多维护全局可查询的几何地图用于运动规划与探索决策，导致内存消耗与空间查询开销随探索面积增长；而仅用机器人中心的局部建图虽能限制内存与计算，却丢失了长时域探索所需的全局任务上下文。因此需要兼顾内存有界与全局任务连续性的表示方案。

Method: 框架分为三层：观测层用轻量 LiDAR 关键帧可见性记录，保留逐射线证据以支持 frontier 推理；任务层将超出局部地图范围、尚未解决的 frontier 保存为关联里程计锚点的紧凑待办任务；拓扑层用稀疏拓扑图连接锚点并保留路径以供回访。返回任务区域时，由观测重建 frontier 与探索视点，拓扑路径在当前局部地图上实例化并验证后执行。

Result: 在完成全部三个仿真场景的最优基线对比中，探索时间缩短 31.98%–41.88%，地图内存占用降低 94%–99%。真实部署中，相对于显式点云 ikd-tree，在森林场景内存占用降低 92.67%，建筑工地场景降低 62.42%，并在受限机载资源下保持长期任务连续性。

Conclusion: 稠密地图容量可随探索扩展保持固定，与探索相关的内存增长仅局限于紧凑的观测与任务上下文，验证了层次化时空表示在受限机载资源下实现长时域自主探索的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV exploration is increasingly used for large-scale surveying and inspection in inaccessible or disaster-affected environments, where onboard memory and computational resources are often severely constrained. Most existing UAV exploration systems maintain globally queryable geometric map representations for motion planning and exploration decision-making, causing memory consumption and spatial-query costs to increase with the explored area. Robot-centric local mapping bounds dense-map memory and computation but discards global task context needed for long-horizon exploration.We present FLAVOR, a hierarchical spatio-temporal framework that confines dense metric geometry to a robot-centric local map while retaining compact global observation and task context. At the observation level, lightweight LiDAR-keyframe visibility records preserve ray-wise evidence for frontier reasoning. At the task level, unresolved frontiers beyond the local-map extent are retained as compact pending tasks associated with odometry anchors. A sparse topological graph connects these anchors through retained routes for future revisits.Upon returning to a task region, FLAVOR reconstructs the frontier and exploration viewpoint from observations, while the topological route is instantiated and validated against the current local map before execution. Dense-map capacity remains fixed as exploration expands, with exploration-dependent memory growth confined to compact observation and task context.FLAVOR achieves 31.98--41.88% shorter exploration time and a 94--99% smaller map memory footprint than the best baseline that completes all three simulated scenes. In real-world deployments, it reduces this footprint by 92.67% in the forest and 62.42% at the construction site relative to an explicit point-cloud ikd-tree while preserving long-term task continuity under constrained onboard resources.

</details>


### [59] [Review the Episodes, Refine the Lessons: Simple Memory Evolution for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11509)
*Yifei Yao,Yude Zou,Xing Gao,Guanbo Jiao,Yukai Wang,Jianming Ma,Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimplEvo 让多模态大模型在完全不更新权重、不做后训练的情况下，仅靠自写的文本记忆（任务记忆＋有界工作记忆）直接控制具身智能体，在 EBench 上将 GPT-6-Astra 成功率从 46.7% 提升到 81.7%，并在 RoboDojo 排行榜上取得 69.63% SR / 75.32 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体研究多围绕 MLLM 做"外挂工程"（后训练策略、递归自我改进循环等），但这些附加组件究竟是否超越了 MLLM 的原始能力仍不清楚。作者希望剥离这些复杂脚手架，探究在直接控制、仅保留自写文本执行经验的条件下，MLLM 自身还能提升到什么程度。

Method: 提出 SimplEvo：不更新权重、不使用后训练策略、不依赖特权信息。MLLM 先从单次演示中写出一份无坐标的任务记忆；在严格训练-测试协议下，仅在训练回合中构建有界工作记忆，并在每回合后依据程序、完成与恢复三方面的经验教训进行精炼；两类记忆在测试时保持固定不变。

Result: 在 EBench 上，SimplEvo 将 GPT-6-Astra 直接控制下的成功率从 46.7% 提升至 81.7%；在 RoboDojo 排行榜以 69.63% SR 和 75.32 分登顶。与同时存储历史视觉帧的记忆方案相比，仅用文本记忆即可达到相近成功率且消耗更少 token；记忆固定时还能泛化到未见布局与随机化场景配置；GPT-6-Astra 写出的工作记忆可原样迁移给更弱的 MLLM 并直接提升其表现。

Conclusion: MLLM 的性能提升很大一部分可归因于其原生能力配合简洁的文本经验记忆，而非复杂的外部脚手架；文本形式的有界记忆高效、可泛化且可跨模型迁移，为具身智能体设计提供了更轻量的替代路线。

Abstract: Recent studies of embodied agents based on frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) develop harness engineering around the MLLM, such as harnesses with post-trained policies and recursive self-improvement loops. Yet whether these additions exceed the native capability of the MLLM remains unexplored. We introduce SimplEvo to explore how far an MLLM can improve under direct control when it retains its execution experience in self-written text memories. It requires no weight updates, post-trained policies, or privileged information. The MLLM writes a coordinate-free task memory once from a single demonstration. Under a strict train-test protocol, it then creates a bounded working memory on training episodes only and refines it after each episode with lessons on procedure, completion, and recovery. Both memories stay fixed at test time. On EBench, SimplEvo lifts the success rate (SR) of GPT-6-Astra under direct control from 46.7% to 81.7%, and tops the RoboDojo leaderboard with 69.63% SR and a score of 75.32. Compared with memories that also store past visual frames, SimplEvo reaches a similar SR with text memory alone while using fewer tokens. With its memories fixed, SimplEvo also generalizes to unseen layouts and randomized scene configurations. Moreover, the working memory transfers across models: written by GPT-6-Astra, it can be reused as is by weaker MLLMs and improve their performance without any adaptation of their own.

</details>


### [60] [Breaking survivorship bias in driving world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11516)
*Zihao Sheng,Zilin Huang,Yuhao Luo,Zhengyang Wan,Boyue Wang,Soyoung Ahn,David A. Noyce,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出驾驶世界模型存在"幸存者偏差"——训练数据中的未来几乎都以安全结束，导致模型碰撞在即时仍想象出良性结果；提出中立世界模型（NWM），混合常规驾驶数据与真实事故视频并用边界框引导目标训练，从而能忠实预测即将发生的碰撞，并作为"想象引擎"评估候选轨迹以辅助决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型的可靠性依赖于训练数据中出现的未来分布，而主流驾驶数据集的未来几乎都是安全结局，形成幸存者偏差；使模型在碰撞迫近时仍想象出良性结果，从而低估风险，影响安全关键场景下的决策。

Method: 1) 将常规驾驶数据与真实事故视频混合进行训练；2) 引入边界框引导的目标函数，使模型同时学习日常未来与安全关键未来；3) 推理阶段作为"想象引擎"：给定多条自车候选轨迹，分别预测其对应未来，再由奖励函数评估所想象的未来并选择动作。

Result: NWM 能更忠实地预见即将发生的碰撞，同时在常规驾驶中保持较低的误报碰撞率与有竞争力的生成质量；在具有挑战性的 NAVSIM 场景中，比基线世界模型更可靠地选出较优轨迹；使用自主研究车辆采集的真实行人假人横穿数据进行零样本实验，进一步证明其可迁移到未见过的传感器观测。

Conclusion: 纠正幸存者偏差可使世界模型更忠实地推理未来，并支撑自动驾驶中的安全关键决策；模型的可靠性不仅取决于架构，也取决于训练未来分布的代表性。

Abstract: World models allow autonomous vehicles to imagine the consequences of candidate actions before execution, but their reliability depends on the futures represented in their training data. We identify a survivorship bias in driving world models: because the futures recorded in prevailing driving datasets almost always end safely, existing models often imagine benign outcomes even when a collision is imminent. We introduce a neutral world model (NWM) that learns both routine and safety-critical futures from a mixture of routine driving and real accident videos with a bounding-box-guided objective. The NWM more faithfully anticipates imminent collisions, while maintaining a low false collision rate and competitive generation quality in routine driving. We further use the model as an imagination engine for decision-making. Given multiple ego trajectories, the NWM predicts the future associated with each candidate, and a reward function evaluates these imagined outcomes before selecting an action. On challenging NAVSIM scenarios, this procedure selects the better trajectory more reliably than the baseline world model. Zero-shot experiments with real-world pedestrian dummy crossing data collected using an autonomous research vehicle further demonstrate transfer to unseen sensor observations. These results show that correcting survivorship bias enables world models to more faithfully reason about futures and support safety-critical decision-making in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [61] [K-PWM: Control-Oriented Structured Bilinear World Models under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11521)
*Santosh Mohan Rajkumar,Sriram Narayanan,Samuel E. Otto,Debdipta Goswami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 K-PWM，一种面向部分可观测系统的概率世界模型，将 Koopman 双线性潜动力学与非线性观测解码器耦合，利用扩展卡尔曼滤波/平滑在已学模型内做潜状态推断，并用广义期望条件最大化（GECM）与梯度法联合训练，实现了数据与计算高效的长时程预测和不确定性感知 MPPI 控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型多聚焦像素级观测，而许多物理系统只提供向量测量且仅部分揭示真实状态；在数据有限、含噪、部分观测条件下学习可靠的世界模型仍然困难，且常需额外训练推断网络，带来额外数据与算力开销。

Method: 1）以 Koopman 双线性潜动力学 + 非线性观测解码器构成模型；2）通过扩展卡尔曼滤波与平滑在学得模型内完成概率潜状态推断，无需独立推断网络；3）双线性结构使高斯置信度可在潜转移中精确传播，支持高效 rollout 与不确定性感知 MPPI 控制；4）训练采用 GECM 过程，对生成矩阵、噪声协方差和潜先验给出闭式更新，解码器用梯度学习；5）提出 Koopman 一致初始化，从数据选择潜维数并初始化动作相关动力学。

Result: 在部分观测的 Gymnasium MuJoCo 系统上，训练数据有限时 K-PWM 的长时程预测误差低于对比基线，且训练时间显著短于最强预测基线；在高维 MuJoCo Menagerie 机器人上取得有竞争力的跟踪精度，单步计算时间低，具备实时部署潜力。

Conclusion: 结果支持将结构化概率动力学作为部分观测下数据与计算高效的世界建模与控制途径。

Abstract: World models learn internal simulators of environment dynamics for prediction, planning, and control, yet learning them from limited, noisy, and partial observations remains challenging. While much progress has focused on pixel-based observations, many physical systems instead provide vector-valued measurements that reveal only part of the underlying state. We introduce K-PWM, a probabilistic world model for prediction and control under partial observation. K-PWM couples Koopman-bilinear latent dynamics with a nonlinear observation decoder and performs probabilistic latent-state inference through the learned model via extended Kalman filtering and smoothing, without a separately trained inference network. Its bilinear structure enables exact propagation of the maintained Gaussian belief through the latent transition and efficient rollouts for uncertainty-aware model predictive path integral (MPPI) control. For training K-PWM, we present a generalized expectation conditional maximization (GECM) procedure with closed-form updates for the generators, noise covariances, and latent prior, complemented by gradient-based decoder learning. A Koopman-consistent initialization selects the latent dimension from data and initializes the action-dependent dynamics. With limited training data, K-PWM achieves lower long-horizon prediction error than the evaluated baselines on partially observed Gymnasium MuJoCo systems, while substantially reducing training time relative to the strongest prediction baseline. On high-dimensional MuJoCo Menagerie robots, it achieves competitive tracking accuracy with low per-step computation times, demonstrating potential for real-time deployment. These results support structured probabilistic dynamics as a data- and compute-efficient approach to world modeling and control under partial observation.

</details>


### [62] [Kimodo-Policy: Learning Kinematic Action Models for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11554)
*Yunheng Wang,Jiaxi Zhang,Wei Lu,Yuetong Fang,Taowen Wang,Yixiao Feng,Shuning Zhang,Junzhe Xu,Yichi Wang,Zizhao Yuan,Kailun Huang,Lusong Li,Mingqian Zhou,Zecui Zeng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Kinematic Action Models (KAMs) 范式，将全身运动作为直接预训练目标，并用预训练运动生成器作为主行动生成骨干，实例 Kimodo-Policy 在多任务基准与真实任务上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人移动操作需要持续协调高维全身运动，但现有 VLA 模型与世界行动模型所迁移的语义与视觉动力学先验并不必然刻画机器人运动本身的结构，缺少以运动为直接建模对象的基础模型。

Method: 以冻结的 Kimodo 文本到运动骨干为主行动生成器，叠加轻量 ControlNet 视觉条件；通过任务条件、视觉观测与执行反馈对时序与跨身体协调先验进行接地；采用 root-body-hand 层级结构协调移动与操作，并用运动历史条件化与重复动作块预测连接运动合成与闭环控制，经大规模移动操作预训练得到 Kimodo-Policy。

Result: 在多任务 HumanoidArena 上取得对比策略中最高的总体成功率 72.14%，在 SIMPLE 上具竞争力，在八项真实任务上平均成功率 60%；模型共 678M 参数（可训练 92M）；消融实验支持预训练运动骨干的贡献。

Conclusion: 结果表明运动生成可作为人形机器人动作学习的互补基础，为以运动为中心的行动建模范式提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires continuous coordination of high-dimensional whole-body motion to realize task objectives. While vision-language-action models and world action models transfer semantic and visual-dynamics priors to robot control, these priors do not necessarily capture the structure of robot motion. We introduce Kinematic Action Models (KAMs), a paradigm that treats whole-body motion as a direct pretraining target and uses a pretrained motion generator as the primary action-generation backbone. KAMs ground learned temporal and cross-body coordination priors through task conditioning, visual observations, and execution feedback. We present Kimodo-Policy as an initial instantiation, combining a frozen Kimodo text-to-motion backbone with lightweight ControlNet-based visual conditioning. A root-body-hand hierarchy coordinates locomotion and manipulation, while motion-history conditioning and repeated action-chunk prediction bridge motion synthesis and closed-loop control. Following large-scale loco-manipulation pretraining, the policy achieves the highest overall success among compared policies on multi-task HumanoidArena (72.14%), competitive performance on SIMPLE, and 60% mean success across eight real-world tasks, with 678M parameters (92M trainable). Ablations support the contribution of the pretrained motion backbone. Together, these results provide initial evidence for motion generation as a complementary foundation for humanoid action learning.

</details>


### [63] [Qubit-to-Motion: Traceable Action Provenance in Hybrid QRL for Robotic Bin-Packing Sequencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11580)
*Hassen Nigatu,Howard Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Qubit-to-Motion 证据中心框架，将量子强化学习（四个 Pauli-Z 读出作为唯一学习输入）用于支撑约束的物体排序与仿真机器人抓放，并实现从决策到接触的可追溯复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子强化学习在机器人决策中多只报告任务成功率、回报或收敛性，未解决量子电路输出是否真正影响受约束动作、以及该影响能否沿机器人执行链路被追溯的问题。

Method: actor 与 critic 仅以四个 Pauli-Z 读出为学习输入；支撑掩码强制合法物体选择；每条决策通过步索引与 SHA-256 指纹链接到密封路径、确定性笛卡尔与逆运动学航点、规定关节运动及 MuJoCo 接触重放；用冻结检查点在五个确定性 30 物体场景变体上评估，并做固定状态零读出干预实验。

Result: 150 次放置全部满足支撑、包容与稳定判据；零读出干预改变 90.0±0.0% 的决策；平均宏观行程 34.537±0.206 m，每条路径推理耗时 8.60±1.27 ms；重放末端平均位置误差 8.764±0.220 μm，最大互穿 0.356 mm。

Conclusion: 结果表明量子电路读出确实被约束动作所依赖，且可实现从决策到物理接触的可复现可追溯性，为该类系统提供了可审计的评估范式。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum reinforcement learning has been explored for robotic decision-making, but evaluations often emphasize task success, return, or convergence, leaving unresolved whether quantum circuit outputs influence constrained actions and remain traceable through robot execution. We present \texttt{Qubit-to-Motion}, an evidence-centered framework for support-constrained sequencing and simulated robotic placement. Four Pauli-$Z$ readouts provide the sole learned input to the actor and critic, while a support mask enforces admissible object selection. Each decision is recorded and linked via step indices and SHA-256 fingerprints to a sealed route, deterministic Cartesian and inverse-kinematics waypoints, prescribed joint motion, and MuJoCo contact replay. A frozen checkpoint is evaluated across five deterministic 30-object scene variants. All 150 placements satisfy support, containment, and settling criteria. A fixed-state zero-readout intervention changes $90.0\pm0.0\%$ of decisions, demonstrating dependence on the joint circuit representation. Mean macro travel is $34.537\pm0.206$~m, inference requires $8.60\pm1.27$~ms per route, and replay achieves $8.764\pm0.220~μ$m mean final position error with 0.356~mm maximum interpenetration. These results establish reproducible decision-to-contact traceability. The implementation is available at \href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Qubit-to-Motion-Quantum-Reinforcement-Learning-F7DC}{\textcolor{linkpink}{the anonymous repository}}.

</details>


### [64] [Kinora: A Versioned Workflow for Text-to-Motion Generation, Robot Retargeting, and Interactive Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11601)
*Fangcheng Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Kinora是一个将文本生成动作、动作库、本地智能体工具、机器人重定向和轨迹精修通过版本化产物串联起来的系统，当前版本采用独立指定的52关节创作骨架与程序化骨架预览，并通过回归测试、CUDA生成/编辑用例、G1重定向与合成夹具验证集成与保持性契约，但未证明语义质量提升、群体可行性或硬件安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作生成工具只产出候选轨迹，而交互式机器人创作还需要可复现的执行、可追溯的编辑、显式几何以及针对特定模型的验证；因此需要一个把生成、库管理、编辑与重定向打通并保持版本可追溯的系统。

Method: 系统以版本化产物为核心连接文本到动作生成、动作库、本地智能体工具、机器人重定向与轨迹精修。当前发布版使用独立规定的52关节创作骨架和程序化骨架预览，从而把所分发的人体几何与外部安装的生成模型和机器人资产分离。人体动作编辑保留所选区间之外的帧，机器人编辑则产生不可变候选，由报告决定接受与导出。

Result: 评估server 0.5.1与Gadget 0.15.2，包含156次核心回归测试执行、49次Gadget执行、4个CUDA生成/编辑用例、一次30帧G1重定向检查，以及3600帧合成G1夹具配合一个挂起的负对照。4个CUDA用例全部通过数值门限，但转向拼接仍保留14.3 cm支撑足漂移代理警告；G1正例夹具完成有界仿真回放，而挂起对照在回放前被隔离。

Conclusion: 结果支持部分集成与保持性契约，但并未确立语义质量改进、群体层面的可行性或硬件安全性。

Abstract: Motion generation tools produce candidate trajectories, but interactive robot authoring also requires recoverable execution, traceable edits, explicit geometry, and model-specific validation. We present Kinora, a system that connects text-to-motion generation, a motion library, local agent tools, robot retargeting, and trajectory refinement through versioned artifacts. The current release uses an independently specified 52-joint authoring rig and procedural skeleton previews, separating the distributed human geometry from externally installed generation models and robot assets. Human-motion edits preserve frames outside a selected interval, while robot edits create immutable candidates whose reports govern acceptance and export. We evaluate server 0.5.1 and Gadget 0.15.2 with 156 core regression test executions, 49 Gadget executions, four CUDA generation/editing cases, a 30-frame G1 retargeting check, and a 3,600-frame synthetic G1 fixture paired with a suspended negative control. All four CUDA cases passed their numerical gates; the turning splice nevertheless retained a 14.3 cm support-foot drift proxy warning. The G1 positive fixture completed bounded simulation replay, whereas the suspended control was quarantined before replay. These results support selected integration and preservation contracts. They do not establish semantic-quality improvements, population-level feasibility, or hardware safety.

</details>


### [65] [ScanDreamer: A Map-Grounded World Model for Quadrotor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11605)
*Yunyi Gao,Yixing Shi,Yize Liu,Hai-Duong Tran,Nhat-Hoang P. Nguyen,Shuai Wang,Yi Deng,Shengming Liu,Sim Kuan Goh,Duc-Thinh Pham,Sameer Alam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ScanDreamer，一种以地图为基座的世界模型导航方法：用深度图与位姿在线构建 2D 扫描记忆地图，并以其条件化世界模型、actor 和 critic，同时定义测地进度奖励与前向锥惩罚。仅 80 万步训练（单 GPU 5.5 小时）即在 Isaac Sim 的 1560 个未见场景回合中达 96.3±0.3% 成功率、3.6±0.3% 碰撞率，显著优于同交互预算下的 AirDreamer（59.6%、28.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前视深度相机视野有限，导致场景理解与避障导航困难；现有世界模型智能体（如 AirDreamer）依赖大量数据，且缺乏显式几何记忆和对相机可观测范围的感知。

Method: 从深度图像与飞行器位姿在线构建 2D 扫描记忆地图；世界模型、actor、critic 均以该地图为条件；地图用于定义测地进度奖励；引入前向锥惩罚抑制相机视野外的水平运动；想象阶段在冻结地图上传播位姿，并由地图几何导出进度与撞墙信号。

Result: 训练 0.8M 环境步（单 GPU 5.5 小时），在 Isaac Sim 上评估 1560 个未见布局与参数范围回合，三随机种子均值±标准差：成功率 96.3±0.3%、碰撞率 3.6±0.3%；同交互预算下 AirDreamer 为 59.6±3.0% 与 28.8±2.8%；去掉前向锥惩罚后碰撞升至 9.9±3.1%。

Conclusion: 显式地图记忆、地图基座的想象推演与感知感知的运动约束三者结合，能显著提升四旋翼在未知杂乱环境中的导航性能与数据效率；代码已开源。

Abstract: Quadrotors inspecting buildings and tunnels must navigate unknown clutter with a forward-facing depth camera whose limited field of view constrains scene understanding and collision-avoiding navigation. Recent world-model agents learn navigation through imagined experience (e.g., AirDreamer), but remain data-intensive and often lack explicit geometric memory and awareness of what the camera can observe. We propose ScanDreamer, a map-grounded world model that constructs an online 2D scan-memory map from depth images and vehicle pose. Our world model, actor, and critic are conditioned on this map, which also defines a geodesic progress reward. Moreover, a forward-cone penalty discourages horizontal motion outside the camera's viewing direction. During imagination, ScanDreamer propagates the quadrotor pose over a frozen map and derives progress and wall-contact signals from the mapped geometry. We train ScanDreamer for 0.8 million environment steps (5.5 h on one GPU) and evaluate it in NVIDIA Isaac Sim over 1560 episodes spanning unseen layouts and parameter ranges. Across three seeds (mean $\pm$ SD), ScanDreamer achieves 96.3 $\pm$ 0.3% success with 3.6 $\pm$ 0.3% collision, compared with 59.6 $\pm$ 3.0% success and 28.8 $\pm$ 2.8% collision for AirDreamer at the same online-interaction budget. Removing the forward-cone penalty increases collisions to 9.9 $\pm$ 3.1%. These results show that combining explicit map memory, map-grounded imagination, and perception-aware motion substantially improves navigation performance and data efficiency. The source code is available at https://github.com/WANGSHUAIzzzz/ScanDreamer.

</details>


### [66] [ColNav: Uncertainty-Aware Spacetime Collision Checking with Bounding Volume Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11646)
*Sanjeev Ramkumar Sudha,Lars Ivar Hatledal,Erlend M. Coates*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ColNav，一种基于几何时空管的鲁棒碰撞检测公式，将目标与自车位置和速度的不确定性编码为时空管并组织为包围体层次结构，单次树遍历即可覆盖整个规划时域并返回鲁棒几何证书与碰撞时间估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在动态不确定环境中避障，通常依赖前向传播运动并在离散时空采样点检测碰撞；这类方法会漏检采样点之间的碰撞，且对状态估计不确定性不鲁棒。

Method: 将目标与自车机器人位置、速度的不确定性编码为时空管（spacetime tubes），分别支持有界集合与高斯两类不确定性描述；把时空管组织成包围体层次结构（BVH），从而用一次树遍历覆盖整个时域而非逐采样时刻检测，并统一表示静态与动态障碍物。

Result: 在预测模型失配条件下成功率达到 100%（最佳基线为 65%），在密集交通中保持最高达三倍的间隙（clearance）；并在实体机器人上验证了实时运行。

Conclusion: 基于时空管的鲁棒碰撞检测可有效消除采样漏检问题并处理不确定性，兼顾鲁棒性与实时性，适用于真实动态环境中的移动机器人导航。

Abstract: Safe navigation for mobile robots in dynamic and uncertain environments requires planning paths that avoid collisions with other moving targets. Typically this involves forward propagating the robot motion and checking for collisions at sampled points in space and time. Such methods can miss collisions between sampled points and are not robust to uncertainty in the state estimates. We propose ColNav, a robust collision check formulation with geometric volumes. The key aspect of our approach is encoding the uncertainty in the targets' and the ego robot's positions and velocities into spacetime tubes, in two variants that consume bounded-set and Gaussian uncertainty descriptions, respectively. The tubes are organised in bounding volume hierarchies, so that a single tree traversal covers the entire horizon, rather than testing sampled time instants, and returns a robust geometric certificate together with an estimate of the time to collision. Static and dynamic obstacles share the same representation. Our approach reaches the goal with a 100% success rate under prediction model mismatch, where the best baseline reaches 65%, and maintains up to three times the clearance in dense traffic. We also validate real-time operation on a physical robot.

</details>


### [67] [PA-SaR: Perception-Aware Learning, Control, and Coordination for Multi-Robot Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11648)
*Jocubas Dimsa,Maria Grouev,Filip Keerberg,Nicolas Laplagne,Tigo Schutgens,Sven Schoonebeek,Anahita Jamshidnejad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PA-SaR框架，用于在任务级目标下统一协调异构局部控制、基于学习的探索、监督式任务分配与自适应引导策略，并通过单/多机器人仿真验证各机制的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主搜救（SaR）需机器人探索未知环境、规避危险、团队协调并引导受困者撤离；但单纯提升单机器人能力未必改善整体任务结果，因为安全运动限制探索、更快发现会增加救援需求、有效引导依赖受困者响应，因此需要任务级视角统一评估这些相互耦合的机制。

Method: 构建PA-SaR数学与架构框架，区分环境真值、机器人个体感知与仿真受困者行为三层；集成模型预测与学习型探索、基于感知的安全屏蔽、事件驱动任务分配、自适应接近-引导受害者策略，并在单机器人与多机器人仿真中开展实验。

Result: 实验显示碰撞规避与搜索完成度之间存在权衡；监督式任务分配比单纯信息共享更能提升搜索完成度；探索增加并不必然带来更多救援；自适应引导相比持续引领提升救援数量，但对平均或最终受困者信任无显著提升，也未降低死亡率。

Conclusion: 在所考虑的仿真环境中，局部控制、团队协调与人员疏散应作为自主搜救中相互关联但彼此独立的三类方面分别评估；PA-SaR为整合与交互分析提供了共同基础。

Abstract: Autonomous SaR requires robots to explore unknown or partially known environments, avoid hazards, coordinate team activities, and guide discovered victims to safety. Improving individual capabilities of robots does not necessarily improve mission outcomes: safer motion can restrict the exploration behavior, faster discovery can increase the rescue demand, and effective guidance depends on victim responses. We introduce PA-SaR, a mathematical and architectural framework for coordinating heterogeneous local control, learning-based exploration, supervisory task allocation, and adaptive approach-lead victim guidance around mission-level objectives. PA- SaR distinguishes environmental ground-truth, robot-specific perceptions, and simulated victim behavior. This provides a common basis for integrating complementary mechanisms and analyzing their interactions across mission stages. We demonstrate the framework through single- and multi-robot simulation studies incorporating model-predictive and learning-based exploration, perception-based safety shielding, event-driven task allocation, and adaptive victim guidance. The results show trade-offs between collision avoidance and search completion, indicate that supervisory allocation improves search completion beyond information sharing alone, and demonstrate that increased exploration does not necessarily yield more rescues. Adaptive guidance increases rescues relative to continuous leading, without a detected improvement in mean or final victim trust or a reduction in mortality. These findings support evaluating local control, team coordination, and evacuation as related but distinct aspects of autonomous SaR within the considered simulation environments.

</details>


### [68] [FlowEval: How Much Adaptation Does an Embodied Foundation Model Need at Deployment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11650)
*Zhang Xi-Jia,Roman Yakunin,Shivang Chopra,Chengyue Huang,Che Chen,Siddharth Karamcheti,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FlowEval——一组无需训练的度量，用 flow-matching 目标函数配合少量成功示范来探测具身基础模型，判断预训练模型与目标部署的匹配程度及还需多少适配数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练降低了具身基础模型的部署适配成本，但预训练模型未必在每个部署场景都达到所需性能。实践中需要事先知道模型与目标部署的匹配度，以及还需收集多少适配数据，而现有方法缺少这种预判手段。

Method: FlowEval 利用 flow-matching 目标函数，在少量成功的目标域示范上计算预测不一致性（predictive disagreement），从而量化预训练模型对目标部署的拟合程度，整个过程无需任何训练或微调。

Result: 在 Pi-0.5、MolmoAct2、GR00T、DreamZero 四个具身基础模型家族上，FlowEval 在真实与仿真操作任务中均可区分已见与未见部署；在 17 个 RoboCasa365 操作任务上微调 Pi-0.5 与 GR00T 后，FlowEval 与随适配数据增加而提升的成功率变化相关。

Conclusion: FlowEval 可作为轻量、无需训练的诊断工具，用于预估模型—部署之间所需的适配程度与数据量。

Abstract: Large-scale pretraining is reducing the amount of adaptation required to deploy embodied foundation models, but it is not guaranteed that a pretrained model already provide the level of performance required in every deployment. Practitioners therefore need to understand how well a pretrained model matches a target deployment and how much adaptation data is left to be collected. We study whether a small set of successful target-domain demonstrations can reveal whether and how much target-domain data will be required before any adaptation. We introduce FlowEval, a set of training-free metrics that use the flow-matching objective to probe an embodied foundation model with a set of demonstrations. FlowEval quantifies predictive disagreement on successful target demonstrations to assess how well a pretrained model fits a target deployment. Across four embodied foundation model families: Pi-0.5, MolmoAct2, GR00T, and DreamZero, FlowEval distinguishes seen from unseen deployments across real-world and simulated manipulation. Further, by finetuning Pi-0.5 and GR00T on 17 RoboCasa365 manipulation tasks, we showed that FlowEval correlates with how success rate improves as additional adaptation data is provided. Together, these results suggest that FlowEval can serve as a lightweight, training-free diagnostic of how much model--deployment adaptation may be required.

</details>


### [69] [Enhancing Balance and Posture in Material Handling Tasks with a Wearable Robotic Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11662)
*Eisa Anwar,Sajeeva Abeywardena,Stuart C. Miller,Ildar Farkhatdinov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受动物用尾巴保持平衡的启发，本文研制了一种可穿戴机器尾巴作为动态配重，并在模拟装卸货任务中验证其能减小压力中心偏移、躯干运动及下肢与腰部肌肉负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频繁负重搬运易导致肌肉骨骼劳损，约40%的体力劳动者受下背痛困扰，现有护具多为被动支撑，缺乏主动动态平衡辅助手段。

Method: 开发可穿戴机器尾巴；10名被试在主动尾巴、被动尾巴、无尾巴三种条件下完成模拟货架装卸任务；测量运动学、压力中心（CoP）及腓肠肌、竖脊肌、胫骨前肌、肱二头肌的表面肌电。

Result: 相较无尾巴，主动尾巴使CoP峰值位移减少>20 mm，躯干运动减少4.3°，竖脊肌与腓肠肌活动分别降低>9%和>20%；被动尾巴的改善幅度较小；主动条件较被动条件下肱二头肌激活增加；踝关节运动学与胫骨前肌激活无变化。

Conclusion: 可穿戴机器尾巴可改善物料搬运时的平衡与躯干稳定性，被动配重已有效果、主动运动可进一步增强，具有作为搬运辅助装备的潜力；但样本量小、任务为模拟场景，需进一步验证。

Abstract: Frequent heavy lifting is linked to musculoskeletal strain, with lower back pain affecting up to 40% of manual labourers. Inspired by animals using tails for balance, we developed a wearable robotic tail as a dynamic counterbalance. Ten participants performed an imitated shelf loading/unloading task across three conditions: active tail, passive tail, and no tail. Kinematics, centre of pressure, and muscle activation (gastrocnemius, erector spinae, tibialis anterior, biceps brachii) were measured. Compared with no tail, the active tail achieved >20 mm reduction in centre of pressure peak displacement, 4.3 degree reduction in trunk motion, and >9% and >20% reductions in erector spinae and gastrocnemius activity, respectively. The passive tail produced smaller reductions, suggesting passive counterbalancing helps while active movement augments it further. Biceps brachii activation increased with the active versus passive tail. The ankle kinematics and tibialis anterior activation were unchanged. These findings support wearable robotic tails for improving balance during material handling.

</details>


### [70] [HINT-VLM: Humanoid Interactive Navigation over Rough Terrain with Embodiment-Grounded Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11665)
*Ziwon Yoon,Seok Hwan Kim,Yiyu Chen,Quan Nguyen,Lu Gan,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合规划框架，将VLM语义先验与高斯过程残差模型结合，实现户外崎岖地形下类人机器人可移动障碍物导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在运动与操作上进展显著，但传统导航假设环境不可交互、只关注通行；已有NAMO方法多局限于平坦环境和反应式交互，缺乏对物体可移动性的预测与前瞻规划。在户外崎岖地形中，机器人需同时推理地形可通行性与物体可移动性，这一需求尚未被满足。

Method: 构建混合规划框架：用视觉语言模型（VLM）提供可移动性的语义先验，用高斯过程（GP）残差模型结合机器人具身交互数据进行校正，得到具身化的概率可移动性估计，并将其嵌入分层的不确定性感知规划中。

Result: GP对VLM先验的校正产生具身化的概率可移动性估计，使规划器能够在不确定性下联合利用移动与操作能力；据作者所述，这是首个通过预测性可移动性推理与概率化VLM融合解决崎岖地形NAMO的方案。

Conclusion: 该工作将语义先验与具身经验相结合，弥补了NAMO在非平坦地形与前瞻性可移动性预测方面的空白，为人形机器人在杂乱户外环境中的高效安全导航提供了新思路。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have achieved significant progress in locomotion and manipulation, enabling traversal over rough terrain and richer interaction with the environment, such as moving obstacles. However, most traditional navigation methods assume non-interactive environments and focus only on traversal. While Navigation Among Movable Obstacles (NAMO) approaches relax this assumption, prior efforts have largely focused on flat environments and reactive interaction, rather than predicting object movability and planning ahead. These limitations become critical in outdoor rough-terrain scenarios, where robots must jointly reason about terrain traversability and object movability. In this work, we address NAMO for humanoid robots in cluttered outdoor rough terrain by proposing a hybrid planning framework that combines semantic priors from a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with embodied robot interaction data through a Gaussian process (GP) residual model. The GP corrects the VLM-derived movability prior to produce embodiment-grounded probabilistic movability estimates, which are then incorporated into hierarchical uncertainty-aware planning. The proposed approach allows humanoids to jointly leverage locomotion and manipulation for efficient and safe navigation, representing the first solution to NAMO in rough terrain through predictive movability reasoning and probabilistic VLM integration.

</details>


### [71] [Brain-Aligned Latent Actions: Imposing Premotor Cortex Geometry on a Robot's Action Vocabulary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11674)
*Amit Jadhav*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人离散潜在动作分词器的码本分配高度不稳定（独立训练ARI仅0.001，bf16推理74%词元重分配），作者利用这一自由度，用直通相似度损失将猕猴PMv皮层的表征几何注入分词器码本，在未见过的形状类别上达到0.52的PMv表征相似度，显著高于基线（0.20–0.26）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略日益依赖从无标注视频中学习的离散潜在动作词表，但"哪个动作对应哪个码"在训练中无人约束。作者发现该分配极不稳定（两次独立训练ARI仅0.001；bf16与fp32推理相差74%词元），说明码本分配是一个可自由操控的"闲置自由度"。作者希望利用这一自由度，检验能否将生物神经表征结构（猕猴腹侧前运动皮层PMv）作为先验植入分词器，从而考察神经先验是否能指导机器人表征学习。

Method: 以Moto的潜在运动分词器为对象，在机器人视频上联合训练重建目标与一个直通（straight-through）相似度损失，使码分配对齐猕猴PMv在画形状任务中记录的神经表征几何。每个目标仅训练一次。在五个损失从未见过的形状类别上评估表征相似度（RSA），并设置多组对照：官方发布的预训练分词器、无先验分词器、随机运动学编码器（含95分位）、运动学/语言/随机目标、标签打乱的PMv对照。下游操控测试在能复现Moto已发表结果的CALVIN流水线上预先注册后再执行。

Result: PMv训练的分词器在未见形状类别上达到与PMv的表征相似度0.52；发布版与无先验分词器为0.20–0.26，随机运动学编码器95分位为0.42；运动学、语言与随机目标均不迁移（-0.17至-0.03）。两项结果限制结论强度：手工设计的运动学描述子"笔画初始方向"与PMv更相似（0.65）；标签打乱的PMv对照仍得0.41。码本不稳定性量化结果：独立训练间ARI=0.001，bf16推理74%词元被重分配。

Conclusion: 码本分配的不稳定性使神经表征几何可被低成本注入离散动作分词器，且该对齐能泛化到未训练过的形状类别并优于非神经先验，但0.65的单变量运动学描述子与0.41的打乱对照削弱了"必须依赖神经结构"的论断。作者将下游操控测试提前明确设定并预注册于CALVIN流水线，以示结果的诚实性与可复现性；整体价值更多在于提出可检验的"神经先验能否改善机器人策略"的问题框架。

Abstract: Robot policies increasingly act through a small vocabulary of discrete latent actions learned from unlabeled video, yet training leaves open which motion receives which code. We find this choice highly unstable: two independently trained runs of Moto's latent motion tokenizer assign the same robot clips to nearly unrelated codes (adjusted Rand index 0.001), and evaluating one tokenizer in bf16 instead of fp32 reassigns 74% of its tokens.
