We use this freedom to impose the representational geometry of macaque ventral premotor cortex (PMv), recorded while a monkey drew shapes, on the tokenizer's code assignment, with a straight-through similarity loss trained alongside its reconstruction objective on robot video. In one training run per target, on five shape categories the loss never saw, the PMv-trained tokenizer reaches a representational similarity of 0.52 with PMv, against 0.20-0.26 for released and no-prior tokenizers and 0.42 for the 95th percentile of random kinematic encoders. Kinematic, language, and random targets do not transfer (-0.17 to -0.03). Two results limit the claim: a hand-picked kinematic descriptor, the stroke's initial direction, matches PMv better (0.65), and a label-shuffled PMv control scores 0.41. We specify the downstream manipulation test, on a CALVIN pipeline that reproduces Moto's published results, before running it.

</details>


### [72] [Streaming Failure Forecasting: VLA Failure Monitors That Keep Learning After Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11688)
*Amit Jadhav,Shaurya Beriwala,David Q. Liu,Yanfei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出流式失败预测（SFF）监控器：离线训练的VLA失败监控器部署后不再冻结，仅用每个回合的“成功/失败”标签在线重拟合最后一层线性读出，在任务或策略版本漂移后仍能有效排序失败，计算开销低（2.18 ms/次）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA失败监控器离线训练后冻结使用，而部署后光照、物体、相机漂移，策略本身也会更新或迁移到新任务。实测显示，任务或策略版本切换后，冻结的旧监控器排序失败的能力接近随机，因此需要一种能在部署后持续适应、且不需要额外标注的监控器。

Method: SFF保持离线监控器网络主干固定，仅在每个回合结束后用该回合的成功/失败单一标签重新拟合最后一层线性层；报警阈值仅在成功回合后调整。模拟中先在锁定所有设置再测试，对比冻结监控器与“检测到漂移后重置”的基线；再将锁定的学习设置迁移到真实SO-101机械臂上4种VLA的434次录制回放，跨未宣告的任务/策略切换进行评估。

Result: 在Jetson单CPU核上每次策略调用仅需2.18 ms。阈值仅在成功后移动的策略在模拟中使误报接近目标，仅在两种漂移同时出现时例外。在5个模拟测试套件中的4个上，SFF在未宣告漂移下优于冻结监控器，并在全部套件上优于“检测到漂移再重置”。在434次真实回放中，跨未宣告任务/策略切换，SFF对每一种VLA都优于冻结监控器；探索性比较中，在真实切换点上也优于完全重置。

Conclusion: 部署的失败监控器不必保持冻结：从结果标签重拟合其读出层成本很低，并在所测试的变化后带来实际收益。这为VLA部署后的持续可靠监控提供了轻量、可落地的方案。

Abstract: Failure monitors for vision-language-action (VLA) policies are trained offline and then frozen. After deployment, lighting, objects and cameras drift, and the policy itself is updated or moved to new tasks. On recorded real-robot runs, after a switch of task or policy version, a frozen monitor fit on the old task ranks failures little better than chance. We present Streaming Failure Forecasting (SFF), a monitor that keeps learning from the one label every episode provides: whether it succeeded. SFF keeps the offline monitor's network fixed and refits only its last linear layer after each episode, at 2.18 ms per policy call on a Jetson CPU core. Its alarm threshold moves only after successes, which in simulation kept false alarms near target except when two drifts came at once. With every setting locked before testing, SFF ranks failures better than the same monitor kept frozen on 4 of 5 simulated test suites under unannounced drift, and above resetting on detected drift on every suite. Carried with its locked learner settings to 434 recorded runs of four VLAs on real SO-101 arms, replayed across unannounced switches of task or policy version, it ranks failures better than the frozen monitor for every VLA and, in an exploratory comparison, above a full reset at the true switch. A deployed failure monitor need not stay frozen: refitting its readout from outcomes is cheap and helps after the changes we tested.

</details>


### [73] [A Physical Testbed for Self-Driving Laboratories: Comparing Bayesian and Agentic Optimization in a Minimal Closed-Loop Color Formulation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11690)
*Tony Qin,Ziheng Wu,Maximilian Schommer,Roya Amini-Naieni,Aldair Gongora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作搭建了一个集成商用实验室自动化仪器的物理自驱动实验室测试平台，在24小时无人值守实验中完成六个闭环发现任务，发现智能体型优化器比贝叶斯优化用更少迭代次数匹配目标吸收光谱，最多减少52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室（SDL）将机器人自动化与AI决策结合以闭合"设计—构建—测试—学习"循环，但融合现成硬件与AI的可运行闭环物理测试平台仍处早期，与商用实验室自动化的集成尤为不足；同时需要一个能低成本、可重复地对比各类AI优化策略（含LLM驱动的智能体）的参考平台，以便在昂贵耗时的真实实验前做评估。

Method: 组装并集成市售实验室自动化仪器，构建物理闭环测试平台；选取最小化的闭环发现任务——自主配制水溶液染料混合物以匹配目标吸收光谱，在固定总体积约束下运行六轮独立实验，每轮48次闭环迭代（约4小时），系统自主完成选择、分液与测量，由AI算法决定下一轮配方；并对比传统贝叶斯优化（BO）与智能体（agentic）方法的性能。

Result: 平台实现累计24小时无人值守的连续闭环自主运行；六轮实验均在无人工干预下完成。智能体优化器用更少迭代次数匹配目标光谱，较BO最多减少52%的迭代次数，并在一个BO在48次迭代内未能匹配的目标上收敛到解。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可运行的自驱动实验室参考物理平台，能够评估既有与新兴AI策略（包括LLM指导的优化），可作为在昂贵、耗时的真实实验场景部署前的验证载体。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) integrate laboratory robotics and automation with AI-driven decision-making to close the design-build-test-learn (DBTL) cycle. However, physical testbeds that combine off-the-shelf hardware and AI into a functioning closed-loop workflow remain in early stages of development, and their integration with commercial laboratory automation is comparatively less developed. In this work, we develop a physical testbed that assembles and integrates commercially available laboratory automation instruments and demonstrate continuous, closed-loop, autonomous operation over 24 cumulative hours of unattended experimentation. We validate this platform on a minimal closed-loop discovery task in which the system autonomously formulates aqueous dye mixtures to match a target absorbance spectrum. The system completed six independent campaigns, each consisting of 48 closed-loop iterations over approximately four hours. In every campaign, the system autonomously selected, dispensed, and measured each mixture, with an AI-driven decision-making algorithm determining subsequent formulations without human intervention. Using this testbed, we compared the performance of two AI-driven decision-making algorithms, traditional Bayesian optimization (BO) and an agentic approach, in selecting mixtures under a fixed total-volume constraint. The agentic optimizer matched target absorbance spectra using fewer iterations than BO, with reductions up to 52%, and converged on a solution for one target that BO failed to match within 48 iterations. These results demonstrate a reference physical platform for SDLs that can evaluate both established and emerging AI strategies, including large language model (LLM)-directed optimization, before their deployment in real-world applications where experiments may be costly and time-consuming to prepare.

</details>


### [74] [Compute Regeneration through Re-Productization: Reorganizing Retired Edge AI Silicon into a Delivered Multilingual Service Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11698)
*Hongyu Luo,Sun Tie,John Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文把"退役边缘硬件的计算再生"重新定义为系统设计问题（再产品化）：保留计算模块、重构整机。案例 GEdgeBot 以退役 32GB Jetson AGX Xavier 为核心构建多语种服务机器人，给出供给清单、架构边界、历史与新测量的性能数据，并通过静态审计与 14 场景回放验证架构假设，结论定位为"存在性主张"而非规模化可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可用的边缘硬件在仍具可观算力时就被终止产品支持，造成算力浪费。作者希望把"计算再生"当作系统设计问题来处理——即"再产品化"：保留算力模块，重新设计其外围产品组织，并验证由此得到的系统是否在功能与实时性上成立。

Method: 以 GEdgeBot（围绕退役 32GB Jetson AGX Xavier 的多语种服务机器人）为案例。给出基于来源的清点：单板即可供给推理，而整机暴露五项端侧 AI 服务、七语种语音链、三个感知域与一条确定性的七字节控制边界。AI 与编排留在 Xavier，有限电机脉冲生成下放至 STM32F407。审计覆盖六模式控制状态机、就绪屏障与声源定向。方法上对比两种执行组织、对 6,000 个实测 50ms 帧间隔重放；进行六项静态审计；并以 14 场景回放执行被审计的 main() 调度器（不打开物理设备、54 个契约校验帧）。11 月开展的活动恢复隔离推理栈、重测 AI 负载并重复控制帧实验。

Result: 历史单原型测量：GPU 内存 1,782 MB（YOLO、Whisper-small、NLLB-200 常驻）；并发 AI 负载下直接控制 p99 偏差由 0.24 ms 升至 49.93 ms（约 208 倍）；六次"触发—跟随"演示平均延迟 4.35 s，且对话语音不可用。重放表明有限脉冲边界可消除主机抖动对脉冲序列的影响，但消除不了指令起始相位；其停止边界由主机强制执行，而 MCU 16 位脉冲字段未做钳位。六项静态审计暴露一处主机—MCU 队列不匹配。14 场景回放覆盖六模式，零物理设备打开、54 帧通过契约校验。

Conclusion: 结果支持一个存在性主张和一条可复现的架构假设，而非车队级可靠性。208 倍直接控制比值尚未复现；MCU 波形捕获与现场故障注入仍是未完成工作。再产品化路径在功能上成立，但实时性保证中的未钳位字段与主机侧的停止约束构成关键遗留风险。

Abstract: Useful edge hardware can lose product support while retaining substantial computation. We treat this as systems design: compute regeneration here means re-productization, retaining the compute module while redesigning the surrounding product organization. Our case is GEdgeBot, a multilingual service robot built around a retired 32 GB Jetson AGX Xavier. A source-grounded inventory shows the box alone supplies inference, while the combined system exposes five on-device AI services, a seven-language speech chain, three sensing domains, and one deterministic seven-byte control boundary. AI and orchestration stay on the Xavier; finite motor-pulse generation on an STM32F407. Audits cover the six-mode control state machine, readiness barriers and sound orientation. Historical single-prototype measurements report 1,782 MB GPU memory with YOLO, Whisper-small, and NLLB-200 resident, a direct-control p99 deviation rising from 0.24 to 49.93 ms under concurrent AI load, and six of six trigger-to-follow demonstrations at 4.35 s mean latency, with conversational voice unavailable. A November campaign restores an isolated inference stack, re-measures AI workloads, and repeats control-frame runs. Driving two execution organisations with the same 6,000 measured 50 ms frame intervals shows the finite-pulse boundary removes host jitter from the pulse train but not the command start phase; its stopping bound is host-enforced, while the MCU 16-bit pulse field is unclamped. A six-check static audit exposes a host--MCU queue mismatch, and a 14-scenario replay executes the audited main() dispatcher across six modes with zero physical device opens and 54 contract-checked frames. Results support an existence claim and a reproducible architectural hypothesis, not fleet-level reliability. The 208 direct-control ratio remains unreplicated; MCU waveform capture and on-site fault injection remain open.

</details>


### [75] [Data-Driven Precision Control of Soft Helicoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11709)
*Roshan S. Kaundinya,Mark Leone,Roberto Sabella,Hugo Buurmeijer,Luis Pabon,John Irvin Alora,Marco Pavone,George Haller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将基于绝热谱子流形（aSSM）的纯数据驱动降阶建模方法，从硬件软体机器人平台采集的数据中学习5维aSSM降阶非线性ODE模型，并结合模型预测控制实现软螺旋躯干机器人的实时高精度轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的aSSM数据驱动方法仅在软体机器人仿真器上验证；作者希望把该方法推广到真实硬件数据，以检验其在物理软体平台上的建模与控制有效性，并应对2D/3D轨迹、静态与动态约束等挑战性闭环任务。

Method: 以绝热谱子流形理论为基础，完全依赖硬件采集数据，学习出描述软螺旋躯干机器人动力学的5维aSSM降阶非线性常微分方程；将该ODE嵌入模型预测控制框架，执行多种闭环轨迹跟踪任务（2D至3D、含静态和动态约束）。

Result: 在所有控制任务中，该方法相比可用的数据驱动线性方法和无模型强化学习均提升了准确性与精度，实现了实时精密控制。

Conclusion: aSSM降阶建模可直接从硬件数据构建高保真低维模型并支撑实时MPC控制；由于只依赖硬件数据，方法可推广到更广泛的软体机器人系统（尽管本文仅以软螺旋躯干机器人为例）。

Abstract: A purely data-driven method grounded in the theory of adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs) was recently used to model and control nonlinear soft robotic simulators. Here, we extend the methodology to learn aSSM-reduced models from data collected from hardware soft robotic platforms. Specifically, we learn a five dimensional (5D) aSSM-reduced nonlinear ordinary differential equation (ODE) that accurately models the dynamics of a soft-helicoid trunk robot. We then use this ODE in a model predictive control scheme to demonstrate real-time precision control across various challenging closed-loop trajectory-tracking tasks, ranging from 2D to 3D trajectories with static and dynamic constraints. We find that our methodology enhances accuracy and precision across all control tasks compared to available data-driven linear methods and model-free reinforcement learning. While we focus here on a soft-helicoid trunk robot, our methodology relies solely on hardware data and is therefore applicable to a broader class of soft robotic systems.

</details>


### [76] [Accelerated Reinforcement Learning for Soft Robotics: Hours Instead of Years](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11718)
*Roshan S. Kaundinya,Hehui Zheng,Robert K. Katzschmann,George Haller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用仅由离线数据学习的低维多项式 aSSM 约简模型作为强化学习训练环境，在气动软臂上数小时内训出可零微调部署的轨迹跟踪策略，精度优于大规模 RNN 与 Transformer 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人硬件难以获得大量交互数据或可靠仿真器，而强化学习恰需要二者之一，因此缺乏可用的训练环境。作者希望利用软体动力学固有的时间尺度分离特性，把高维动力学约简为低维不变流形，从而用极少量参数构建可信的替代仿真环境。

Method: 采用绝热谱子流形（aSSM）约简：从预先采集的数据中学习一个简单的多项式常微分方程（6,924 个参数，辨识约 5 分钟），将六维 aSSM 约简模型作为 RL 训练环境，在气动软臂上训练轨迹跟踪策略，并与相同策略在 480 万参数 RNN、170 万参数 Transformer 上训练的结果对比。

Result: 在六维 aSSM 约简模型上 3 小时即完成策略训练，部署时无需微调；若在真实硬件上复现同等训练量需约 3 年交互。该策略对动态目标的跟踪误差比在 RNN 和 Transformer 上训练的同类策略低 40%，而后者辨识分别需 13 小时和 100 小时（本方法仅 5 分钟）。

Conclusion: 数学上可解释的低维 aSSM 约简模型可作为高效、准确的 RL 训练环境，在数据效率、训练成本和最终性能上同时优于大型黑箱序列模型，为软体机器人控制提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) requires large amounts of interaction data or a reliable simulator, both hard to obtain for nonlinear soft robotic hardware. Recent work shows that a time-scale separation inherent to soft robot dynamics justifies their reduction to low-dimensional invariant manifolds, known as adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs). We demonstrate that an aSSM-reduced model, a simple polynomial ordinary differential equation learned purely from pre-collected data, can serve as an RL training environment. On a pneumatically actuated soft arm, we train a trajectory-tracking policy in 3 hours on a six-dimensional aSSM-reduced model and deploy it without fine-tuning, whereas reproducing this policy training extrapolates to 3 years of hardware interaction. Our policy tracks dynamic targets with 40% lower error than otherwise identical policies trained on a 4.8-million-parameter recurrent neural network (RNN) or a 1.7-million-parameter transformer, which take 13 and 100 hours to identify, against 5 minutes for our mathematically justified 6,924-parameter model.

</details>


### [77] [Particle Filtering with Generative Trajectory Models for State Estimation and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11732)
*Lukas Lao Beyer,Samvit Das,Sertac Karaman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在不修改预训练扩散模型的前提下，用粒子滤波维护状态历史假设，并将粒子权重同时用于状态估计与动作选择，在部分可观测导航和视觉操作任务上同时提升估计与控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人中生成模型常被当作策略直接采样动作，但联合建模状态、动作与观测的模型还能预测状态与环境演化。作者希望探究：仅改变推理流程（而非重训模型），能否利用这些预测同时改善状态估计与控制。

Method: 使用单个预训练扩散模型作为粒子滤波的提议生成器：每个粒子对应一条状态历史假设，模型同时生成该假设下的预测观测与动作提议；新观测到来后，依据预测观测与真实测量的匹配程度对粒子重新加权。与常规用法不同的是，这些权重不仅用于状态估计，还被用于引导动作选择，偏向那些能更好解释观测的假设所对应的动作提议。

Result: 在部分可观测导航与基于视觉的操作仿真实验中，相较于不进行滤波的直接动作生成，状态估计精度与任务表现均有提升，且使用同一预训练模型、不引入额外测试时信息。

Conclusion: 将粒子滤波与预训练联合生成模型结合，把估计权重复用于动作选择，是一种无需微调、即插即用的推理改进方案，可同时受益于状态估计与控制。

Abstract: Generative models can support both state estimation and control in robotics. For control, a common approach is to use the model as a policy that directly samples actions conditioned on observation histories. However, models that jointly represent states, actions, and observations can also predict how the robot state and its environment evolve. We investigate whether these predictions can improve state estimation and control through a change in inference procedure, without modifying the pretrained model. To this end, we use particle filtering to maintain competing hypotheses about state histories. A single pretrained diffusion model generates these hypotheses along with their predicted observations and associated action proposals. As new measurements arrive, particles are reweighted according to how well their predicted observations match the measurements. While particle filtering conventionally uses these weights only for state estimation, we also use them to guide action selection, favoring proposals associated with hypotheses that better explain the observations. Simulation experiments in partially observed navigation and vision-based manipulation demonstrate improvements in both state estimation and task performance compared to action generation without filtering, using the same pretrained model and no additional test-time information.

</details>


### [78] [Graph-Sparse Distributed Predictive Control for Multi-Robot Motion Planning vianEdge-Consensus ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11755)
*Josep Borras-Borras,Pablo Segovia,Carlos Ocampo-Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向双积分动力学平面机器人的分布式MPC碰撞规避运动规划架构：用时变预测冲突图选取交互、每步一次凸化得到固定QP、以边一致ADMM分解并行求解，并由集中式HOCBF-QP选择性监督执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式MPC在机器人数目增大时协调与计算负担重、扩展性差，需要降低耦合与求解规模，同时保持碰撞规避与任务性能。

Method: 基于热启动轨迹的时变预测冲突图筛选成对交互；每MPC更新仅凸化一次碰撞约束，形成固定凸QP；采用ADMM边一致形式分解，实现机器人本地并行优化与到分离半空间的独立闭式投影；当分布式候选解不满足直接执行条件时，由集中式HOCBF-QP接管监督。

Result: 在8、16、32台机器人的C++仿真中实现无碰撞执行，显著提升计算可扩展性，任务级性能与集中式全连接基线相当。

Conclusion: 稀疏化交互加一次凸化加ADMM分解，配合HOCBF后备监督，可在保证安全的同时获得良好扩展性。

Abstract: This paper presents a distributed model predictive control (MPC) architecture for collision-avoiding motion planning of planar robots with double-integrator dynamics. To reduce coordination demands, a time-varying predicted conflict graph selects relevant pairwise interactions. The associated collision-avoidance constraints are convexified once per MPC update using warm-start trajectories, yielding a fixed convex quadratic program (QP). The resulting QP is then decomposed using an edge-consensus formulation of the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), enabling parallel robot-local optimization and independent closed-form projections onto separating half-spaces. Execution is selectively supervised by a centralized high-order control barrier function (HOCBF) QP when the distributed candidate does not satisfy the direct-execution conditions. Evaluated in C++ simulations involving 8, 16, and 32 robots, the proposed architecture achieves collision-free execution and substantially improves computational scalability while maintaining task-level performance comparable to a centralized complete-graph baseline.

</details>


### [79] [Planners Exploit Deterministic Error: Dithered Low-Bit World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11780)
*Yong Hwi Song,Keon Myung Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 低比特世界模型中，采样规划崩溃的根源不是舍入误差大小，而是误差是否"跨迭代持续"且"跨候选共享"；共享抖动（每次迭代重抽）可在同比特宽度下无需训练挽回大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI/CEM等采样规划器每个控制步需查询学习到的世界模型数千次，低比特量化是降低模型基控制成本的直接手段。但作者发现：在round-to-nearest激活量化且无校准的情况下，规划会崩溃（成功率下降最多100点），而世界模型的一步隐空间预测误差却与能正常规划的随机舍入方案相当，说明误差指标无法解释失败，需要找出真正决定因素。

Method: 对比round-to-nearest与随机舍入在相同比特宽度下的规划表现；提出并检验"误差持续性×跨候选共享性"假设：R2N的误差在规划器迭代间持续、被所有候选共享，迭代规划器会向该误差偏好的候选漂移。在TD-MPC2中通过固定同一步/同一回合内的抖动来复现漂移；采用所有候选共享、每次迭代重抽的抖动（无需训练）来消除损坏。同时测试NVFP4/MXFP4等分块缩放格式，并在Ampere GPU上用CUDA graph测量W8A4整数模型的实际加速。假设、阈值与分析代码在数据前固定。

Result: 共享抖动在相同比特宽度下修复了大部分损坏：TD-MPC2全部19个受影响Meta-World单元（任务×比特宽度）至少挽回一半（中位95%），DINO-WM 5个单元中4个，317M参数多任务TD-MPC2中全部8个受影响任务；共享抖动最多增加2.3%延迟。CUDA graph下W8A4整数TD-MPC2的规划rollout比FP32快1.9–3.3×、完整规划步快1.3–1.5×，但不快于预转换权重的BF16。块缩放NVFP4消除大部分损坏、MXFP4仅部分，将失败归因于带共享尺度的粗网格。

Conclusion: 低比特世界模型用于采样规划的成败取决于量化误差的结构（是否跨迭代持续、是否跨候选共享），而非误差幅度；无训练的共享抖动是廉价有效的补救。分块缩放格式因避免共享尺度而受益，支持"粗网格+共享尺度"导致失败的解释。失败的假设也已如实报告。

Abstract: Sampling-based planners such as MPPI and CEM query a learned world model thousands of times per control step, so low-bit world models are an obvious way to make model-based control cheaper on a robot. With round-to-nearest activation quantization and no calibration, we find that planning can collapse, by up to 100 points of success, while the world model's latent one-step prediction error stays no larger than that of a stochastic rounding scheme that plans well. At the same bit width, what decides the outcome is not the size of the rounding error but whether it persists across planner iterations and is shared across candidates. Round-to-nearest leaves an error that persists, and an iterating planner drifts toward the candidates that this error favors; in TD-MPC2, holding the same dither fixed within a step or an episode brings the drift back. Dither shared by all candidates and redrawn at every iteration removes most of the damage at the same bit width and without training: at least half of it in all 19 affected Meta-World cells (task $\times$ bit width) of TD-MPC2 (median 95%), in 4 of 5 DINO-WM cells, and in all 8 affected tasks of a 317M-parameter multi-task TD-MPC2. Captured as CUDA graphs on an Ampere GPU, a W8A4 integer TD-MPC2 world model runs its planning rollouts 1.9-3.3$\times$ and the whole planning step 1.3-1.5$\times$ faster than FP32, though not faster than BF16 with pre-converted weights; shared dither adds at most 2.3% latency. In TD-MPC2, block-scaled NVFP4 removes most of the damage and MXFP4 part of it, which ties the failure to coarse grids with a shared scale. Hypotheses, thresholds and analysis code were fixed before the data that test them, and the hypotheses that failed are reported.

</details>


### [80] [HolidayFactory 1.0: Simulating, Generating, and Evaluating Industrial Robot Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11789)
*Holiday Robotics,:,Jeongmin Lee,Taehyeon Kim,Dohyun Jang,Minji Lee,Seonghun Oh,Dongwoo Park,Geonhyup Lee,Jaehyung Kim,Jaemin Yoon,Jinseop Bok,Jungwoo Kim,Minseok Yang,Philsik Chang,Soomin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HOLIDAYFACTORY 1.0 是一个用于仿真、生成和评估工业机器人持续作业的框架，包含高保真接触/执行器/触觉仿真器、智能体化场景构建，以及不重置场景的 24/7-BENCH 七项工业任务评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人通常在"重置后开始"的回合中评估，而工业工作是结果会影响后续操作的重复性任务；大规模评估此类工作需要仿真关键物理交互、构建工业环境，并在场景状态持续时测量性能。

Method: HOLIDAY-SIM 在 Newton 基于 SDF 的流体弹性接触上扩展本构律与半隐式原始-对偶求解器，将状态化执行器动力学与关节运动耦合，并从仿真状态导出触觉与关节观测；智能体化世界构建通过几何与交互检查生成可仿真资产和场景变体，并基于任务条件提出评估供人工确认与可执行校验；24/7-BENCH 提供七项工业任务，在不重置场景下测量完成/失败/未完成作业数、吞吐、延迟与电机能耗。

Result: 受控实验刻画了所提出的接触、执行器与触觉模型，验证了验证引导的世界构建的有效性；场景与评估生成实验揭示了模型评论者的局限，以及确保生成评估忠实反映任务需求的困难。

Conclusion: 框架支持作业级评估（如脚本化轨迹/代码即策略），但尚未在持续运行中比较不同策略的效果。

Abstract: Robots are typically evaluated in episodes that begin from a reset, while industrial work consists of repeated jobs whose outcomes affect subsequent operations. Evaluating such work at scale requires simulation of task-critical physical interactions, construction of industrial environments, and measurements of performance as scene state persists. We present HOLIDAYFACTORY 1.0, a framework for simulating, generating, and evaluating industrial robot work. Given a human-specified workplace and task, the framework defines a world pairing a scenario of scene variants and process logic with an evaluation defining a job, metrics, and a rubric. HOLIDAYFACTORY 1.0 comprises three components. HOLIDAY-SIM extends Newton's SDF-based hydroelastic contact with additional constitutive laws and a semi-implicit primal-dual solver, couples stateful actuator dynamics to articulated motion, and derives tactile and joint observations from simulated states. Agentic world construction generates simulation-ready assets and scene variants using geometric and interaction checks, and proposes task-conditioned evaluations for human confirmation and executable checks. 24/7-BENCH introduces seven industrial tasks in which jobs are evaluated without resetting the scene, measuring completed, failed, and unfinished jobs, throughput, latency, and motor energy. Controlled studies characterize the proposed contact, actuator, and tactile models and examine the effectiveness of verification-guided world construction. Experiments on scene and evaluation generation further reveal the limitations of model critics and the difficulty of ensuring that generated evaluations faithfully capture task requirements. We examine job-level evaluations with scripted trajectories (e.g., code-as-policy) but have not yet compared policies over persistent runs.

</details>


### [81] [PAIR: Proprioception-Aligned Image-Ray Representations for Viewpoint-Robust Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11798)
*Sung-Wook Lee,Xuhui Kang,Dylan Losey,Yen-Ling Kuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PAIR，一个利用机器人本体感知（proprioception）对齐联合图像-射线（image-ray）表示的对比学习框架，通过让相似机器人状态在不同视角下的观测在表示空间中保持接近，为学习视角鲁棒特征提供结构化grounding信号，可训练DP或post-train VLA模型，在仿真和真实操作任务中相比无射线条件的行为克隆基线分别提升12.7%和26.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习在通过规模化实现可泛化机器人操作方面取得进展，但视角鲁棒性仍是主要瓶颈。视觉运动策略依赖视觉输入，而图像在视角变化下本质上是歧义信号：相似场景在不同视角下可能外观迥异，不同状态又可能共享部分视觉重叠。策略不仅要推断任务相关状态，还要推断该状态在特定相机配置下的视觉投影。这种视角相关外观与任务相关状态的纠缠导致学习低效并限制对未见视角的泛化。

Method: 指出现有方法的两点不足：相机射线条件（camera-ray conditioning）仅把相机信息作为额外上下文，并不能让视觉表示真正grounded于任务场景的视觉投影方式；而无相机上下文的几何对齐（geometric alignment）会因忽略视角相关投影而引入虚假对应。据此提出PAIR：一个使用机器人本体感知对齐联合图像-射线表示的对比学习框架，鼓励具有相似机器人状态的观测在跨视角的表示空间中保持接近。框架高度可适配：既可用于训练Diffusion Policy（DP），也可用于后训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型。

Result: 在带相机变化的仿真和真实世界操作基准中，PAIR稳定优于无射线条件的行为克隆基线，分别提升12.7和26.7个百分点，展现了对未见视角更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过本体感知作为桥梁对齐跨视角的图像-射线联合表示，是一种有效且结构化的视角鲁棒表示学习信号；PAIR作为通用可插拔框架，可为DP训练与VLA后训练提供一致的视角泛化增益。

Abstract: While imitation learning has made substantial progress toward generalizable robot manipulation through scaling, viewpoint robustness remains a major bottleneck. Visuomotor policies rely heavily on visual inputs, yet images provide an inherently ambiguous signal under viewpoint change: similar scenes may appear markedly different across viewpoints, while distinct states may share certain visual overlaps. A policy must therefore infer not only the task-relevant state, but also how it is visually projected under a particular camera configuration. This entanglement of viewpoint-dependent appearance and task-relevant state can make learning inefficient and limit generalization to unseen viewpoints. Recent work has begun to address this challenge through camera-ray conditioning and representation learning using geometric cues to induce invariance. However, providing camera information as additional context does not by itself ground visual representations in how the task scene is visually projected, while geometric alignment without camera context can introduce spurious correspondences by ignoring viewpoint-dependent projection. We propose PAIR, a contrastive learning framework that aligns joint image-ray representations using robot proprioception. PAIR encourages observations with similar robot states to remain close in representation space across viewpoints, providing a structured grounding signal for learning viewpoint-robust features. PAIR is a highly adaptable framework: it can be used to train Diffusion Policy (DP) or post-train vision-language-action (VLA) models. In simulated and real-world manipulation benchmarks with camera variation, PAIR consistently outperforms the behavior cloning baseline without ray conditioning by 12.7% and 26.7% points, respectively, demonstrating stronger generalization to unseen viewpoints. Project website: PAIRrobot.github.io.

</details>


### [82] [Budget-aware Correspondence-driven View Selection for Real-time 3D Perception of Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11809)
*Vaishnav Ramesh,Jacob Carter,Shivali J. Patel,Sanjeev J. Koppal,Md Jahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析并介绍 BCVS——一种无需位姿、基于特征对应打分的视图采样算法，可在 CPU 上达到 22+ FPS，实现移动机器人 3D 感知的在线图像冗余过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中机器人与智能交通系统会产生冗长冗余的图像序列，拖累下游 3D 感知与场景重建流程；现有方法依赖完整 SfM 流程或相机位姿估计，计算代价高、难以在线运行。

Method: BCVS 摒弃位姿估计，采用纯特征对应（correspondence-based）打分策略：在保证几何稳定所需重叠度的前提下优先选择视图新颖性；给定用户预算后迭代选取最具差异性的帧并抑制近重复帧，输出保持连通性、最小化重建损失的紧凑子集。

Result: 基准测试中，BCVS 在紧预算下优于各类 CPU 友好基线，预算增大时仍具竞争力；空中与海洋机器人实地数据试验显示其可将冗余降低最多 16%，同时保持稳健的 SfM 配准与高质量 3D 重建。

Conclusion: BCVS 以无位姿、CPU 实时的轻量设计实现了实用的在线视图选择，兼具效率与可扩展性，适合移动机器人端的 3D 感知前端过滤。

Abstract: 3D visual perception and scene reconstruction are critical for autonomous robots and intelligent transportation, yet real-world deployments often generate long, redundant image sequences that hinder downstream processing. This report investigates BCVS (budget-aware correspondence-driven view sampling), a pose-free view selection algorithm that offers 22+ FPS on a CPU, enabling online filtering for efficient 3D perception by mobile robots. Unlike existing approaches that rely on full structure-from-motion (SfM) pipelines or camera pose estimation, BCVS employs a purely correspondence-based scoring strategy that prioritizes view novelty while maintaining sufficient overlap for geometric stability. Given a user-specified budget, BCVS iteratively selects the most distinct frames and suppresses near-duplicates, producing compact subsets that preserve connectivity and minimize reconstruction loss. Extensive experiments on benchmark show that BCVS outperforms CPU-friendly baselines at tight budgets and remains competitive as budgets scale. Field trials with large aerial and marine robotics data further validate its practicality and scalability: BCVS reduces redundancy up to 16% while sustaining robust SfM registration and high-quality 3D reconstructions.

</details>


### [83] [A Benchmark and Layered Evaluation Protocol for Evidence-Grounded Shared Belief Updating in Connected Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11842)
*Ranulfo Bezerra,Kimitoshi Yamazaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出将互联机器人中的共享推理评估为"证据到信念"的轨迹，并发布CREG基准（350个确定性场景、7类证据情形、全证据前缀的规范性后验参照），用于测量方法是否归因证据、更新信念、处理矛盾并论证决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多只打分最终答案或动作，无法判断方法是真正归因了证据、正确更新了信念、处理了矛盾并为决策给出理由，因此需要一种能刻画中间推理轨迹的评测方式。

Method: 构建Connected Robotics Evidence Graph (CREG)基准：350个确定性生成场景覆盖7类情形（有据修正、信念保持、关系关联、矛盾出现与消解、陈旧/延迟证据、来源可靠性），在每个证据前缀给出规范性后验参照，并由独立对数几率校验器与盲审专家审核；设置9种表示条件以分离关系内容与序列化、排序、元数据的影响；评测4个开源指令模型与6个透明融合基线。

Result: 三点发现：(1) 实体接地解释了弱模型差距的相当一部分；(2) 删除语义关系使关系依赖场景的严格信念状态准确率下降15.3个百分点（Holm校正p<0.001），但信息等量的结构化列表优于完整图，打乱图结构也不损害性能——关系内容在需要时重要，图序列化并无稳定优势；(3) 信念准确率沿证据序列强烈非单调，存在仅看端点的评估无法发现的轨迹中段失败。

Conclusion: 应以"证据到信念轨迹"为互联机器人共享推理的评测对象，CREG提供了可测量该轨迹的基准；所有场景、参照、提示、指标与分析代码均已发布，支持离线、无需硬件的复现。

Abstract: Connected physical systems fuse observations from robots, IoT sensors, and edge-AI modules into a shared account of the world, yet most evaluations score only the final answer or action and cannot tell whether a method attributed the evidence, updated its belief, handled contradictions, and justified its decision. We argue that shared reasoning in Connected Robotics should be evaluated as an evidence-to-belief trajectory, and introduce the Connected Robotics Evidence Graph (CREG) Benchmark to make that trajectory measurable. CREG comprises 350 deterministically generated scenarios across seven regimes (justified revision, belief preservation, relation association, contradiction onset and resolution, stale/delayed evidence, and source reliability), each exposing a normative posterior reference at every evidence prefix, validated by an independent log-odds checker and screened by a blinded expert audit. Nine representation conditions separate relation content from serialization, ordering, and metadata. Evaluating four open-weight instruction models and six transparent fusion baselines yields three findings. First, entity grounding accounts for a substantial part of the weak-model deficit. Second, deleting semantic relations costs 15.3 points of strict belief-state accuracy on relation-dependent scenarios (Holm-corrected p<0.001), but an information-matched structured list outperforms the full graph and shuffling the graph does not hurt: relation content matters where required, while graph serialization confers no consistent advantage. Third, belief accuracy is strongly non-monotonic along the evidence sequence, with mid-trajectory failures that endpoint-only evaluation cannot detect. All scenarios, references, prompts, metrics, and analysis code are released for offline, hardware-free reproduction.

</details>


### [84] [Decoupled Asynchronous System for Humanoids (DASH): Latency-Agnostic Scaling of Vision-Language-Action Models for Humanoid Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11854)
*Bingzhang Wang,Nathan Zhao,Jialeng Ni,Jiajin Zheng,Yin Zhang,Kehua Chen,Jian Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DASH 将 VLA 策略解耦为慢速视觉语言骨干（System 2）与快速流匹配动作头（System 1），通过 LCM 并发运行，使控制频率只由动作头决定，从而在骨干从 0.8B 扩展到 8B 时仍保持 20 Hz 控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环控制中视觉语言骨干处于控制关键路径上：骨干计算期间机器人仍在运动，导致执行动作基于越来越过时的观测；骨干越大，反应性越差，在人形灵巧操作中尤为严重。

Method: 将策略拆分为慢速 VLM（System 2）与快速流匹配动作头（System 1），以 LCM 协议作为并发进程运行；动作头融合冻结骨干提供的陈旧语义与自身 ResNet 编码器的新鲜视觉；采用去同步训练使动作头经历部署时的陈旧性；并用自适应时间聚合器在可变推理延迟下对齐重叠的动作分块。

Result: 在将推理延迟计入仿真时间的异步 RoboTwin 基准上，DASH 在 0.8B–8B 骨干范围内稳定保持 20 Hz 控制，而同步方案从 9.7 Hz 降至 4.1 Hz，且各规模下成功率更高；在配备灵巧手的真实人形上，抓取放置成功率明显高于同步流水线。

Conclusion: 通过解耦骨干与动作头的异步架构，可在不牺牲模型规模的前提下保持高控制频率与更高任务成功率，验证了异步 VLA 在人形灵巧操作中的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models pair a vision-language backbone with an action head, but in closed-loop control the backbone sits on the control-critical path: while it computes, the robot keeps moving, so each executed action is conditioned on an increasingly stale observation. A larger backbone therefore directly blocks reactivity, especially in humanoid dexterous manipulation. We present DASH, a Decoupled Asynchronous System for Humanoids that removes this coupling. DASH splits the policy into a slow vision-language model (System 2) and a fast flow-matching action head (System 1) that run as concurrent processes over the LCM communication protocol, so the control rate is set by the action head alone, independent of backbone size. The action head fuses stale semantics from a frozen backbone with fresh vision from its own ResNet encoder, desynchronized training exposes it to the staleness it meets at deployment, and an adaptive temporal aggregator aligns overlapping action chunks under variable inference latency. On an asynchronous RoboTwin benchmark that charges inference latency in simulated time, DASH holds 20 Hz control rate from 0.8B to 8B backbones while the synchronous rate falls from 9.7 to 4.1 Hz, with higher success at every size. On a real humanoid with dexterous hands, DASH achieves clearly higher pick-and-place success than the synchronous pipeline.

</details>


### [85] [Fade to... Red? Scheduling Coloured Noise Exploration Supports Efficient Robot Learning with Reduced Movement Jerk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11868)
*Francisco M. López,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统比较白噪声、粉噪声、红噪声及gSDE共49种噪声颜色调度方案，在四个稀疏奖励操作任务上发现：训练末期噪声颜色不影响学习效率或最终性能，但向红噪声渐变可使探索抖动减半，故推荐红噪声调度作为离策略机器人学习的默认探索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续控制深度强化学习常用时间不相关的高斯白噪声驱动探索，导致动作抖动且探索受局部约束；而已有研究表明时间相关的有色噪声（尤其粉噪声）能带来更高效的探索。作者由此提出疑问：在训练过程中调度噪声颜色的变化，是否能优于白噪声用于连续控制机器人任务。

Method: 在Fetch机械臂与Shadow灵巧手上的四个稀疏奖励操作任务中，系统研究了白噪声到红噪声之间的49种线性调度方案，并与广义状态相关探索（gSDE）进行对比。

Result: 训练末期噪声颜色既不影响学习效率也不影响最终性能；向红噪声渐变在每个任务中都使探索抖动减半。综合学习效率、最终性能与平滑性，红噪声表现最佳：学习效率与粉噪声相当，而动作抖动约为其一半。

Conclusion: 红噪声是首选探索策略，建议将向红噪声渐变的探索调度器作为离策略机器人学习的默认方案。

Abstract: Exploration in deep reinforcement learning (RL) for continuous control is commonly driven by temporally uncorrelated Gaussian (white) action noise, which leads to jerky motions with locally constrained exploration. In contrast, temporally correlated coloured noise, particularly pink noise, has been shown to support more efficient exploration in RL. Motivated by these findings, we inquire whether scheduling changes to the noise colour over the course of training can outperform white noise for continuous control robotics. We perform a systematic study of 49 linear schedules between white and red noise, together with generalised state-dependent exploration (gSDE), on four sparse-reward manipulation tasks with a Fetch arm and a Shadow Dexterous Hand. We find that the noise colour at the end of training does not affect learning efficiency or end performance, whereas fading towards red noise halves the jerk of exploration in every task. Balancing learning efficiency, end performance and smoothness, red noise emerges as the preferred strategy: it learns as efficiently as pink noise with almost 2-fold less movement jerk. We therefore recommend exploration schedulers that fade towards red noise as the default for off-policy robot learning.

</details>


### [86] [Action Representation for Spatial Generalization in Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11869)
*Yuhang Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究用受控机器人剥离(peeling)基准比较世界坐标、物体坐标与表面坐标三种匹配的动作表征，发现空间泛化的关键在于动作表征是否保留决定物理后果的动作—状态关系，而非简单地最小化坐标变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人世界模型的空间泛化依赖于动作表征能否捕获在不同空间构型下依然成立的物理关系。然而同一交互可用不同坐标描述，相似运动在局部物理条件改变时也会产生不同结果；这些差异如何影响学习到的预测仍缺乏系统理解。

Method: 构建受控的机器人剥离(peeling)基准，定义在世界坐标系、物体坐标系和表面坐标系下相互匹配的动作表征，并设计全局位姿实验、接触重定位实验、剥离厚度信息移除实验、坐标系不确定性实验以及表面—工具联合中心化实验进行对比评估。

Result: 全局位姿与接触重定位实验显示，当等价交互获得不同空间描述时，有限训练数据带来明显代价；移除剥离厚度信息造成不可消除的歧义，相同预测输入可对应不同材料去除结果；基于表面的坐标在局部观测准确时改善后果预测与反事实动作选择，但预测器仍易受坐标系不确定性影响；表面与工具联合中心化可消除对共同原点平移的敏感性，而错误的接触几何与实际执行偏差则引入不同的失效机制。

Conclusion: 有效的空间泛化取决于能否保留动作与状态之间决定物理后果的关系，而不是单纯最小化坐标变化；这一视角为设计能在不同空间构型间复用物理知识、同时保留预测与规划所必需的区分信息的机器人世界模型提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Spatial generalization in robot world models depends on whether action representations capture the physical relationships that remain valid across changing spatial configurations. Yet different coordinates can describe the same interaction, whereas similar motions can produce different outcomes when local physical conditions change. How these distinctions affect learned prediction remains insufficiently understood. This study evaluates matched action representations defined in world, object, and surface coordinate frames using a controlled robotic peeling benchmark. Global pose and contact relocation experiments reveal a cost associated with limited training data when equivalent interactions receive different spatial descriptions. Removing information about peel thickness creates an irreducible ambiguity because identical predictive inputs can correspond to different material removal outcomes. Coordinates defined relative to the surface improve consequence prediction and counterfactual action selection under accurate local observations, but learned predictors remain vulnerable to uncertainty in the coordinate frame. Joint centering of the surface and tool removes sensitivity to translation of their common origin, whereas incorrect contact geometry and actual execution deviations introduce different failure mechanisms. These results show that effective spatial generalization depends on preserving the relationships between actions and states that determine physical consequences, rather than simply minimizing coordinate variation. This perspective provides a principled basis for designing robot world models that reuse physical knowledge across spatial configurations while retaining the distinctions essential to prediction and planning.

</details>


### [87] [Design of a hybrid tendon-linkage finger with coupled and adaptive motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11885)
*Hyojae Kang,Giup Seo,Jae-Young Lee,Yeong Jae Park,Jongwoo Park,Hyunmin Do,So-Hee Park,Dongil Park,Jeongdo Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由三个电机驱动、具有四个机械自由度的混合腱—连杆机器人手指，通过齿轮与连杆机构实现关节耦合与自适应，从而在不增加执行器的情况下获得协调运动与接触适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手指需要各关节协调运动并对物体接触具备适应性，但独立驱动多个关节会显著增加传动机构的复杂度，因此需要一种用较少执行器实现多自由度功能的机构方案。

Method: 设计混合腱—连杆手指：MCP外展/内收由锥齿轮驱动，MCP与PIP屈伸由各自的双向腱传动驱动；DIP不与PIP独立驱动，而通过带扭簧与机械限位的四连杆耦合；腱路径还引入MCP与PIP间的机械耦合。开展运动学分析描述耦合与自适应运动，并用ArUco视觉标记进行实验测量。

Result: ArUco测量表明耦合运动与理论预测吻合良好，而自适应运动受连杆部件干涉部分受限；样机对圆柱、长方体及不规则形状物体呈现不同接触姿态，指尖平均峰值力为4.56 N。

Conclusion: 该机构在无需为DIP关节增加执行器的前提下，同时提供了耦合运动与依赖接触的自适应能力，为简化多自由度灵巧手指的传动设计提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic fingers require coordinated joint motion and adaptability to object contact, while independently actuating multiple joints increases the complexity of the transmission mechanism. This paper presents a hybrid tendon--linkage robotic finger with four mechanical degrees of freedom driven by three actuators. The MCP abduction/adduction joint is driven through a bevel gear, while MCP and PIP flexion/extension are driven by separate bidirectional tendon transmissions. The DIP joint is not independently actuated but is coupled to the PIP joint through a four-bar linkage with a torsional spring and mechanical stopper. The tendon routing also introduces mechanical coupling between the MCP and PIP joints. Kinematic analysis was conducted to describe the coupled and adaptive motions, and experiments were performed to evaluate the joint motion characteristics. ArUco-based measurements showed that the coupled motion closely matched the theoretical prediction, whereas the adaptive motion was partially limited by interference between linkage components. The fabricated finger demonstrated different contact postures with cylindrical, cuboid, and irregularly shaped objects, and the mean peak fingertip force was 4.56 N. The proposed mechanism provides both coupled motion and contact-dependent adaptation without requiring an additional actuator for the DIP joint.

</details>


### [88] [Distilling Instructions into Weights: Closed-Form Edits Fix a Robot Policy's Misreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11894)
*Ali-Adeeb Abbas,Esmaeil Seraj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"闭式指令蒸馏"：无需梯度、训练数据或改指令，直接把显式改写的逐层效应拷贝进VLA策略语言模型MLP权重，修复"一步失败"的机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略常在任务的某一步失败。作者探究这种失败能否通过直接编辑策略权重来修复，而非依赖梯度、训练数据或修改指令；并以π0.5在RoboLab中"把积木放入碗"提前松手为案例。

Method: 先诊断：用21个决策在改写指令下重放，发现策略把"in the bowl"错误绑定到别的物体，属于语言接地错误。再修复：以闭式方式把显式改写的逐层效应蒸馏进语言模型MLP权重；并做对照实验——同尺寸随机方向更新、蒸馏到动作专家/动作头、针对感知/抓取/接触物理/夹爪阈值的编辑，以及7个盲审Claude Opus编辑器。

Result: 128个留出种子上成功率由27升到106（81修复、2破坏，p=7×10⁻²²）；同尺寸随机更新无变化。该编辑还提升另外2个任务且对剩余13个无可检测损害；存储0.16%参数的变体保留大部分效果。置于动作专家或动作头效果更小或无效果。工具在留出新种子上端到端复现（8/64→36/64）。仅拿到误读指令的编辑器通过留出评分。同权重重跑会使约1/3配对结果翻转。

Conclusion: 权重编辑在"已存在已知良好输入可供蒸馏"时最有效。评测应配对种子、重跑未编辑策略，并在留出种子上确认增益。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies often fail at one step of a task they can otherwise perform. We ask whether such a failure can be repaired by editing the policy's weights directly, without gradients, training data, or a change to the instruction. Told to put the cube then the banana in the bowl, a zero-shot $π_{0.5}$ vision-language-action policy in RoboLab releases the cube before it reaches the bowl. Replaying 21 of these decisions under rewordings shows that it binds "in the bowl" to the wrong object. Closed-form instruction distillation copies the per-layer effect of an explicit rewording into the language model's MLP weights. On 128 held-out seeds, success rises from 27 to 106 episodes, with 81 fixed and 2 broken ($p=7\times10^{-22}$), while an update of the same size in random directions leaves success unchanged. The edit also lifts two other tasks without detectable harm on the remaining 13, and a variant that stores 0.16% of the parameter count keeps most of its effect. Placed in the action expert or action head instead, the same distillation helps less or not at all. Our released tool reproduced the fix end to end on new held-out seeds, from 8/64 to 36/64. Edits aimed at perception, a missing grasp, contact physics, or the gripper threshold did not help, and of seven blind Claude Opus editors, only the one given the misread instruction produced an edit that passed held-out scoring. These results suggest that weight edits work best when a known-good input already exists to distil. Because reruns with unchanged weights flip about a third of paired outcomes, evaluations should pair seeds, rerun the unedited policy, and confirm gains on held-out seeds.

</details>


### [89] [Reliability-Adaptive Multimodal Relative Pose Tracking for Underwater Leader-Follower Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11940)
*Mingyang Yang,Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Jun Wang,David Chen Wang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可靠性自适应多模态感知框架，用于两台运动水下机器人之间的六自由度相对位姿跟踪；融合视觉、声学测距与水动力测量，按可靠性自适应加权，并以线性复杂度建模时序依赖，实现嵌入式实时估计。648次水池试验平均平移误差3.8 cm、姿态误差2.4°，Jetson AGX Orin上推理延迟17.62 ms，较Transformer融合基线加速2.69倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下leader-follower编队控制需要在感知退化与机载算力受限条件下持续获得相对位姿；单一模态（视觉、声学或水动力）在光照差、遮挡、噪声等情形下可靠性时变，难以稳定支撑闭环编队，因此需要能刻画模态物理特性与可靠性变化的高效多模态融合方案。

Method: 构建视觉、声学测距与水动力三类互补测量的融合框架：显式考虑各模态物理特性与时变可靠性，自适应分配模态权重；以线性计算复杂度建模时序依赖，从而支持嵌入式硬件上的高效位姿估计。基于自建AUV平台开展实验，覆盖2种leader载体、3种光照条件、9种轨迹模式共648次水池试验，并与Transformer融合基线对比，同时进行闭环编队验证。

Result: 平均平移误差3.8 cm，平均姿态误差2.4°；在机载NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上融合网络推理延迟17.62 ms，相对Transformer融合基线取得2.69倍加速；闭环实验中在运动leader与机载算力约束下实现稳定的编队保持。此外，包含全部648次试验测量的多模态数据集将公开。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可靠性自适应的多模态融合可在有限算力下兼顾精度与实时性，为水下编队相对位姿跟踪提供可行方案；大规模多条件试验与数据集公开也为其可复现性和后续研究提供支撑。

Abstract: Underwater leader-follower formation control requires continuous relative-pose estimation despite degraded sensing conditions and limited onboard computation. This paper presents a reliability-adaptive multimodal perception framework for six-degree-of-freedom relative-pose tracking between two moving underwater robots. The framework integrates complementary visual, acoustic-ranging, and hydrodynamic measurements while accounting for their distinct physical characteristics and time-varying reliability. It adaptively weights modality contributions and models temporal dependencies with linear computational complexity, enabling efficient pose estimation on embedded hardware. Extensive experiments were conducted using a custom-built AUV platform, with 648 pool trials spanning two leader vehicle types, three illumination conditions, and nine trajectory patterns. Evaluation yields an average translational error of 3.8 cm and an average orientation error of 2.4°. On an onboard NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, the fusion network achieves an inference latency of 17.62 ms, providing a 2.69-fold speedup over the evaluated Transformer-based fusion baseline. Closed-loop experiments further demonstrate stable formation maintenance with a moving leader under onboard computational constraints.
  A multimodal dataset containing measurements from all 648 trials will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [90] [Flip Anything with Agentic Robotics Self-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11941)
*Shuangyu Xie,Kaiyuan Chen,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文展示了一个编码智能体（coding agent）能在物理仿真中通过迭代学习，自主开发并改进双臂夹爪机器人的物体翻转策略，任务成功率从24.7%提升到95.0%，并在真实机器人上实现多阶段翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 翻转（将物体绕平行于支撑面的轴旋转180°）是工业检测、机械装配、工具使用等任务中的常见需求，但为双臂夹爪机器人编写能应对多样物体的翻转代码十分困难，因此作者希望让智能体自动习得该技能而无需人工逐物体编程。

Method: 采用编码智能体（coding agent）在物理仿真中针对不同几何形状的物体进行迭代学习，逐步习得从基础交接（handover）到推倒（toppling）与推挪（pushing），再到稳健抓取与感知，以及薄物体操作与失败恢复的分阶段技能，最终生成图结构（graph-based）的代码，将高层策略选择与反应式控制相结合。

Result: 任务成功率由24.7%提升至95.0%；所生成的图结构代码具备高层策略选择与反应式控制能力，并成功迁移到物理机器人上完成多阶段翻转。

Conclusion: 通过仿真中的迭代学习，编码智能体能够自主构建可解释、可迁移的图结构编程方案，实现多样物体的双臂翻转操作，说明该方法在复杂操作技能自动编程方面的潜力。

Abstract: Flipping is a fundamental robotic manipulation skill that requires reorienting a target object in a stable pose on a planar support surface by rotating it 180$^\circ$ about an axis parallel to the support surface -- think of flipping a coin from heads to tails. Although flipping is a common objective in industrial tasks such as parts inspection, mechanical assembly, and tool use, coding a bimanual robot with grippers to flip diverse objects is a significant challenge. In this paper, we show that a coding agent can autonomously develop and refine object-flipping strategies through iterative learning in physics simulations across diverse object geometries. Progressive skill acquisition, from basic handover to toppling and pushing, robust grasping and sensing, and thin-object manipulation with recovery, improves task success from 24.7% to 95.0%. The resulting graph-based code integrates high-level strategy selection with reactive control, enabling multi-stage flipping on physical robots.

</details>


### [91] [The Relative Value of Demonstration Data: Execution Time Limits and Operator Mixtures in Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11947)
*Jiaxuan Guo,Xingyu Ren,Youran Sun,Jingxin Yang,Kejia Zhang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"数据倍数"（data multiple）指标，用于衡量两组遥操作者（robomimic 中标记为 Better 与 Worse 的两组）所需示范数量的比值，以达到相同策略成功率，并在 Square 与 Can 任务上系统测量该倍数及其成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示范数据集常混合不同经验水平遥操作者的数据，采集团队需要知道一组示范相当于另一组多少条，以便合理配比与成本核算，但此前缺乏可比较的量化指标。

Method: 定义"数据倍数"为两组达到同一成功率所需示范数之比；在 robomimic 的 Square 和 Can 任务上训练行为克隆策略，改变数据集规模与 Worse 示范占比，并考察不同时间限制（20–200 秒）、按长度加权或均匀抽样等设置。

Result: Square 任务在 25 秒限制下，全 Worse 训练集所需示范数为全 Better 集的 1.55 倍；差异主要来自完成偏晚，限制放宽到 100 秒时倍数降至 1.11。Can 任务在 100 秒时差异仍大部分保留。Square 上半 Better 半 Worse 的数据集需 1.22 倍示范数；若按全 Worse 集"1/1.55 折算"预期，实际需求还多出 13%，且该超额在 20–200 秒各限制下均存在，改为均匀抽样训练也依然存在。考虑 Worse 示范录制更耗时，只有在 Worse 遥操作者时薪不到 Better 的三分之一时，等成功率下换人采集才更划算。

Conclusion: 数据倍数随任务与时间限制变化，且混合数据存在超出线性折算的额外代价；选择遥操作者需同时考虑示范数量与采集时长对应的经济成本。

Abstract: Robot demonstration datasets combine demonstrations from teleoperators of uneven experience, so collection teams need to know how many demonstrations from one group match those from another. We measure this with a data multiple: the ratio of the numbers of demonstrations two groups need for their policies to reach the same success rate. On robomimic's simulated Square and Can tasks, we train behavior-cloning policies on demonstrations from the two teleoperators robomimic labels Better and the two it labels Worse, varying dataset size and the share of Worse demonstrations. On Square, within robomimic's 25-second time limit, an all-Worse training set needs 1.55 times as many demonstrations as an all-Better set. Most of this difference is late completion: policies trained on Worse demonstrations finish later, as the Worse teleoperators do, and with a 100-second limit the multiple falls to 1.11. On Can, most of the difference remains at 100 seconds. If each Worse demonstration were worth 1/1.55 of a Better one, as in an all-Worse set, a half-Better, half-Worse set on Square would need 1.22 times as many demonstrations as an all-Better set. In fact, it needs 13% more than that, and this excess persists at every time limit from 20 to 200 seconds. The excess remains when training draws demonstrations uniformly instead of in proportion to their length. On Square, within the 25-second limit, switching to these Worse teleoperators is cheaper at equal success only if they earn less than a third of the Better teleoperators' hourly pay, because Worse demonstrations also take longer to record.

</details>


### [92] [CSG-Nav: Confidence-Aware Semantic-Geometric Fusion for Robust Exploration and Accurate Zero-Shot Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11978)
*Shoubin Chen,Rui Xu,Zehao Wu,Kai Zhang,Tao Huang,Zhou Liu,Qingquan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CSG-Nav，一个以置信度感知多模态融合为核心的零样本物体目标导航框架，通过语义源融合、空间置信度融合与语义-几何决策融合三种机制抑制长程感知不可靠、幻觉方向偏置与错误停止问题，在HM3Dv1/v2和MP3D上取得58.9%/71.6%/44.3%成功率，并完成真实环境部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本物体目标导航需在无任务训练条件下探索未知环境并定位目标，观测不确定性使感知可靠性不足、目标终止缺乏物理依据。现有基于基础模型的方法虽提升泛化性，但仍受三类问题困扰：长程感知不可靠、幻觉导致的方向偏置、以及语义上看似合理但几何上错误的停止决策。

Method: 围绕置信度感知的多模态信息融合构建三个互补机制：(1) 语义源融合——整合开放词汇检测结果、检测置信度、场景观测与关系推理，生成更可靠的方向级语义分数以抑制幻觉方向偏置；(2) 空间置信度融合——用高斯衰减函数对多视角语义证据加权，抑制不可靠的远距离观测并累积空间一致的探索偏好；(3) 语义-几何决策融合——将语义目标检测与基于深度的三维定位结合，支撑有物理依据的停止决策。

Result: 在HM3Dv1、HM3Dv2和MP3D上分别取得58.9%、71.6%和44.3%的成功率，零样本性能具有竞争力；融合层级消融实验以及室内外真实环境部署进一步验证了方法在不确定感知下的有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: 置信度感知的多模态融合是提升零样本物体目标导航鲁棒性的有效途径，可同时缓解感知幻觉、远距离观测噪声与错误终止问题，并具备向真实场景迁移的实用性；代码已开源。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation requires autonomous agents to explore unseen environments and locate target objects without task-specific training, posing challenges in perceptual reliability under observation uncertainty and physically grounded goal termination. Although foundation-model-based approaches improve generalization, they remain vulnerable to unreliable long-range perception, hallucination-induced directional bias, and semantically plausible but geometrically incorrect stopping decisions. To address these limitations, we propose CSG-Nav, a zero-shot navigation framework centered on confidence-aware multimodal information fusion. CSG-Nav integrates three complementary fusion mechanisms. First, semantic-source fusion integrates open-vocabulary detection results, detection confidence, scene observations, and relational reasoning to produce more reliable direction-level semantic scores and suppress hallucination-induced directional bias. Second, spatial-confidence fusion weights multi-view semantic evidence using a Gaussian decay function, suppressing unreliable distant observations while accumulating spatially consistent exploration preferences. Third, semantic-geometric decision fusion combines semantic target detection with depth-based 3D localization to support physically grounded stopping decisions. Experiments on HM3Dv1, HM3Dv2, and MP3D demonstrate competitive zero-shot performance, achieving success rates of 58.9%, 71.6%, and 44.3%. Fusion-level ablations and real-world deployments in indoor and outdoor environments further verify the effectiveness and practicality of CSG-Nav for robust navigation under uncertain perception. Code is available at https://xr-ui.github.io/CSGNav.github.io/

</details>


### [93] [Thermo-VLA: A Thermal-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model with Gated Residual Modulation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11981)
*Jingxuan Chen,Junshi Luo,Chencheng Dong,Xiaojian Nie,Tongge Huang,Qiang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Thermo-VLA模型，通过ThermoRes编码器将热成像分解为冷/热属性表征并用语言条件残差调制注入RGB token，实现温度感知的关系推理操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽引入热感知，但把热图像当作普通视觉输入，对温度感知的关系推理支持有限。

Method: 设计ThermoRes编码器，将热图像解耦为冷、热属性表示，通过语言条件残差调制注入RGB token，支持温度驱动的目标选择与连续操作；并在双臂Unitree G1平台上构建六个真实机器人任务（从基础定位到关系推理）。

Result: 在四个依赖热感知的任务上平均成功率0.86，较直接融合RGB-热观测的pi_0.5基线高18个百分点；在未见物体与场景变化下平均成功率0.78，泛化性能最优。将同一残差调制机制迁移到颜色属性后，仿真与真机实验均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 属性解耦+语言条件残差调制是有效的多模态注入方式，且该机制可超越热属性推广到颜色等其他属性。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have begun incorporating thermal sensing, but existing approaches typically treat thermal images as ordinary visual inputs, providing limited support for temperature-aware relational reasoning in manipulation. To address this limitation, we propose Thermo-VLA with a ThermoRes Encoder, which decomposes thermal images into cold and hot attribute representations and injects them into the RGB token through language-conditioned residual modulation, enabling temperature-driven target selection and continuous manipulation. We further construct six real-world robotic tasks on a dual-arm Unitree G1 platform, spanning basic grounding to relational reasoning. Experiments show that Thermo-VLA achieves an average success rate of 0.86 across four thermal-dependent tasks, outperforming a pi_0.5 baseline that directly incorporates RGB-thermal observations by 18 percentage points. Under unseen-object and scene variations, Thermo-VLA maintains an average success rate of 0.78, achieving the best generalisation performance among the compared methods. Beyond thermal sensing, we transfer the same residual modulation mechanism to colour attributes and observe consistent improvements in both simulation and real-robot experiments, indicating its potential to generalise beyond thermal attributes. Project website: https://j-x-chen.github.io/Thermo-VLA-web/

</details>


### [94] [OASIS: Optimization-Aware Subgoal Inference with Safety Constraints for Autonomous Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11996)
*Arian Hajizadeha,Javad Pourrafia,Negar Shenavara,Sana Bashiria,Seyed Majid Esmailzadeh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OASIS分层导航框架，将NMPC的优化结果反馈给基于SAC的高层子目标生成策略，从而缓解"几何上合理的子目标未必可解"的问题，在五个静态/动态障碍场景中取得最高综合导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境下移动机器人需在满足运动学约束、避障要求的前提下向目标推进；MPC受限于局部预测时域，需要学习型子目标策略引导。但仅从几何角度选取的合适子目标不一定能形成可行的控制问题，缺少对底层控制器可解性的反馈。

Method: 高层策略用Soft Actor-Critic生成局部子目标；底层用非线性MPC在运动学限制、几何避碰约束及嵌入式高阶控制屏障函数（HOCBF）约束下跟踪子目标。每个子目标区间内的控制器优化结果被总结为状态输入，在策略下一次决策时提供给策略，且不额外增加优化调用。对比包括分层与直接目标控制的变体，以及在三个训练随机种子下的匹配比较和不含优化状态输入的等价分层结构。

Result: 在五个含静态与移动障碍、密集交互与感知不确定性的导航场景中，OASIS的综合导航成功率最高；三个训练种子下相对无优化状态输入的相同分层结构均保持稳定优势；所选策略比较显示主要优化失败次数更少，平均控制器耗时相近。

Conclusion: 将控制器优化状态作为高层学习的反馈信号，是一种低成本且有效的提升分层导航可解性与成功率的方式。

Abstract: Navigation in dynamic environments requires mobile robots to make progress toward their goals while respecting motion limits and avoiding surrounding obstacles. Learned subgoal policies can guide model predictive control beyond its local prediction horizon, but a geometrically suitable subgoal does not necessarily lead to a successfully solved control problem. This paper proposes OASIS, a hierarchical navigation framework that incorporates recent optimization outcomes into reinforcement learning-based subgoal generation. A high-level policy trained using Soft Actor--Critic generates local subgoals, while a nonlinear model predictive controller tracks them subject to motion limits, geometric collision-avoidance constraints, and embedded high-order control barrier function constraints. The outcomes of controller optimizations over each subgoal interval are summarized and provided to the policy at its next decision, without requiring additional optimization calls. The framework is evaluated in five navigation scenarios with static and moving obstacles, including dense interactions and sensing uncertainty. Comparisons with hierarchical and direct-goal control variants show that OASIS achieves the highest aggregate navigation success. Matched comparisons across three training seeds further show a consistent success advantage over the otherwise identical hierarchy without optimization-status input. The selected-policy comparison also shows fewer primary optimization failures with similar mean controller time.

</details>


### [95] [Grasp-Taxonomy Conditioning as a Mode Switch for Dexterous Grasping of Climbing Holds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12017)
*Rumi Loghmani,Abraham George,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BetaGrasp，一种以四类抓握标签（crimp、sloper、pinch、jug）为条件的 3D 扩散策略（DP3 风格点云策略），并构建岩点抓握滑移力基准；带标签标签使正确抓握类型从 25% 提升至 94%，硬件上平均滑移力由 9.6 N 升至 14.3 N，完全脱落率由 44% 降至 10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人灵巧抓握依赖与物体几何相匹配的手部构型，但仅凭点云观测往往难以区分不同抓握类型所需的手部形态；作者希望探究点云编码器能否自主区分不同抓握类别，以及显式抓握标签是否必要。

Method: 在 Franka 机械臂 + 16 自由度 LEAP 手上，构建以四类抓握标签为条件的 DP3 风格点云扩散策略（BetaGrasp）；同时设计攀岩支点（climbing-hold）基准：弹簧测试台通过棘轮装置读取以牛顿为单位的滑移力。对比无标签的 DP3 基线，并分析点云编码器的类别可分性。

Result: 点云编码器未能区分四类抓握，单靠扫描信息几乎无法选择抓握方式；无标签基线从复位位姿出发的仿真 rollout 无论何种支点均收敛为 jug 抓握（抓握类型正确率 25%，BetaGrasp 为 94%）。在 80 组配对硬件试验中，BetaGrasp 将平均滑移力从 9.6 N 提升到 14.3 N，完全滑脱比例从 44% 降至 10%。

Conclusion: 抓握标签本质上充当"开关"，决定策略执行哪一类抓握；显式类别条件对灵巧抓握策略的有效性与鲁棒性至关重要，点云几何信息本身不足以支撑抓握类型选择。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping in robotics relies on distinct hand configurations depending on object geometry. We introduce BetaGrasp, a 3D diffusion policy that conditions a DP3-style point-cloud policy on a four-class grasp label (crimp, sloper, pinch, and jug), running on a Franka arm with a 16-DoF LEAP hand. We also introduce a climbing-hold benchmark: a spring testbed whose ratchet gives a slip-force reading in newtons. In our analysis the policy's point-cloud encoder did not separate the four holds, so the scan alone gave it little to choose a grasp from. Without the label (a DP3-style baseline), simulated rollouts from the robot's reset pose all end in a jug grasp, whatever the hold (25% correct grasp type, versus 94% for BetaGrasp). The label works as a switch for which grasp the policy performs. On 80 paired hardware trials, BetaGrasp raises average slip force from 9.6 N to 14.3 N, and grasps slipping fully off the hold drop from 44% of trials to 10%.

</details>


### [96] [DPGS-SLAM: Diffusion-Prior and Provenance-Aware Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Dynamic Visual Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12038)
*Yifan Luo,Takafumi Matsumaru,Vibekananda Dutta,Xin He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DPGS-SLAM 是一个面向动态场景的单目 RGB 高斯泼溅 SLAM 框架，通过异步扩散补全与"来源感知（provenance-aware）"高斯建图，对暂未观测到的静态背景进行临时表示，从而在排除动态观测的同时缓解遮挡背景缺失问题。平均 ATE RMSE 达 TUM RGB-D 1.2 cm、BONN 2.7 cm，在 StaDy4D 上较 WildGS-SLAM 平均 PSNR 提升 4.60 dB（仅用单目 RGB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态物体会破坏 3DGS-SLAM 的静态场景假设，导致跟踪与建图不稳定；而简单剔除动态观测又会造成被遮挡静态背景缺失或约束不足。作者希望在不牺牲背景完整性的前提下抑制动态干扰。

Method: 采用光流运动发现 + SAM2 时序一致掩码传播识别动态区域；对选定关键帧用微调扩散修复模型生成被遮挡区域的合理外观，并由 Metric3D v2 从补全图像预测度量深度以初始化"临时"高斯原语；通过来源标签区分生成原语与观测支持原语，生成内容不参与相机位姿估计与观测地图优化；当被遮挡背景重新可见时移除合格生成原语，并用真实 RGB 与预测深度独立初始化观测支持原语。整体为异步扩散背景补全 + 来源感知高斯建图的单目框架。

Result: 相机跟踪具有竞争力：平均 ATE RMSE 为 TUM RGB-D 1.2 cm、BONN RGB-D 2.7 cm；在 StaDy4D 上仅用单目 RGB 输入即比 WildGS-SLAM 平均 PSNR 高 4.60 dB。

Conclusion: 将扩散先验作为"临时背景表示"并结合来源标签管理，可在动态环境中兼顾跟踪鲁棒性与稠密建图质量，且无需深度传感器；生成内容与观测内容的严格隔离与替换机制是保证几何一致性的关键。

Abstract: This paper presents DPGS-SLAM (Diffusion-Prior and Provenance-Aware Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM) for dense mapping in environments containing dynamic objects. Dynamic observations can contaminate camera tracking and scene reconstruction in visual SLAM (simultaneous localization and mapping). Although 3DGS (3D Gaussian splatting) enables efficient dense mapping, existing 3DGS-SLAM systems may exhibit instability when dynamic objects violate their underlying static-scene assumption. Excluding dynamic observations can reduce their influence on tracking and mapping, but may leave occluded static backgrounds missing or weakly constrained. Our proposed DPGS-SLAM is a monocular RGB SLAM framework that supports provisional representations of insufficiently observed static backgrounds through asynchronous diffusion-based background completion and provenance-aware Gaussian mapping. Dynamic regions are identified through optical-flow-based motion discovery and temporally consistent mask propagation by SAM2 (segment anything model 2). For selected keyframes, a fine-tuned diffusion inpainting model generates plausible background appearance in occluded regions, while Metric3D v2 predicts metric depth from the completed images to initialize provisional Gaussian primitives. Provenance labels distinguish generated primitives from observation-supported primitives, excluding generated content from camera-pose estimation and observed-map optimization. When previously occluded backgrounds become visible, eligible generated primitives are removed, and observation-supported primitives are independently initialized from real RGB observations and predicted depth. Experimental results demonstrated competitive camera tracking, with average ATE RMSE values of 1.2 cm on TUM RGB-D and 2.7 cm on BONN RGB-D. On StaDy4D, DPGS-SLAM improved average PSNR by 4.60 dB over WildGS-SLAM using only monocular RGB input.

</details>


### [97] [Learning Contextual Motion Constraints for Object Rearrangement Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12072)
*Lexing Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Gang Zeng,Hangxin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双流神经网络MoCo-Net，从部分视角点云中联合预测铰接部件的连续运动范围与障碍物热力图，同时建模内部关节限制与外部障碍约束，为机器人构建紧凑配置空间以高效完成场景重排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接部件在杂乱环境中的运动既受自身关节极限（内在约束）限制，也受外部障碍物（外在约束）限制；但现有方法仅考虑物体铰接属性，忽略外部障碍，导致预测的运动范围不准确、跨场景泛化差。

Method: 构建双流神经网络MoCo-Net：一路预测铰接部件的连续可动范围，另一路预测标示潜在碰撞区域的障碍物热力图；两流共享部分视角点云输入并相互增强，从而同时捕获内部与外部运动约束，并据此为机器人构建场景配置空间，供定制规划器探索重排动作。

Result: 实验表明双流设计相互促进，运动范围预测与障碍识别均更准确；对新颖物体与新场景的泛化能力提升；生成的紧凑配置空间使定制规划器能高效完成场景重排。

Conclusion: 同时建模内在关节限制与外在障碍约束是提升铰接物体可动性预测的关键，MoCo-Net的双流联合预测为杂乱环境下的机器人重排任务提供了有效的配置空间表示。

Abstract: The motion of an articulated part in cluttered surroundings is constrained not only by an object's intrinsic joint limits but also by extrinsic obstacles. However, existing approaches primarily consider object articulation properties alone. Here we present Motion Constraint Prediction Network (MoCo-Net), a two-stream neural network that jointly predicts the continuous motion ranges of articulated parts and obstacle heatmaps highlighting potential collision regions from partial-view point cloud observation. By capturing both internal and external motion constraints, MoCo-Net produces more accurate feasible motion range prediction and obstacles recognition, improves generalization for novel objects in new scenes, and enables robots to construct a scene configuration space for efficient exploration of rearrangement actions. Our experiments show that MoCo-Net's two-stream design enhances each other to deliver more accurate predictions, which subsequently form a compact configuration space for a customized planner to efficiently rearrange the scene.

</details>


### [98] [SAM-VTP: Video Memory and Measured Geometry for Continuous Traversability Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12099)
*Lucas Hoefs,Hui Xie,Senjian An,Fangyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出SAM-VTP，通过微调SAM2的视频记忆通路实现稠密、无提示词的可通行性分割，并用深度几何量与离线连续监督提升预测一致性与精度，在闭环实车试验中显著减少规划失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的可通行性预测大多逐帧独立处理，导致预测在时间上不一致；同时仅凭外观推断地形几何不可靠，且二元标签无法表达"条件可行性"这类连续、概率化的通过难易程度。

Method: 1) 微调SAM2的视频记忆路径，使每帧分割以历史观测的持久记忆为条件，实现稠密、无需提示词的可通行性分割；2) 直接从深度计算坡度、粗糙度与高度，并融合进图像特征，而非从外观推断几何；3) 设计离线连续监督流水线，从深度几何与语义先验生成[0,1]的逐像素稠密可通行性标签，无需机器人部署与逐像素人工标注。

Result: 在TartanGround合成地面机器人数据集上，相较逐帧SAM2基线SAM-TP，模糊Jaccard由0.908升至0.924，二元IoU由0.854升至0.886，随机种子间波动小于0.001，帧间闪烁降低31%；在两个野外场地的闭环试验中，相同规划器与控制器下，基线因掉帧导致无可行路径的频率分别高出3.3倍（铺装路面）和6.9倍（崎岖地面）；SAM-VTP在机器人搭载的Orin Nano上达到21.4 FPS。

Conclusion: 引入视频记忆与深度几何监督能同时提升可通行性预测的精度、时间一致性与下游规划可靠性，并可在嵌入式平台实时运行，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Traversability prediction is a core component of autonomous navigation, yet most vision-based methods operate frame-wise and produce temporally inconsistent predictions. We present SAM-VTP (Video Traversability Prediction), which fine-tunes SAM2's video memory pathway for dense, prompt-free traversability segmentation, conditioning each frame on a persistent memory of prior observations. Rather than inferring terrain geometry from appearance, we measure it: slope, roughness and height are computed from depth and fused into the image features. We also introduce an offline continuous supervision pipeline that generates dense per-pixel traversability targets in [0,1] from depth-derived geometry and semantic priors, requiring no robot deployment and no per-pixel annotation, capturing conditional feasibility that binary labels cannot represent. On TartanGround, a large-scale synthetic ground-robot dataset, SAM-VTP improves fuzzy Jaccard from 0.908 to 0.924 and binary IoU from 0.854 to 0.886 over SAM-TP, a frame-wise SAM2 baseline, against a seed-to-seed spread below 0.001, and cuts frame-to-frame flicker by 31%. In closed-loop trials at two field sites, driving the same planner and controller, the baseline's dropouts leave the planner without a feasible path 3.3x more often on a paved course and 6.9x on rough ground, while SAM-VTP runs at 21.4 FPS on the Orin Nano the robot carries.

</details>


### [99] [SPARK: Simple Post-training for Adapting pRetrained Knowledge to Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12102)
*Taekyung Kim,Jeongeun Park,Byeongho Heo,Sungjoon Choi,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SPARK，一种用于将预训练视觉基础模型适配到机器人控制任务的后训练（post-training）方法，通过时间抽象与知识保持两个目标提升表征的控制相关性与语义保留性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练基础模型提供强通用视觉特征，但其预训练目标并非为机器人表征学习设计，嵌入偏向全局语义而缺乏控制所需的时间敏感信息；同时从零训练机器人模型受限于数据规模小、采集成本高和预训练算力开销大。

Method: SPARK 基于两个原则：(1) 时间抽象，促使编码器学习紧凑特征，保留理解时间变化与动作相关场景结构所需的信息；(2) 知识保持，将 patch 级表征与原始基础模型对齐，以保留有用的预训练语义。

Result: 在多个机器人基准上，SPARK 相比原始基础模型持续提升成功率与泛化能力，并证明这些增益可迁移到真实机器人操作任务。

Conclusion: 通过轻量的后训练即可将通用视觉基础模型高效适配为紧凑、语义有效且保留先验知识的机器人视觉状态表征，避免昂贵的大规模重训练。

Abstract: Large-scale representation learning has produced public foundation models that provide strong general-purpose visual features. However, their pretraining objectives are not designed for robot representation learning, so the resulting embeddings tend to emphasize global semantics rather than the temporally sensitive representations required for robot control. Training robot models from scratch is also undesirable due to the limited scale and high collection cost of robotics data, as well as the substantial computational burden of pre-training. In this paper, we propose SPARK (Simple Post-training for Adapting pRetrained Knowledge), a post-training method that adapts foundation models to robot control. SPARK is built on two principles: temporal abstraction, which encourages the encoder to derive compact features that preserve information essential for understanding temporal changes and action-relevant scene structure, and knowledge preservation, which aligns patch-level representations with those of the original foundation model to retain useful pretrained semantics. These objectives yield compact visual state representations that remain semantically meaningful while preserving useful prior knowledge. Experiments across multiple robotics benchmarks show that SPARK consistently improves success rates and generalization over vanilla foundation models, and further demonstrate that these gains transfer to real-world robot manipulation.

</details>


### [100] [GAP: Generalized Adaptation Prior for Dexterous Piano Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12111)
*Jinyi Chen,Hongyi Li,Lei Jiang,Xianwei Liu,Yanhong Liang,Yulin Chen,Yucheng Tao,Yongbin Jin,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GAP框架：在仿真预训练阶段学习"如何适应"而非"如何行动"，将适应先验迁移到真实灵巧手完成钢琴演奏，显著降低真机交互成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手操作能力强，但仿真训练的策略因非理想因素在硬件上性能退化，且高维动作空间使真实世界自适应代价高昂。钢琴演奏需要精确协调的多指运动，是有代表性的测试平台。

Method: GAP（Generalized Adaptation Prior）在多次重复执行之间利用反馈调整下一次执行的动作：训练一个梯度引导网络，从成对执行数据中学习"动作调整如何影响演奏表现"，并用其梯度指导动作修正；该适应先验在仿真中预训练后迁移至硬件。

Result: 相同交互预算下，仿真中F1提升约为现有快速适应基线的2.4倍；学到的先验可泛化到预训练未见的曲目；在真实灵巧手上，同一仿真预训练先验经约7分钟适应后，在三首不同难度曲目上F1提升3.6%–23%。

Conclusion: 学习"适应先验"是降低灵巧手真实部署所需交互量的可行路径。

Abstract: Dexterous hands enable versatile manipulation, but policies trained in simulation often degrade on hardware owing to non-idealities, and the high-dimensional action space makes real-world adaptation costly. Piano playing, which requires precise and coordinated multifinger motion, offers a representative testbed. We propose GAP (Generalized Adaptation Prior for Dexterous Piano Playing), a sample-efficient adaptation framework that learns how to adapt, rather than how to act, during simulation pretraining and transfers this adaptation prior to hardware. Between repeated executions, GAP uses feedback to adjust the actions of the next one: a gradient guidance network learns from paired executions how adjusting actions affects playing performance, and its gradients guide these adjustments. In simulation, GAP yields approximately 2.4 times the F1 scores improvement of the existing fast-adaptation baseline for dexterous piano playing under the same interaction budget. The learned prior also generalizes to musical pieces held out from pretraining. On a physical dexterous hand, the same simulation-pretrained prior improves F1 scores by 3.6%-23% across three pieces of varying difficulty after approximately 7 min of adaptation each. These results suggest a practical route to reduce the real-world interaction needed to deploy dexterous hands.

</details>


### [101] [Motion Belief Action: From a Visual Scene Map to Physical Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12113)
*Dibyendu Ghosh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出运动信念动作（MBA）决策层，在力限制下结合物体被移动频率、所需力、负载与受阻状态，决定"再看、轻推、打开、支撑或放弃"，风险水平由任务代价设定；在9个厨房与17处真实拉取中优于FreMEn、Perpetua、持续性滤波及按零件类型取均值等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人群中工作的机器人必须判断看不见的东西：物体是否还在上次看到的位置，拉动门或抽屉是否值得冒险。现有方法各有缺口——跟踪物体变化的地图不含力信息；物理属性地图只在固定风险水平上回答风险问题；视觉-语言-动作模型不保留对隐藏物理的显式估计。

Method: 提出MBA（Motion Belief Action），作为场景地图之上的决策层。依据人移动物体的频率、所需力、承载负载以及是否受阻，在力上限约束下于"再看一次、轻推、打开、支撑、放弃"中选择，其风险设定由任务代价决定。在真实场景中与FreMEn、Perpetua、持续性滤波、零件类型均值及已发表风险规则对比；在仿真中结合SmolVLA与Octo验证。

Result: 在9个厨房的真实活动中，MBA预测物体是否仍在原位优于FreMEn与Perpetua，且比以前两者和持续性滤波更低成本地决定何时再看（约四分之一放置会在30秒内被取走）。在17处地点的真实门/抽屉拉取（由带力传感夹爪的人执行）中，地图的力估计优于零件类型均值；由代价设定风险也优于已发表风险规则在其固定水平下的表现。仿真中，在训练部件中位力作为力上限时，MBA通过"放弃拉取"提升SmolVLA与Octo的得分；多数被放弃的门拉取本会失败，但抽屉上更多本可打开，零件类型均值仅获得约一半增益。

Conclusion: 已发表风险规则在取这些代价所隐含的风险水平时即为MBA针对单次拉取的规则，并在主要设置中表现相当。开启力与承载力的关联、试探性轻推以及用于相机跟踪的动作先验在主要设置中无帮助，且轻推在真实拉取扫描中略有代价。

Abstract: A robot that works among people must judge what it cannot see: whether an object is still where it was last seen, and whether a pull on a door or drawer is worth its risk. Maps that track object change hold nothing about force, maps of physical properties answer a risk question at a fixed risk level, and vision-language-action models keep no explicit estimate of hidden physics. We propose Motion Belief Action (MBA), a decision layer on the scene map. From how often people move an object and how much force it needs, how much load it holds and whether it is blocked, MBA chooses under a force limit to look again, nudge, open, brace or decline, with its risk set by the task's costs. On real activity in 9 kitchens, it predicts whether an object is still in place better than FreMEn and Perpetua, and decides when to look again at lower cost than these and the persistence filter, because about a quarter of placements end in a pick-up within 30 s. On real pulls of doors and drawers in 17 locations, made by a person with a force-sensing gripper, the map's force estimates decide better than a part-type average. Setting the risk from the costs also beats a published risk rule at its fixed level. In simulation, with the force limit at the training parts' median force, MBA raises SmolVLA's and Octo's scores by declining pulls; most declined door pulls would fail, but on drawers more would have opened, and a part-type average gets about half of this gain. In both, a published risk rule set to the risk these costs imply is MBA's rule for a single pull and decides as well in the main settings. A link between opening and holding force, a probing nudge and an action prior for camera tracking do not help in the main settings, and over the real-pull sweep the nudge costs a little.

</details>


### [102] [Compact and Wide-Force-Range Magnetorheological Hand Exoskeleton for Kinesthetic Feedback in Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12130)
*Xianlong Mai,Rui Yu,Rui Chen,Daniele Leonardis,Wenbo Li,Domenico Chiaradia,Antonio Frisoli,Shuaishuai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于磁流变（MR）制动器的手部外骨骼系统，可在遥操作中提供大范围、紧凑可穿戴的力觉反馈。MR制动器输出0.01–0.918 Nm扭矩，整机质量仅39.8 g，指尖力可达21 N，反向驱动力1.01 N。实验表明用户仅凭力觉即可辨别物体刚度；遥抓取任务中成功率均为100%时，力觉反馈使接触力比纯视觉反馈降低28.2%，问卷显示可靠性、真实感与信心提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部力觉反馈对于机器人手部遥操作中恢复和增强人对力与位移的感知至关重要。宽范围的力反馈对渲染不同刚度物体（尤其是高刚度渲染）十分关键，但现有方法难以在保持紧凑、可穿戴设计的同时实现宽范围力反馈。

Method: 设计一种采用磁流变（MR）制动器的手部外骨骼系统，在遥操作中提供宽范围力觉反馈；MR制动器提供0.01–0.918 Nm制动扭矩，结构紧凑、总质量仅39.8 g；外骨骼可渲染最高21 N指尖力，同时保持1.01 N的反向驱动力。通过刚度辨别实验与遥抓取任务（力觉反馈 vs 仅视觉反馈）进行验证，并结合问卷调查评估主观体验。

Result: 系统允许用户仅凭力觉反馈辨别物体刚度；在遥抓取任务中，两种条件下成功率均为100%，而力觉反馈使物体接触力相比仅视觉反馈降低28.2%，提升了对象安全性与操作者沉浸感；问卷结果表明力觉反馈改善了遥操作中的可靠性、真实感与用户信心。

Conclusion: 基于MR制动器的手部外骨骼以紧凑轻量设计实现了宽范围力觉反馈，兼顾高刚度渲染与低反向驱动力，能有效支持刚度辨别并提升遥抓取的安全性与沉浸感，具有良好的可穿戴遥操作应用潜力。

Abstract: Hand kinesthetic feedback is crucial for restoring and enhancing human perception of force and displacement in robotic hand teleoperation. Wide perception range of force feedback is vital in rendering objects of different stiffness, especially for high-stiffness rendering. However, existing approaches struggle to achieve wide range of force feedback while maintaining compact and wearable design. This paper presents a hand exoskeleton system employing magnetorheological (MR) brakes to provide a wide range kinesthetic feedback during teleoperation. The MR brake provides a braking torque ranging from 0.01 to 0.918 Nm, while maintaining a compact structure with a total mass of only 39.8 g. The proposed exoskeleton can render fingertip forces up to 21 N while maintaining back-driving force of 1.01 N. Experimental results show that the system allows users to discriminate object stiffness using kinesthetic feedback alone. In a tele-grasping task, when both conditions achieved a 100% success rate, kinesthetic feedback led to a 28.2% reduction in object contact force compared to visual feedback only, enhancing object safety and operator immersion. Questionnaire results further indicate that kinesthetic feedback improves reliability, realism, and user confidence during teleoperation.

</details>


### [103] [LHNav: Long-Horizon Humanoid Navigation via Multirate Coordination and Physics-Grounded Action Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12136)
*Xueyang Wang,Zehong Shen,Dingkun Wei,Huaijin Pi,Changpeng Wang,Xing Zhu,Hujun Bao,Yujun Shen,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LHNav 提出"大脑—小脑—脑干"三层异步架构，将长程语言指令导航拆分为任务推理、局部运动生成与全身执行三个时间尺度，实现多目标连续导航与自然、忠实路径的全身运动，仿真单目标导航优于 OmniNav/DualVLN，长程任务成功率 75.56%，并在 Unitree G1 上完成真机零样本长程任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，任务分解本身无法保证执行过程中的持续进展；而基于路点的导航也无法保证自然、忠实路线的全身运动。机器人需要在穿越狭窄空间时连续产生自然稳定的全身动作，同时保持对连续目标的进度。

Method: 核心思想是按不同时间尺度组织三种互补角色：Brain 负责任务推理，Cerebellum 负责局部运动生成（基于 VLA 的 motion-token 预测），Brainstem 负责全身控制，并用进度与到达反馈进行异步协调。为训练 Cerebellum 生成自然、忠实路线的动作，构建物理落地的数据引擎，提升沿避障路线对运动参考的物理跟踪，并将示范与渲染视觉观测配对。

Result: 仿真中导航 VLA 在单目标成功率与运动平滑度上优于 OmniNav 与 DualVLN，碰撞与摔倒更少；仅用单目标示范训练的 VLA，在长程导航任务上达到 75.56% 成功率；在 Unitree G1 上完成真机单目标导航及零样本长程任务执行，包括物体位置改变的场景布局。

Conclusion: 通过多时间尺度的三层角色划分与异步协调机制，任务规划可与连续运动并行进行，并将单目标导航扩展到连续多目的地，且保持自然、忠实路线的全身运动，具备较强仿真与真机泛化能力。

Abstract: We study humanoid navigation across multiple destinations under long-horizon language instructions. This task is challenging because robots must maintain progress across successive goals while continuously producing natural, stable whole-body motion through confined spaces. However, task decomposition in existing methods does not by itself ensure progress throughout execution, while waypoint-based navigation does not guarantee natural, route-faithful whole-body motion. Our key idea is to organize navigation into three complementary roles operating at different time scales: a Brain for task reasoning, a Cerebellum for local motion generation, and a Brainstem for whole-body execution. We realize this idea in LHNav, an asynchronous system that uses progress and arrival feedback to coordinate navigation goals, VLA motion-token prediction, and whole-body control. To train the Cerebellum for natural, route-faithful motion, we develop a physics-grounded data engine that improves physical tracking of motion references along collision-aware routes and pairs the resulting demonstrations with rendered visual observations. Together, these designs allow task planning to proceed alongside continuous movement and extend single-goal navigation to successive destinations with natural, route-faithful whole-body motion. In simulation, our navigation VLA outperforms OmniNav and DualVLN in single-goal success and motion smoothness, with fewer collisions and falls. Using a VLA trained only on single-goal demonstrations, LHNav achieves a 75.56% success rate on long-horizon navigation tasks. Deployment on the Unitree G1 further demonstrates single-goal navigation and zero-shot long-horizon task execution in real-world environments, including layouts with repositioned objects. Project website: https://robbyant-research.github.io/LHNav/.

</details>


### [104] [BioMARS 2.0: a robotic biologist for formulating and adapting biological experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12139)
*Yibo Qiu,Zan Huang,Yilin Qiao,Ziyang Ling,Yi Wang,Zhengyang Lu,Hangke Peng,Haoliang Ye,Ao Tian,Shu'ang Sun,Handi Liu,Yuqing Long,Mohan Yang,Kan Ding,Zhenzhen Zhou,Di Wu,Yu Xiong,Yaxuan Han,Jun'ao Song,Haoran Zhang,Ronald X Xu,Mingzhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BioMARS——一个人在回路的多智能体机器人系统，将知识引导的实验决策与机器人执行、生物反馈相连接，在流程、参数和干预三个抽象层级上实现反馈驱动的自动化生物实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物实验无法简化为单一优化问题，需要在多个抽象层级上做决策（从文献知识到实验流程、从表型反馈到培养条件调整、从证据到干预假设）。单纯自动化实验执行本身无法决定"下一步做什么实验"以及"如何根据生物反馈调整实验"，因此需要将决策与执行和反馈闭环结合起来。

Method: 构建BioMARS多智能体机器人系统（人在回路），分三个层级：流程层生成有文献依据的方案与可追溯行动清单；参数层利用机器人视网膜类器官培养的图像表型选择后续培养条件；干预层基于生物医学证据提名药物重定位候选，并交由研究者开展湿实验验证。在四个模型后端上进行专家评审对比。

Result: 流程层在四个模型后端上均提升了专家评审的协议质量分数；参数层在三轮实验中，后续轮次报告的分数更高；干预层提名的候选中有六个进入研究者的湿实验测试。

Conclusion: 由反馈驱动的机器人生物实验可在同时涵盖实验流程、培养条件与生物学干预决策的统一框架内实现，表明多智能体决策与机器人执行、生物反馈的耦合是可行的，且分层级的决策抽象是自动化实验的重要组织方式。

Abstract: Biological experimentation is not reducible to a single optimization problem, but requires decisions at multiple levels of abstraction: translating scientific knowledge into experimental procedures, adapting culture conditions to measured phenotypes and selecting interventions that test biological hypotheses. Automating experimental execution does not, by itself, determine which experiments should be performed next or how they should change in response to biological feedback. Here we introduce BioMARS, a human-in-the-loop multi-agent robotic system that connects knowledge-guided experimental decision-making with robotic execution and biological feedback.
