At the procedural level, BioMARS generated literature-grounded protocols and traceable action plans, improving expert-reviewed protocol quality scores across four model backends. At the parametric level, BioMARS used image-derived phenotypes from robotic retinal organoid culture to select subsequent experimental conditions, with higher reported scores in later rounds of the three-round experiment. At the intervention level, BioMARS nominated drug-repurposing candidates from biomedical evidence and advanced six candidates to wet-lab testing by researchers. Together, these studies demonstrate feedback-driven robotic biological experimentation within a framework that supports decisions about experimental procedures, culture conditions and biological interventions.

</details>


### [105] [GeoMind: A Benchmark for Spatial Understanding in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12175)
*Jinghe Wang,Xinrui Cao,Duo Wu,Chenghao Gu,Yong Zhong,Linjia Kang,Tianyi Xiong,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 GeoMind，一个面向桌面操作的结构化空间理解基准，包含空间推理与空间记忆两类共 54 个任务变体，并配有可扩展的自动化任务生成与标注流程；实验显示现有具身智能体在空间理解上普遍薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界中的可靠操作需要稳健的空间理解，即复杂布局下的目标识别（空间推理）以及随交互而持续演化场景的适应能力（空间记忆）。但现有具身基准存在两点不足：一是常引入强视觉线索，从而绕过了真正的空间推理；二是聚焦静态场景，难以反映智能体的空间记忆能力。

Method: 构建 GeoMind 基准：以桌面操作任务为载体，围绕空间推理与空间记忆两个维度组织 54 个评测任务变体；同时设计可扩展的数据流水线，自动生成并标注任务实例，保证目标可验证、交互可执行。

Result: 对多种具身智能体开展广泛评测，发现它们在空间理解上表现出一致的薄弱之处：难以在空间变化后持续追踪目标身份，也难以将关系性或几何性约束转化为可执行动作。

Conclusion: GeoMind 填补了具身基准在结构化空间理解评测上的空白，揭示了当前智能体在空间推理与空间记忆上的共性缺陷，并开源基准以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation has advanced rapidly in mapping visual observations and language instructions to actions. However, reliable manipulation in open-world environments requires robust spatial understanding: the ability to identify targets in complex layouts (spatial reasoning) and adapt to scenes that continuously evolve through interaction (spatial memory). Yet existing embodied benchmarks fall short in evaluating spatial understanding: they often introduce strong visual cues that bypass spatial reasoning, and focus on static scenes that offer little insight into an agent's capacity for spatial memory. To address this gap, we introduce GeoMind, a systematic benchmark for structured spatial understanding through tabletop manipulation. GeoMind comprises 54 evaluated task variants organized around spatial reasoning and spatial memory. It also incorporates a scalable pipeline that automates the generation and annotation of task instances with verified targets and executable interactions. Extensive evaluations across diverse embodied agents reveal their consistent weaknesses in spatial understanding. Our findings highlight that these agents struggle to track identities after spatial changes and convert relational or geometric constraints into executable actions. We release the GeoMind benchmark at https://github.com/Notfound-JH/GEOMIND-benchmark.git.

</details>


### [106] [Embodied First Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12177)
*Kangrui Wang,Qineng Wang,Changhe Chen,Pingyue Zhang,Chi Gui,Feishi Wang,Canyu Chen,Wenli Xiao,Xiaoyu Huang,Tonghe Zhang,Linjie Li,Zihan Wang,Minyeong Kim,Yiyou Sun,Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou,Qi Zhu,Han Liu,Eric P. Xing,Huaxiu Yao,Zhiwen Fan,Yuejiang Liu,Dawn Song,Pieter Abbeel,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Yuke Zhu,Guanya Shi,Ruohan Zhang,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"Embodied First Exam"——一个面向编码智能体（coding agents）的统一、全面、原生智能体式评测框架，覆盖35个套件、1156个任务、47种机器人，支持直接控制、写代码控制、训练策略控制三种自选方式，并设标准、实时、特权三种评测设置。在456个核心任务上最强智能体成功率仅34%，并通过失败阶段标注分析失败原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准围绕视觉-运动策略学习构建，而近期智能体式机器人学的早期成功暗示了用编码智能体控制机器人的范式转变可能；但编码智能体能否在物理世界约束下可靠控制多样机器人仍是开放问题，缺少相应评测。

Method: 构建统一的智能体原生评测框架：允许智能体自行选择控制方式（直接控制、代码控制、训练策略控制）；任务集涵盖35个套件共1156个任务、47种机器人，其中9个新套件超越抓取放置，涵盖空间谜题、灵巧操作、多机器人交互、动态全身控制与sim-to-sim工程；每个任务可在三种设置下运行——标准设置（仅传感器观测、默认不可重置环境）、实时变体（环境在智能体推理时持续运行）、特权设置（可访问完整仿真状态与无限重置，用于搜索oracle解，成功率作为近似上界）；并对失败尝试所处的阶段进行标注，跨智能体、设置与阶段类别比较失败模式。

Result: 在456个核心任务的Standard Setting中，八个智能体中最强的GPT-6 Astra（运行于Codex）成功率仅34%，表明仍有较大提升空间；通过对失败阶段标注的对比分析，揭示了智能体在不同设置与阶段类别下的失败分布。

Conclusion: 编码智能体在物理世界约束下的多样机器人控制仍远未成熟，该基准可作为编码智能体进入物理世界前的"第一场考试"，并为后续改进（尤其是失败阶段定位）提供统一、可比较的评测基础。

Abstract: Early successes on agentic robotics point to a potential paradigm shift toward using coding agents to control robots. Existing robotics benchmarks are built around visuomotor policy learning, leaving it open whether coding agents can reliably control diverse robots under physical-world constraints. We therefore introduce the Embodied First Exam, a unified, comprehensive, and agent-native evaluation framework where coding agents can choose their own control approach: direct control, code to control, or train a policy to control. We hope it serves as a first exam for coding agents before they enter the physical world, with 1,156 tasks in 35 suites on 47 robots, including 9 new suites that go beyond pick-and-place to spatial puzzles, dexterous manipulation, multi-robot interaction, dynamic whole-body control, and sim-to-sim engineering. Each task can be run in up to three evaluation settings. The Standard Setting mimics the real world: the agent receives only sensor observations and, by default, cannot reset the environment. Its Realtime variant keeps the environment running while the agent reasons. The Privileged Setting gives the agent access to the full simulator state and unlimited resets, allowing it to search for an oracle solution for training purposes. Success rates in this setting serve as an approximate upper bound. On the 456 core tasks, the strongest of eight agents, GPT-6 Astra in Codex, achieves a success rate of 34% in the Standard Setting, leaving much room for improvement. To see where agents fail, we label the stage at which failed attempts go wrong and compare failures across agents, settings, and stage categories.

</details>


### [107] [Graph-Based RL for Sequential Rendezvous Planning with Multiple Patrolling USVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12205)
*Seung Woo Han,Hyeon Bin Yeo,Seung Heon Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出融合栅格路径规划、代理旅行时间预测与图强化学习的框架，用于多无人水面艇巡逻任务中指挥艇的会合路径规划与优先级感知的会合排序，显著降低任务完成时间与总延误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多USV巡逻任务中，指挥艇需依次与各巡逻艇会合以支持编队管理。该过程既要生成运动可行的路径（受洋流与转艏率约束），又要在依赖优先级的截止期下安排会合顺序，以最小化任务完成时间与总延误；直接反复调用路径搜索代价过高。

Method: 1）基于栅格的路径规划器考虑洋流，并通过多阶段机动近似转艏率约束，路径可行性用轨迹跟踪仿真验证；2）构造可行转移图（FTG）表示候选会合事件与时间可行的转移，用图神经网络挖掘时空会合关系进行策略学习；3）用轻量代理模型基于紧凑的洋流与运动特征预测旅行时间，避免在FTG构建与策略训练中重复路径搜索。

Result: 在栅格路径规划评估下，目标值平均降低约10.5%（对比贪心）、3.5%（对比MLP-RL）、1.5%（对比两步前瞻贪心），且计算时间约为两步前瞻贪心的1/35；在未见过的优先级层级设定下仍优于MLP-RL，且优势随舰队规模增大而扩大。

Conclusion: 将可行性感知的图表示与GNN策略学习、以及快速旅行时间代理相结合，可在保证路径运动可行的前提下高效求解优先级感知的会合排序问题，兼顾解质量与计算效率，并具备良好的泛化性与可扩展性。

Abstract: In patrol missions involving multiple unmanned surface vehicles (USVs), a designated command USV can support fleet management by sequentially rendezvousing with each patrolling USV. These operations require motion-feasible route planning and priority-aware rendezvous sequencing, with the objective of minimizing mission completion time and total tardiness relative to priority-dependent due dates. We propose a framework combining grid-based path planning, surrogate travel-time prediction, and graph-based reinforcement learning. The path planner accounts for ocean currents and approximates turn-rate constraints through multi-stage maneuvers, with path feasibility empirically validated through trajectory-tracking simulations. For rendezvous sequencing, a Feasible Transition Graph (FTG) represents candidate rendezvous events and temporally feasible transitions, enabling a graph neural network to exploit spatiotemporal rendezvous relationships for policy learning. A surrogate predicts travel times from compact current and motion features to avoid repeated path searches during FTG construction and policy training. Evaluation using grid-based path planning shows statistically significant average objective reductions of 10.5%, 3.5%, and 1.5% compared with greedy, MLP-RL, and 2-step lookahead greedy, respectively. The policy achieves these improvements while requiring approximately 1/35 of the latter's computation time. It maintains its advantage over MLP-RL under unseen priority-tier settings, with larger gains as fleet size increases.

</details>


### [108] [A Game-Theoretic MPC Planner for Cooperative and Adversarial Drone Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12226)
*Zdeněk Bouček,Miroslav Flídr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种用于无人机协作到达目标与对抗追逃任务的博弈论模型预测控制（MPC）规划器，结合3D预测、个体目标、可选共享分离约束与轨迹跟踪接口；通过比较协作博弈求解器、分析计算复杂度影响，并用仿真与实机飞行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机在协作与对抗场景下需要实时可行的轨迹规划，但博弈论MPC的计算负担与实时性边界尚缺乏系统量化的认识。

Method: 构建博弈论MPC规划器，集成3D预测、个体目标、可选共享分离约束与轨迹跟踪接口；对比不同协作博弈求解器求解冲突的能力；开展计算研究，量化无人机数量、预测时域与move blocking（将控制输入在若干预测步上保持不变以减少独立控制变量）对计算时间与数值可接受性的影响；用PyBullet仿真验证协作到达与追逃，并用实验室实机飞行验证轨迹跟踪接口。

Result: 协作博弈求解器在求解冲突可行轨迹方面存在差异；无人机数量、预测时域与move blocking显著影响计算时间与数值可接受性，move blocking可降低计算时间；计时测量给出测试配置下实时规划的实际上限；仿真实现协作到达与追逃，实机飞行验证了轨迹跟踪接口。

Conclusion: 该规划器可作为后续评估与控制器开发的基础框架。

Abstract: We present and evaluate a game-theoretic model predictive control planner for cooperative goal reaching and adversarial pursuit-evasion with drones. The planner combines 3D prediction, individual objectives, optional shared separation constraints and trajectory-tracking interfaces. A comparison of cooperative game solvers assesses their ability to find feasible trajectories that resolve conflicts. A computational study quantifies how number of drones, prediction horizon and move blocking affect computation time and numerical acceptance. Move blocking holds control inputs constant over groups of prediction steps, reducing the number of independent control variables and lowering computation time. Timing measurements identify the practical limits of real-time planning in the tested configurations. PyBullet simulations demonstrate cooperative goal reaching and pursuit-evasion. A laboratory flight complements the simulations by validating the trajectory-tracking interfaces on physical drones. The planner can serve as a basis for further evaluation and controller development.

</details>


### [109] [Hierarchical Multi-Channel Tip-Over Prediction via Isolated Temporal Models for Planetary Rover Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12261)
*Siddhant Shete,Raul Dominguez,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于隔离时序学习的层次化多通道翻车预测框架：多时域TCN捕捉渐进失稳，GRU前向动力学模型通过预测残差检测突变扰动，经决策级融合与优先级仲裁输出分级安全动作，在Rock框架中以100Hz部署，实现97.58%事件预测覆盖率与1.82s中位数提前量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星车在非结构化地形上作业，通信延迟使人工实时干预不可行，可靠的翻车预防至关重要。现有基于学习的方法通常只优化单一预测目标，难以同时刻画渐进的失稳演化与突发的地形扰动两类失效模式，因此需要互补的多信号预测机制。

Method: 构建层次化多通道预测框架：（1）多时域TCN从IMU惯性测量中学习渐进失稳模式；（2）基于GRU的前向动力学模型预测名义运动，通过预测残差检测意外动态偏差；（3）两路信号经决策级融合与优先级仲裁生成分级安全动作（早期预警、紧急制动）；（4）以Orocos RTT安全组件形式集成于Rock机器人框架，在行星车软件栈上以100Hz运行。

Result: 在跨异构地形采集的IMU样本实验中，系统达到97.58%的事件预测覆盖率，中位提前时间为1.82s，性能优于单一预测通道的对比方案。

Conclusion: 结果验证了将隔离时序模型与层次化决策融合相结合，可为不确定地形下的行星车实时安全提供鲁棒解决方案；多通道互补设计比单目标预测更能兼顾渐进与突变两类翻车风险。

Abstract: Reliable tip-over prevention is critical for planetary rovers operating in unstructured environments where communication delays prevent real-time human intervention. Existing learning-based approaches typically optimize a single prediction objective, limiting their ability to capture both gradual instability evolution and abrupt terrain disturbances. This work presents a hierarchical multi-channel tip-over prediction framework based on isolated temporal learning. A multi-horizon Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) learns progressive instability patterns from inertial measurements, while a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU)-based forward dynamics model predicts nominal rover motion and detects unexpected dynamic deviations through prediction residuals. These complementary signals are integrated through decision-level fusion and priority-based arbitration to generate graded safety actions, including early warning and emergency braking. The proposed system is deployed as an Orocos Real-Time Toolkit (RTT)-based safety component within the Rock robotics framework, operating at 100 Hz on a planetary rover software stack. Experiments on Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) samples collected across heterogeneous terrains demonstrate 97.58% event prediction coverage with a 1.82s median lead time, outperforming individual prediction channels. The results validate that isolated temporal models combined through hierarchical decision fusion provide a robust solution for real-time rover safety in uncertain terrain.

</details>


### [110] [VLA-MOPD: On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Task VLA Policy Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12286)
*Yongxuan Lv,Zhen Fang,Lichen Man,Hao Li,Pu Li,Hengyi Wang,Langfeng Cao,Lin Chen,Jie Huang,Xiu Su,Shan You,Fei Wang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-MOPD：把多个流匹配VLA专家"在策略蒸馏"进同一个学生模型，在观测与ODE中间状态上做逐流步监督，机械臂任务成功率从75.4%升至98.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能要求智能体把语言落地到观测并执行动作，同时保留已有技能。VLA策略提供统一接口，但任务专属后训练往往产生彼此孤立的专家；混合任务强化学习只有稀疏的轨迹级信用分配，离线蒸馏又会偏离学生实际访问的观测，且仅监督终点动作、不监督中间动作生成状态，因此合并专家很难。

Method: 提出VLA-MOPD协议用于合并流匹配VLA专家：先获得任务级或套件级专家，再由共享学生模型采集机器人rollout，在每个访问到的观测与每个ODE中间状态上查询按指令路由的冻结教师；用脱离梯度的逐流步转移目标，通过掩码局部匹配损失监督有效动作块；可选引入参考策略项以抑制其他任务遗忘。

Result: LIBERO上每任务仅一条轨迹起步，平均success-once从75.4%提升至98.9%（端点动作蒸馏为98.15%）；RoboTwin 2.0平均成功率从73.0%提升至89.3%；Piper实机上两轮合并把平均成功率从70.0%提升至90.0%；近似匹配的Spatial适配实验中，参考匹配把Object套件降幅限制在3.0个点，无参考项时为13.2个点。

Conclusion: 在策略、逐流步的教师监督是合并流匹配VLA专家的有效方案，优于端点蒸馏，并能在实机多轮合并中保持已有技能；参考策略项对缓解遗忘至关重要。

Abstract: Physical intelligence requires embodied agents to ground language in observations and execute actions while retaining useful skills. Vision-language-action (VLA) policies offer a shared interface, but task-specific post-training often produces separate specialists. Consolidation is difficult: mixed-task reinforcement learning provides sparse trajectory-level credit, while offline distillation may miss student-visited observations and endpoint distillation does not supervise intermediate action-generation states. We introduce VLA Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation (VLA-MOPD), a protocol for consolidating flow-matching VLA specialists. After acquiring task or suite experts, a shared student collects robot rollouts and queries the instruction-routed frozen teacher at each visited observation and intermediate ordinary differential equation (ODE) state. Detached per-flow-step transition targets supervise valid action chunks through a masked local matching loss; an optional reference-policy term supports retention on other tasks. Starting from one trajectory per LIBERO task, VLA-MOPD raises mean success-once from 75.4% to 98.9%, compared with 98.15% for endpoint action distillation. It improves mean RoboTwin 2.0 success from 73.0% to 89.3%; on a Piper robot, two consolidation rounds improve average success from 70.0% to 90.0%. In a near-matched Spatial-adaptation study, reference matching limits the Object suite decrease to 3.0 points, versus 13.2 without the reference term.

</details>


### [111] [Be My Eye: Learning to See Beyond a Robot's Own Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12295)
*Baifu Huang,Shilin Fang,Jianan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出面向任务的主动上下文共享框架，将机器人观测压缩为紧凑的任务相关表示，在通信预算受限下通过两阶段训练与VLA策略结合，提升多机器人协作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境存在部分可观测性，多机器人协作时单体观测不足；单纯传输原始观测或通用视觉特征效率低，无法体现任务目标与执行进度等关键信息，因此需要按任务需求主动筛选并共享信息。

Method: 两阶段训练框架：第一阶段将机载观测与任务指令联合编码为紧凑上下文表示，由面向任务的未来预测与软任务进度对应关系学习引导，实现与具体协作方式无关的压缩；第二阶段把学到的表示接入预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，实现双向上下文交换，各机器人融合伙伴互补信息与本地观测生成动作，并用动作监督联合优化表示与策略。

Result: 在受限通信预算下，受控实验显示性能显著提升；三项真实世界实验在复杂双机器人任务中验证了框架有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法将被动视觉传输转变为主动、面向任务的上下文共享，直接服务于协作决策，表明信息共享应围绕任务目标与执行进度进行选择性编码。

Abstract: Robots operating in the real world are inherently subject to partial observability, particularly in multi-robot collaboration, where individual observations may be insufficient for effective task execution. A key challenge is leveraging complementary observations from partner robots to support collaborative decision-making. We argue that information sharing should go beyond passively transmitting raw observations or generic visual features. Instead, robots should selectively encode task-relevant information according to task objectives and execution progress. To this end, we propose a task-oriented active context sharing framework that transforms robot observations into compact representations, preserving critical information under constrained communication budgets. The framework adopts a two-stage training paradigm. In the first stage, onboard observations and task instructions are jointly encoded into compact context representations. Task-oriented future prediction and soft task-progress correspondence learning guide this collaboration-agnostic compression, preserving information about task objectives and execution progress. In the second stage, the learned representations are integrated into a pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policy, enabling bidirectional context exchange. Each robot combines complementary partner information with local observations to guide action generation. Action supervision jointly refines the context representations and policy, aligning information sharing with collaborative decision-making. Controlled experiments demonstrate substantial performance gains under constrained communication budgets, while three real-world experiments validate the framework in complex dual-robot tasks. These results highlight a shift from passive visual transmission toward active, task-oriented context sharing that directly supports collaborative decision-making.

</details>


### [112] [ExoStack: A Modular and Reconfigurable Software Framework for Wearable Robotics Using ROS 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12306)
*Debadrata Sarkar,Upamanyu Ray,Soumen Sen,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ExoStack：一个模块化 ROS 2 框架，贯通嵌入式传感—步态解析—助力生成—执行，并在两台气动外骨骼服装上验证复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴机器人依赖从体表传感、步态解算、助力生成到执行器的软件链条，且个性化要求每次测量响应与产生它的助力参数保持绑定；现有实现往往耦合度高、跨嵌入端与主机难以统一，缺乏可复用、可扩展的框架。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：ESA 库将 ESP32 配置为基于 microROS 的传感/执行枢纽；ExoCore 以配置实例化节点形式提供多枢纽同步、步态事件检测、剖面生成、运动规划、代价评估与人机闭环（HIL）优化；PASE 桌面应用负责配置编辑、流程启动与 ROS 2 话题可视化。硬件驱动与算法插件通过既定接口接入，无需修改通用处理节点。

Result: 在三台嵌入式计算平台上刻画了枢纽与规划器的时序性能，并在两台气动外骨骼服装上展示了框架的复用能力。

Conclusion: ExoStack 以模块化、配置驱动的方式统一了可穿戴机器人软件链条，兼顾数据与助力参数的可追溯性及扩展性，具备跨硬件平台与跨样机的可复用性。

Abstract: Wearable robots depend on a software chain that links body-worn sensing to gait interpretation, assistance generation, and actuation. Personalization further requires each measured response to remain associated with the assistance parameters that generated it. This paper presents ExoStack, a modular ROS 2 framework for implementing that chain across embedded hubs and a host computer. Its Embedded Sensing and Actuation (ESA) library configures ESP32 boards as microROS sensing and actuation hubs. ExoCore supplies multi-hub synchronization, gait-event detection, profile generation, motion planning, cost evaluation, and human-in-the-loop (HIL) optimization as configuration-instantiated nodes. The PASE desktop application edits configurations, launches the processing stages, and visualizes ROS 2 topics. Hardware drivers and algorithm plugins can be added through defined interfaces without modifying the generic processing nodes. We characterize hub and planner timing on three embedded computers and demonstrate reuse on two pneumatic exosuits.

</details>


### [113] [UniOMA: Unified Optimal-Transport Multi-Modal Structural Alignment for Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12351)
*Xinrui Zu,Mark Hoogendoorn,Kevin Sebastian Luck,Shujian Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在多模态对比损失上叠加几何感知Gromov–Wasserstein距离正则化的对齐方法，通过动态学习的GW重心减小跨模态结构差异，在五个机器人基准上提升了结构对齐与下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习需要可泛化且良好对齐的多模态表示。已有方法多通过对条件依赖或高阶统计依赖建模来实现模态对齐，但没有显式考虑模态间的结构一致性（structural coherence）。此外，力、触觉等模态数据样本密度不一致，进一步加大了对齐难度。

Method: 在现有对比损失（InfoNCE类）基础上引入基于最优传输的几何感知Gromov–Wasserstein（GW）距离正则项：先为每个模态编码模态内几何结构，再通过动态学习的GW重心（GW barycenter）来缩小跨模态的结构差异，从而在实例级对比对齐之外额外施加结构一致性约束。该策略被应用于包含力与触觉等欠探索模态的机器人感知任务。

Result: 在五个机器人基准上，方法在结构对齐效果与下游性能上均优于当前先进的多模态对比基线，覆盖机器人与环境状态预测等广泛下游任务；且该GW正则项可无缝集成到大多数InfoNCE类目标中。

Conclusion: 显式建模并约束跨模态的模态内几何结构一致性，是对实例级对比对齐的有效补充；基于GW重心的最优传输正则可作为通用插件提升多模态机器人表示学习的对齐质量与泛化性。

Abstract: Achieving generalizable and well-aligned multimodal representations remains a core challenge in robot learning. While recent approaches have attempted to align modalities by modeling conditional or higher-order statistical dependencies, they do not explicitly consider the structural coherence across modalities. In this work, we propose a novel multimodal alignment method that augments existing contrastive losses with a geometry-aware Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) distance-based regularization. To this end, we encode intra-modal geometry for each modality and reduce cross-modal structural discrepancies through a dynamically learned GW barycenter, thereby encouraging structural consistency in addition to instance-level contrastive alignment. We apply this optimal-transport-based alignment strategy to robot perception tasks involving underexplored modalities such as force and tactile signals, where modality data often exhibit varying sample densities. Extensive experiments on five robotic benchmarks show that our method improves structural alignment and downstream performance over state-of-the-art multimodal contrastive baselines across a wide range of downstream tasks, including robot and environment state prediction. Moreover, our GW-based regularization can be seamlessly integrated into most InfoNCE-like objectives.

</details>


### [114] [Safety-Constrained Intelligent Control of a 2-DOF Robotic Manipulator Using Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12378)
*Gabriel da Silva Lima,Made Widhi Surya Atman,Wallace Moreira Bessa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究一种RBF神经网络补偿器，其输出由笛卡尔跟踪控制器与关节空间CBF滤波器共享；通过由“请求力矩与实际施加力矩之差”驱动的辅助状态修正学习信号，降低滤波器干预对自适应学习的直接影响，并在二连杆机械臂上验证其在参数不确定下兼顾跟踪精度与关节、速度、力矩约束的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBF滤波器依赖动态模型，而当模型参数与外部力存在不确定性时模型不准确，导致滤波器性能与安全性受损；同时滤波器对控制量的干预会干扰补偿器的自适应学习过程。

Method: 提出基于RBF神经网络的补偿器，其输出同时供笛卡尔跟踪控制器和关节空间CBF滤波器使用（共享补偿）；引入由请求力矩与实际施加力矩之差驱动的辅助状态，用于修正神经网络的学习信号，削弱滤波器干预对自适应过程的直接影响；在二连杆机械臂上进行约束调节、消融实验、匹配不确定性与扰动试验，以及工作范围与时间性研究。

Result: 实验表明，所提控制器在参数不确定性下提升了跟踪性能，同时保持关节、速度与力矩约束；消融结果说明共享补偿与辅助修正确实各自对性能改进有贡献。

Conclusion: 将神经网络补偿输出在跟踪控制器与CBF滤波器间共享，并辅以基于力矩差异的辅助学习修正，可在模型不确定情形下实现兼顾跟踪精度与约束满足的安全控制，是一种有效的CBF滤波器增强方案。

Abstract: Control barrier function (CBF) filters depend on dynamic models that can be inaccurate when their parameters and external forces are uncertain. This paper investigates a radial basis function (RBF) neural network compensator whose output is shared by a Cartesian tracking controller and a joint-space CBF filter. An auxiliary state driven by the difference between requested and applied torques corrects the learning signal, reducing the direct influence of filter intervention on adaptation. A two-link manipulator is evaluated through constrained regulation, ablations, matched uncertainty and disturbance trials, and operating-range and timing studies. The results demonstrate that the proposed controller improves tracking while maintaining joint, velocity, and torque limits under parameter uncertainty, and that both the shared compensation and the auxiliary correction contribute to this improvement.

</details>


### [115] [AutoVLA-D: Learn a Trajectory-Geometric Condition from Reasoning for Autonomous Driving VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12382)
*Yuguang Yang,Zhewen Tan,Canyu Chen,Linlin Yang,Cheng Chi,Chunyang Liu,Kehua Sheng,Jinyu Yang,Baochang Zhan,Bo Zhang,Yan Wang,Xianbin Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出 TrajCond，将离散的轨迹几何条件（1D-Brohan 差分动作轨迹）引入 VLA 规划，并配合两阶段训练与离散/数值轨迹同步自回归生成（AutoVLA-D），在 NAVSIM、nuScenes、NeuroNCAP 上一致提升连续轨迹预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 VLA 规划通常直接预测连续轨迹，但驾驶指令描述的是"动作"而非自车状态，导致最终连续轨迹缺乏直接的指令条件约束，存在动作语义与轨迹输出之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出 TrajCond——一种离散轨迹几何条件，用 1D-Brohan 差分动作轨迹表示，并通过两阶段训练框架先学习该离散表示，再进行最终连续轨迹预测；解码时同时自回归生成离散轨迹与数值轨迹，方法命名为 AutoVLA-D（Double）。

Result: 在 NAVSIM navtest、nuScenes 和 NeuroNCAP 上的实验表明，离散轨迹作为直接输出时效果较弱，但作为 TrajCond 条件时更有效，在闭环与开环驾驶基准上均能一致地提升最终连续轨迹预测精度。

Conclusion: 离散轨迹的价值不在于直接输出，而在于作为动作级的中间条件引导连续轨迹生成；TrajCond 与两阶段训练、双轨迹自回归的结合为 VLA 驾驶规划提供了一条有效且通用的改进路径。

Abstract: Autoregressive VLA planning usually predicts continuous trajectories as the final output, but driving instructions often describe actions rather than ego states, which weakens direct conditioning on the final continuous trajectory. We address this gap with TrajCond, which introduces a discrete trajectory-geometric condition for VLA planning. Specifically, we represent TrajCond as a 1D-Brohan differential action trajectory and learn it with a two-stage training framework before final continuous trajectory prediction. With the simultaneous autoregressive generation of discrete and numeric trajectories, we name our method AutoVLA-D (Double). Experiments on NAVSIM navtest, nuScenes, and NeuroNCAP show that discrete trajectories are weaker as direct outputs but more effective as TrajCond, consistently improving final continuous trajectory prediction across closed-loop and open-loop driving benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/ShivaTeam/AutoVLA-D.

</details>


### [116] [PCTac: Mechanochromic Photonic-Crystal Tactile Sensor for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12383)
*Ruilin Zhang,Xuanshuo Liu,Junzhe Dai,Kefei Wu,Xuhao Qin,Ziyuan Jiao,Wanlin Li,Yurui Qu,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开源机械致变色光子晶体(PCTac)指尖传感器，以色相场作为触觉观测，直接接入Diffusion Policy，在3个真实机器人任务90次rollout中成功率75.6%，显著优于RGB触觉(66.7%/63.3%)和纯视觉(32.2%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视触觉传感器主要靠成像柔性界面变形（光度立体、深度重建）来感知接触几何，力相关信息仍需通过标定、几何重建或学习映射间接推断。机械致变色光子晶体材料可随载荷改变周期微结构与本征光谱响应，将局部应力直接编码为空间结构色场，但此前很少被用于闭环机器人学习的视触觉传感。

Method: 构建开源的机械致变色PC指尖传感器PCTac，以稠密色相场作为触觉观测；开展加载表征实验验证法向载荷与色相响应的可重复对应关系；将该色相表示直接输入Diffusion Policy策略，并在三个真实机器人操作任务上与传统RGB触觉（PCTac RGB、楔形Wedge RGB）及纯视觉基线对比。

Result: 90次物理rollout中，PCTac Hue成功68次(75.6%)，PCTac RGB 60次(66.7%)，Wedge RGB 57次(63.3%)，纯视觉29次(32.2%)；PCTac Hue在每个任务上均取得最高成功率，在扰动试管插入任务中为16/30，优于RGB触觉基线的13/30及纯视觉的1/30。

Conclusion: 机械致变色结构色可作为紧凑、载荷敏感的触觉表示，有效服务于机器人学习与闭环操作，为视触觉传感提供了一种绕开复杂几何重建与标定的直接物理编码路径。

Abstract: Modern visuotactile sensors primarily sense contact by imaging the deformation of a compliant interface. Using techniques such as photometric stereo, they can recover detailed contact geometry, often represented as surface normals or depth. However, force-related information typically still needs to be inferred indirectly through calibration, geometric reconstruction, or learned mappings. Mechanochromic photonic-crystal (PC) materials offer an alternative physical encoding mechanism: contact loading changes their periodic microstructure and spectral response, directly transforming local stress into a spatially resolved structural-color field. Despite this potential, PC materials have rarely been explored as visuotactile sensors for closed-loop robot learning. We present PCTac, an open-source mechanochromic PC fingertip that uses a dense hue field as its tactile observation. Loading characterization demonstrates a repeatable relationship between the applied normal load and the observed hue response. We further integrate this representation directly into a Diffusion Policy and evaluate it on three real-robot manipulation tasks. Across 90 physical rollouts, PCTac Hue achieves 68 successes (75.6\%), compared with 60 (66.7\%) for PCTac RGB, 57 (63.3\%) for Wedge RGB, and 29 (32.2\%) for vision-only. PCTac Hue also achieves the highest observed success rate on each evaluated task, including Perturbed Test-Tube Insertion, where it achieves 16/30 successes versus 13/30 for tactile RGB baselines and 1/30 for vision-only. These results demonstrate the potential of mechanochromic structural color as a compact, load-sensitive tactile representation for robot learning and closed-loop manipulation. Project page: https://bey0ndt1me.github.io/PCTac-page/.

</details>


### [117] [Bridging Task Planning and Execution for Language-Conditioned Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12409)
*Emanuele Cuzzocrea,Giuseppina Iannotti,Luca Rossini,Arturo Laurenzi,Nikos Tsagarakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个零样本视觉-语言框架，将执行可行性推理直接融入双臂人形机器人任务规划，通过结构化场景表示、分阶段双臂动作与层级验证器，在杂乱环境中兼顾任务语义与物理可行性，并在真机上验证了更高的成功率与更少的重规划查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭辅助与物流等真实场景中环境杂乱，机器人需同时推理任务目标与执行可行性；双臂人形平台还需协调两个操作臂带来的交互与运动学约束，现有方法往往割裂规划与执行，缺乏对执行可行性的显式建模，且失败后倾向于全量重规划、代价高。

Method: 构建结构化场景表示，融合语义物体关系与执行约束；据此选择障碍物搬移与执行选项，保持任务语义不变。将兼容动作组织为带显式前置/后置条件的并行双臂阶段；引入层级验证器，在感知不确定性下评估条件，并利用阶段级执行历史支持局部恢复或定向重规划。整个框架为零样本视觉-语言方法。

Result: 在CENTAURO轮腿混合人形机器人上针对九类语言条件操作场景实验，任务成功率高于非执行感知基线；在专门设计的失败场景中，层级验证可在比"总是重规划"更少的模型查询次数下从执行失败中恢复。

Conclusion: 将执行推理直接嵌入任务规划能带来实质收益：既提升杂乱环境下的双臂操作成功率，又以更低的查询成本实现失败恢复，说明规划与执行一体化的设计对真实机器人操作具有价值。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly expected to execute natural-language manipulation tasks in real-world environments. This is particularly relevant in household assistance and logistics, where clutter requires reasoning about both task objectives and execution feasibility. This challenge becomes even more pronounced for bimanual humanoid platforms, where coordinating two manipulators introduces additional interaction and kinematic constraints. We present a zero-shot vision-language framework for execution-aware bimanual manipulation that jointly reasons about task objectives and physical feasibility. Planning is grounded in a structured scene representation that combines semantic object relations with execution constraints, enabling the selection of obstacle relocations and execution choices while preserving task semantics. Compatible actions are organised into parallel bimanual stages with explicit pre- and post-conditions. A hierarchical validator evaluates these conditions under perceptual uncertainty and uses stage-level execution history to support local recovery or targeted replanning. Experiments on the CENTAURO hybrid wheeled-legged humanoid robot across nine language-conditioned manipulation scenarios show higher task success than a non-execution-aware baseline. In dedicated failure scenarios, hierarchical validation recovers from execution failures with fewer model queries than always replanning. These results demonstrate the benefits of combining execution reasoning directly into task planning. Videos and additional material are available on the Project Webpage: https://advrhumanoids.github.io/bridging-planning-execution/.

</details>


### [118] [V3DSAM: Semantic-Geometric Loop Verification and Geometry-Anchored Persistent Instance Mapping for Feed-Forward Dense SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12452)
*Maolin Ye,Dongqing Zhao,Linyang Li,Shuai Xue,Yuan Liu,Yiyuan Wang,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: V3DSAM是一个开放词汇语义密集SLAM系统，结合VGGT-SLAM 2.0的前馈几何估计与SAM3的开放词汇语义观测，通过场景级与物体级的物理对应关系解决长序列前馈密集SLAM中的跨视角关联不可靠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列前馈密集SLAM中跨视角关联不可靠。现有方法依赖骨干网络内部特征和时间维度的ID传播，缺乏鲁棒的长期关联能力。

Method: 系统融合VGGT-SLAM 2.0的前馈几何估计与SAM3的开放词汇语义观测。提出语义-几何双尺度回环验证，利用像素级双向重投影和物体级3D空间对应识别假回环，无需访问骨干内部特征；并提出几何锚定的持久实例建图，在优化后的全局坐标系中通过类别约束的3D连通性定义物理实例，无需帧间身份传播即可分配全局ID。

Result: 在TUM RGB-D评测中验证器拒绝了全部四个假回环候选；长序列ScanNet实验显示实例身份一致性得到提升。

Conclusion: V3DSAM降低了对骨干内部特征和时间ID传播的依赖，实现了鲁棒的长期跨视角关联。

Abstract: To address unreliable cross-view association in long-sequence feed-forward dense SLAM, this letter presents V3DSAM, an open-vocabulary semantic dense SLAM system that establishes physical correspondences at both the scene and object levels. The system combines feed-forward geometric estimates from VGGT-SLAM 2.0 with open-vocabulary semantic observations from SAM3. We introduce semantic-geometric dual-scale loop verification, which identifies false loop closures using pixel-level bidirectional reprojection and object-level 3D spatial correspondences, without accessing backbone-internal features. We further propose geometry-anchored persistent instance mapping, which defines physical instances through class-constrained 3D connectivity in an optimized global coordinate system and assigns global IDs without inter-frame identity propagation. The proposed verifier rejects all four false loop candidates in our TUM RGB-D evaluation, while long-sequence ScanNet experiments show improved instance identity consistency. Thus, V3DSAM reduces dependence on internal backbone features and temporal ID propagation for robust long-term cross-view association.

</details>


### [119] [Galilean Robots, Aristotelian Sensors: Joint Spatiotemporal Trajectory Estimation and Extrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12526)
*Abhishek Goudar,Jonathan Kelly,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出用特殊伽利略群（机器人轨迹）与亚里士多德子群（传感器外参）统一建模多传感器系统的联合轨迹估计与时空标定，把时间偏移与运动的耦合内嵌于群结构，从而无需单独参数化连续时间轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将空间外参（刚体变换）与时间偏移（标量）分离建模，时间偏移需通过运动补偿、插值或连续时间轨迹在平移时间上求值，机制割裂、实现繁琐。

Method: 以特殊伽利略群表示机器人轨迹、亚里士多德子群表示传感器外参，并推导twist驱动与IMU驱动两类运动先验；同时考虑理想与非理想时钟；在仿真与真实数据上使用相机、位置、测距三类量测进行验证。

Result: 在多种异构传感器配置下，标定精度与轨迹估计性能与现有成熟方法相当。

Conclusion: 该框架在不牺牲性能的前提下简化了时空标定的建模与实现，是相较现有方法的一种易用性提升。

Abstract: Multi-sensor state estimation requires accurate spatial and temporal calibration between heterogeneous sensors. Existing spatiotemporal calibration methods typically represent sensor extrinsic parameters as a rigid-body transformation together with a scalar temporal offset, causing the spatial and temporal parameters to enter the estimation problem through separate mechanisms. While the spatial transformation composes directly with the robot pose, accounting for the temporal offset requires evaluating the trajectory at a shifted time, typically through motion compensation, interpolation, or a continuous-time trajectory representation. In this work, we formulate joint trajectory estimation and spatiotemporal calibration using the special Galilean group for the robot trajectory and the Aristotelian subgroup for sensor extrinsics. This representation incorporates the coupling between motion and temporal offset directly through the group structure, avoiding the need for a separately parameterized continuous-time trajectory. We develop twist- and IMU-driven motion priors, accommodate both ideal and non-ideal clocks, and evaluate the framework with camera, position, and range measurements. Simulated and real experiments demonstrate calibration and trajectory-estimation performance comparable to established methods across heterogeneous sensor configurations. Our simplified framework therefore offers a quality-of-life improvement over existing methods without compromising performance.

</details>


### [120] [Modeling and Estimation of a Towed Marine System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12568)
*Josef Matouš,Simon Hoff,Damiano Varagnolo,Marius S. Rundhovde,Kristin Y. Pettersen,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对由两艘自主水面航行器、脐带缆、拖曳鱼和尾环缆组成的拖曳海洋系统，建立集总质量模型并基于Lyapunov方法验证其稳定性，随后仅利用ASV船载传感器测量提出降阶观测器与完美测量扩展卡尔曼滤波(PMEKF)两种状态估计方法，并通过仿真与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拖曳海洋系统（ASV-缆-拖曳鱼-尾环缆）的完整动力学建模与状态估计具有挑战性：缆绳与拖曳鱼动态复杂、难以直接测量，而实际系统通常只能获取ASV自身的船载传感器信息，因此需要在保证计算可处理性的前提下实现可靠的状态估计。

Method: 采用集总质量(lumped-mass)方法对缆绳和拖曳鱼建模，以在保留关键动力学的同时保持计算可行性；通过基于Lyapunov的分析验证模型的稳定性与收敛性；在该模型基础上，利用仅来自ASV船载传感器的测量，设计降阶观测器和完美测量扩展卡尔曼滤波(PMEKF)两种状态估计器。

Result: 仿真与实验结果表明，所提出的降阶观测器与PMEKF能够基于有限的ASV船载测量有效估计拖曳系统状态，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 集总质量建模结合Lyapunov稳定性分析与基于ASV测量的状态估计方案，可为双ASV拖曳海洋系统提供可行且有效建模与状态估计框架，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper addresses the modeling and state estimation of a towed marine system consisting of two autonomous surface vehicles (ASVs), a pair of umbilical cables, towfish, and a trailing loop cable. Inspired by the existing literature, we employ a lumped-mass modeling approach for the cables and towfish, capturing the essential dynamics while maintaining computational tractability. A Lyapunov-based analysis is conducted to verify the stability and convergence properties of the proposed model. Building on this model, we propose two state estimation methods, namely a reduced-order observer and a perfect-measurement extended Kalman filter (PMEKF), using only the measurements from the ASVs' onboard sensors. Numerical simulations and experimental results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [121] [Correctness Boundaries of Typed Plans for Natural-Language Robot Fleet Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12654)
*Artem Tuzov,Dmitrii Vizitei,Evgenii Shlomov,Senya Belykh,Anastasia Malakhova,Alexander Maloletov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文考察自然语言机器人集群控制中“计划校验与执行报告”究竟能证明什么，指出通过校验和执行报告并不等于满足原始指令，需将有效分配、目标与报告结果对照原始命令核查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人集群自然语言控制研究常以“计划通过校验”或“任务报告显示完成”作为成功依据，但二者可能掩盖分配重复、目标偏移、编队形状错误、报告漏报等问题，因此需要明确这些证据的实际证明力。

Method: 在基于ROS 2的三层架构与20台仿真机器人上，将命令经由类型化计划、确定性纠错、集群协调与结果检查进行追踪；构建44个用例的离线基准检验计划结构与部分命令要求；在五种无响应模式的本地配置中进行多轮运行，并选取18次仿真系列对照位姿任务条件；通过保存的计划与日志分析重复取货分配、任务区内目标调整、编队轨迹与到达报告，并用搬运任务记录同一机器人的取货与卸货区域存在情况。

Result: 无响应模式时五种配置的响应通过率为70.0%–87.3%；选定18次仿真系列的全部运行均满足基于位姿的任务条件，但该系列包含两次在软件变更后替换不完整运行的重复实验；日志显示存在重复取货分配与目标调整；编队轨迹中出现结构上被接受但编队形状错误、路线被拒绝，以及重规划后到达报告遗漏所请求机器人的情况。

Conclusion: 计划校验与执行报告只能证明有限层面的性质，无法保证原始命令的语义被满足；用例区分了可执行动作的改变与完成报告所覆盖要求的改变，因此应把有效分配、目标与报告结果同原始命令进行核对。

Abstract: We examine what plan validation and execution reports establish in natural-language robot fleet control. Commands are traced through typed plans, deterministic corrections, fleet coordination and outcome checks in a three-level Robot Operating System 2 implementation with twenty simulated robots. An offline benchmark of 44 cases checks plan structure and selected command requirements. Across five local configurations without a response schema, 70.0-87.3% of responses pass. All runs in a selected 18-run simulation series meet their pose-based task conditions. This series includes two successful repeats that replaced incomplete runs after a software change. Saved plans and logs document duplicate pickup assignments and goal adjustments within the original task zones. Separate formation traces show a structurally accepted plan with the wrong formation shape and a rejected route, and an arrival report that omitted a requested robot after replanning. Carry completion records pickup and subsequent drop-off zone presence for the same robot. The cases distinguish changes to executable actions from changes to the requirements covered by a completion report. They motivate checking the effective assignments, goals and reported outcome against the original command.

</details>


### [122] [VLA Adaptation with Real and Simulated Data: A Case Study on Robotic Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12676)
*Matěj Kripner,Milan Straka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在"按方格名称在棋盘上移动棋子"的精密操作任务上，系统研究了VLA模型微调中的数据规模、训练步数、检查点选择与仿真数据利用等实际问题，并给出若干可操作结论与一个负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型需针对每个下游任务和机器人单独微调，实践者面临"需要多少数据与训练更新、如何选择检查点、能否用仿真数据降低真机采集成本"等缺乏明确答案的问题。

Method: 构建需精确操作与空间理解的任务：SO-101机械臂按给定方格名称（如e4、a5）在棋盘上移动棋子。用不同数据规模与更新预算微调π_{0.5}，数据包括遥操作演示与由随机化脚本专家生成的仿真演示（模仿人类演示的多样性）；真机上最多用256条遥操作演示微调，并以每条25次试验评估10个策略。

Result: 仿真中成功率随数据量与更新次数增加而提升；短暂的学习率降温（cool-down）可带来最高31个百分点的提升，且是检查点比较的必要条件；验证损失与动作误差不能可靠用于模型选择。真机部分结果显示，仿真演示预训练帮助显著，在真实数据稀缺时尤为明显。负面结果：使用策略自身rollout的过滤行为克隆、RECAP式优势条件、自动教练纠正或残差强化学习，均未较普通行为克隆带来可测量提升。

Conclusion: 为VLA微调的实践决策（数据量、更新预算、检查点选择、仿真预训练）提供了具体经验证据，并公开仿真器、专家与真实演示数据；同时指出在本文设定下多种进阶训练方法相对行为克隆无明显收益。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are trained as general-purpose foundation models for robot learning, and require fine-tuning for each downstream task and robot. This raises practical questions such as how much data and training updates are needed, how checkpoints should be selected, and whether simulation can be leveraged to alleviate the cost of real data collection. We study these questions on a task that requires both precise manipulation and spatial understanding of the scene. In this task, an SO-101 arm moves a chess king between two squares that are given only by their names, such as e4 and a5. We fine-tune $π_{0.5}$ with various dataset sizes and update budgets, using teleoperated demonstrations as well as simulated demonstrations from a randomized scripted expert that imitates the variability of human demonstrations. In simulation, the success rate scales with both data and updates. Importantly, we find that a short learning-rate cool-down adds up to 31 points and is necessary for comparing checkpoints, and that validation loss and action error are unreliable for model selection. On the real arm, we fine-tune on up to 256 teleoperated demonstrations and evaluate ten policies in 25 trials each. Partial results indicate that pretraining on the simulated demonstrations helps substantially, most clearly when real data is scarce. We also present a negative result, in which training on the policy's own rollouts with filtered behavior cloning, RECAP-style advantage conditioning, corrections from an automatic coach, or residual reinforcement learning did not yield a measurable improvement over plain behavior cloning in our setting. We release the simulator, the expert and the real demonstrations.

</details>


### [123] [Library of Hands: Fix the Hands, Not the World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12678)
*Hasaan Ahmad,Qizhen Ying,Yuxuan Sun,Tianjin Huang,Guangming Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的Library of Hands (LoH)：保持世界模型冻结，仅通过冻结模型从图像变化中"读出"命令到实际动作的映射（称为hand）并存储，从而在相机或控制器变化时快速适配。闭环测试中三个仿真任务成功率从28.6%提升至62.3%，无变化时仅损失0.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型+规划器范式中，相机移动或控制器更换会使同一命令产生不同实际动作（如命令被旋转90度时，冻结规划器的成功率从77.5%骤降至7.8%）。现有修复手段需重训练、需数据，或在环境回归时遗忘旧设置，缺乏免训练且可复用、可记忆的适配方案。

Method: LoH不改动世界模型，只校正"命令→实际动作"的映射（hand）。它不依赖额外传感器，而是借助冻结的世界模型，从两帧相机图像的变化中反解出最能解释该变化的动作作为一次"读数"（reading）；识别到的每个hand都被存入库中，使回归的设置可被直接识别而非重新学习。

Result: 闭环实验中，三个仿真任务的成功率由28.6%提升到62.3%（提升超过一倍），且环境不变时性能仅下降0.6个百分点；方法可原样迁移到另外两种世界模型，并在三个真实机器人数据预训练模型中的两个上有效；其增益随读数的质量提高而增大。

Conclusion: LoH表明：仅校正动作映射层、保持世界模型冻结，即可在无需训练数据与重训练的前提下实现跨相机/控制器的鲁棒适配，并具备对回归环境的记忆能力与较好的通用性。

Abstract: A world model predicts how a scene changes after an action, and a planner uses it to search for actions that reach a goal. If the camera moves or the controller changes, the same command moves the robot differently: when its commands are turned by 90 degrees, a frozen planner with the LeWM world model that succeeds in 77.5% of episodes succeeds in only 7.8%. Current fixes retrain the model, need training data, or forget a set-up when it returns. We propose Library of Hands (LoH), a training-free method that keeps the world model frozen and corrects only the map from commands to applied actions, which we call the hand. LoH reads the hand through the frozen model, with no extra sensor: it finds the action that best explains the change between two camera images (a reading). It stores every hand it identifies, so a returning set-up is recognised rather than re-learnt. In closed-loop tests, LoH more than doubles success on three simulated tasks, from 28.6% to 62.3%, and loses only 0.6 percentage points when nothing changes. It also works, unchanged, with two other world models and on two of three models pretrained on real robot data, and its gain grows with the quality of its readings.

</details>


### [124] [Sliding into Balance: Benefits of a Sliding Payload for Load Distribution and Disturbance Rejection in Dual-UAV Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12754)
*Jorge Vicente-Martinez,Francisco José Mañas-Álvarez,Edgar Ramirez-Laboreo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了双无人机吊运同一根绳索上可滑动载荷的协同运输问题，建立了含粘性摩擦的平面无量纲动力学模型，并与固定载荷构型对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双机吊运采用两根独立绳索连接载荷，易出现绳索松弛与张力不均问题。作者希望考察单绳跨接、载荷可滑动的构型能否改善张力分配、扩大可达范围与抗扰能力。

Method: 建立载荷沿单绳滑动的平面无量纲动力学模型（含粘性摩擦）；在无人机静止假设下，与等总绳长的固定载荷构型进行平衡态与扰动下的对比分析，并进行含真实阵风剖面的动态仿真。

Result: 滑动载荷在任意无人机相对位置下都能均衡两段绳索张力，平衡时无绳索失张；载荷可达区域扩大一倍；除低摩擦且无人机间距较大的情形外，可容许扰动集合均增大；阵风仿真中两机间平均张力不平衡较固定载荷方案降低。

Conclusion: 滑动载荷单绳构型在张力均衡、可达范围与抗扰性能上总体优于固定载荷构型，是一种有潜力的双机协同吊运方案，但低摩擦、大间距工况下需注意其扰动容许性下降。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the cooperative transportation of a payload suspended from two drones in which, instead of being attached to two independent ropes, the payload is allowed to slide along a single rope connecting both UAVs. First, a planar nondimensional dynamical model of the sliding motion with viscous friction is presented. Then, considering stationary drones, this configuration is compared with a fixed payload with the same total rope length, both at equilibrium and under disturbances. The analytical results show that the sliding payload equalizes the tension in both rope segments for any relative position of the drones, so that no rope loses tension at equilibrium; it doubles the region reachable by the payload and, except for low friction combined with a large drone separation, it enlarges the set of admissible disturbances. Finally, a dynamic simulation also shows that under realistic gusty wind profiles, the average tension imbalance between the drones is reduced with respect to the fixed-payload solution.

</details>


### [125] [Benchmarking Real-World Robotic Manipulation through Stochastic Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12804)
*Elias Gläßer,Gülhan Şikaroğlu,Pavlo Kupyn,Marc Pollefeys,Zuria Bauer,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"随机挑战"（stochastic challenges）这一机器人基准测试新范式：基于可复现的真实世界场景，但任务样本从同一分布中随机抽取，从而兼顾系统级可复现性、跨平台可比性与隐藏测试集的严谨性和意外性，并在一个移动操作新基准上比较了两家实验室的两台机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作（manipulation）研究的关键瓶颈是真实环境下方法的可复现性与可比性。操作是交互式任务，无法用静态观测数据集评估；仿真是常见替代方案，但无法覆盖真实世界测试的全部复杂性。

Method: 提出随机挑战范式：借鉴 YCB 物体等可复现真实场景的思路，构建可复现的真实世界场景；同时利用随机性，从同一分布中抽取随机任务样本，模拟隐藏测试集的严谨与意外。基于该范式实现一个移动操作（mobile manipulation）新基准。

Result: 在该基准上对分别测试于两个不同实验室的两台机器人进行了对比，说明若任务样本来自同一分布，可通过其在随机任务样本上的成功率来比较不同实验室的不同机器人。

Conclusion: 随机挑战可作为机器人基准测试的通用范式，兼顾真实世界可复现性与隐藏测试集式严谨评估，支持跨平台、跨实验室的系统级比较。

Abstract: A challenging bottleneck in manipulation research is reproducibility and comparability of methods in real-world environments. Since manipulation is an interactive task, it cannot be evaluated on a static dataset of observations. Benchmarking in simulation is a common remedy, but cannot cover the whole complexity of real-world tests. This paper proposes a new paradigm of stochastic challenges. Like the well-known YCB objects, it is based on reproducible real-world scenes and therefore allows system-level innovation and comparability between different platforms. Due to their stochastic nature, our challenges however, mimic the rigor and surprise of hidden test sets. To this end, we show how different robots in different labs can be compared based on their success rate over different random task samples, if these samples come from the same distribution. We propose stochastic challenges as a general paradigm for robot benchmarking and implement this paradigm in a novel benchmark for mobile manipulation, on which we compare two robots tested in two different labs.

</details>


### [126] [Learning Heterogeneous Tactile Representation for General Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12806)
*Bing Han,Yichuan Peng,Haoyu Dong,Yuanmeng Hu,Yongyi Jia,Fangjing Wang,Kecheng Zheng,Yiming Zhang,Yanhong Zeng,Xiaoqing Zhang,Chen Song,Rundong Zou,Anqi Dong,Yuan Guan,Ka Leong Cheng,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Nan Xue,Wei Ning*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 LingBot-Touch，一个触觉基础模型，通过跨传感器统一表征将触觉理解从传感器特定物理中解放出来，生成即插即用的触觉 token，可接入扩散策略与 VLA 类策略模型，在仿真与真实机器人上提升复杂操作能力并保持计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉是接触密集型灵巧操作不可或缺的模态，但商用触觉传感器发展尚不成熟，各类传感器各有不可替代的优势与缺陷（物理特性不统一）。因此核心挑战是让触觉理解摆脱传感器特定物理限制，使具身模型能有效利用异构触觉数据，从而通过跨传感器数据规模化触觉学习。

Method: 提出触觉基础模型 LingBot-Touch，具备三点设计：（i）对压阻式、磁式、视触觉等不同触觉传感器产生统一表征，并易扩展至新传感器类型；（ii）生成的触觉 token 为即插即用形式，可无缝集成到不同范式的策略模型中（扩散策略、π_{0.5}、LingBot-VLA 等 VLA 模型）；（iii）在仿真与真实机器人、多硬件平台上开展大量实验验证。

Result: 实验表明，该统一触觉表征能显著提升复杂操作任务的表现，且在不同策略范式中一致地增强操作灵巧性，同时保持良好的计算效率；跨仿真与真实部署、多硬件平台均得到验证。

Conclusion: 研究结果展示了利用跨传感器数据规模化触觉学习的潜力，为未来触觉硬件设计与具身智能模型设计提供了有价值的启示。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is an indispensable modality for contact-rich dexterous manipulation, a capability that hints at the ultimate frontiers of embodied intelligence. However, commercially available tactile sensors are still at a relatively early stage of development, with each type of sensor offering irreplaceable strengths alongside its own drawbacks. A key challenge is thus to free tactile understanding from sensor-specific physics, so that embodied models can effectively leverage heterogeneous tactile data. To this end, we develop LingBot-Touch, a tactile foundation model characterized by the following properties. (i) Our model yields a unified representation across different tactile sensors (i.e., piezoresistive, magnetic, and visuotactile sensors), and is easily extensible to new sensor types. (ii) The resulting tactile tokens are plug-and-play: they can be seamlessly integrated into policy models across paradigms, from diffusion policy to vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $π_{0.5}$ and LingBot-VLA, consistently enhancing manipulation dexterity. (iii) Extensive experiments, spanning simulation and real-robot deployments across multiple hardware platforms, demonstrate that our tactile representation substantially benefits complex manipulation tasks while maintaining strong computational efficiency. We believe the findings in this work demonstrate the potential of leveraging cross-sensor data to scale tactile learning, and offer encouraging insights for the design of future hardware and embodied models.

</details>


### [127] [Simulating Vein Compressibility for Ultrasound-Guided Femoral Access: Speed, Accuracy, and Phantom Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12818)
*Amit Manicka,Laurent Coulon,Darwin Ang,Michael Loushin,Timothy M. Kowalewski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文评估在NVIDIA Isaac Sim（Healthcare版）中用单GPU物理仿真模拟超声引导股血管穿刺时的组织形变与血管塌陷，验证其速度与精度能否满足机器人交互与强化学习训练需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声引导股动脉穿刺时，探头压迫会造成组织形变并改变血管解剖结构，机器人感知到的解剖与真实不同；仿真器需快速复现这些力学过程，但实现"静脉先于动脉塌陷"等临床有意义信号所需算力尚不明确。

Method: 在Isaac Sim for Healthcare中采用Stable Neo-Hookean组织模型，以Vertex Block Descent在单GPU上求解；先对几何与力学参数已知的低保真圆柱形台式模体进行机器人加压仿真，再在不同单元尺寸、时间步与求解迭代下考察精度与耗时；最后以文献中的真实力学参数构建数字腿部模型，检验静脉与动脉的塌陷差异。

Result: 台式模体仿真在各深度上对动脉预测误差均在5个百分点内，在25.4 mm按压的前11 mm内静脉误差在3个百分点内，并复现了低保真物理模体的塌陷行为；计算时间随求解工作量线性增长而精度不再提升；选定参数使每帧求解时间从29,700 ms降至30.4 ms（约976倍），与最精细参考设置相差仅1.03个百分点；纯物理求解30.4 ms/帧，含超声与图形渲染的完整流程为56.4 ms/帧；数字腿模型在各设置下静脉受压程度均远大于动脉。

Conclusion: 基于物理的Isaac Sim仿真能够以实时交互速度复现血管塌陷等关键临床信号，平台或可用于增强自主股动脉穿刺的强化学习训练或在线仿真。

Abstract: In ultrasound-guided femoral access, probe pressure deforms the tissue and changes the vascular anatomy a robot observes. A simulator must reproduce these mechanics fast enough for robotic interaction, yet the computational effort needed for a clinically meaningful signal like vein collapse before artery is unclear. We test the feasibility of a physics-based simulation in NVIDIA's Isaac Sim for Healthcare, with Stable Neo-Hookean tissue solved by Vertex Block Descent on one GPU. A simulation of a low-fidelity cylindrical benchtop phantom with known geometry and mechanical properties, pressed by a robot, predicted its artery within 5 percentage points at every depth. It matched the vein within 3 points through 11 mm of a 25.4 mm press and followed the collapse behavior of the low-fidelity physical phantom. Across element size, time step, and solver iterations, wall clock time per frame scaled linearly with solver work, but accuracy did not improve with more work. The chosen setting reduced solver time per frame by a factor of 976, from 29,700 ms to 30.4 ms, while remaining within 1.03 points of the finest reference setting. The physics solver alone took 30.4 ms per frame, while the full stack, including ultrasound and graphics rendering, took 56.4 ms per frame. Finally, when seeded with realistic literature-based mechanical properties, the digital leg model compressed the vein far more than the artery at every setting, reproducing the expected clinical signal of vein collapse before artery, which is used to distinguish them. In total, these results suggest that the NVIDIA Isaac Sim platform may be suitable for augmenting reinforcement learning training or online simulation for autonomous femoral access with ultrasound.

</details>


### [128] [DreamLander: A 6-DOF World Model based Reinforcement Learning Planetary Lander](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12824)
*George Nehma,Patrick Quinn,Madhur Tiwari,Camilo Riano-Rios*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DreamLander，一种基于DreamerV3的模型驱动强化学习算法，用于为真实的6自由度进入、下降与着陆(EDL)问题生成最优制导与控制轨迹，实现高样本效率、强泛化性，着陆误差在2米内、速度范数小于0.3 m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软着陆在太空探索与科学推进中愈发重要，需高精度、安全地投送关键载荷；但行星着陆燃料严重受限，对制导轨迹与控制的最优性提出很高要求，而传统最优轨迹生成过程高度复杂。

Method: 以DreamerV3为基础构建模型驱动强化学习算法DreamLander，针对真实的6自由度EDL问题生成最优制导与控制；该方法构建样本高效且高泛化的世界模型，并在多种场景中复用；同时使用状态传感器观测，向端到端EDL方案迈进。

Result: DreamLander在不同场景下保持高精度，着陆点误差在指定着陆区2米以内，速度范数低于0.3 m/s，并展现出良好的样本效率与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 模型驱动强化学习可有效简化复杂的最优制导轨迹与控制生成过程，DreamLander为真实6自由度EDL问题提供了高精度、可泛化的解决方案，并为端到端EDL方法奠定基础。

Abstract: Soft powered descent landings are becoming evermore important in the field of space exploration and scientific advancement, because of their ability to deliver mission critical payloads, both highly accurately and safely. The high efficiency restriction on planetary landings due to the severely limited fuel supply results in the need for optimal guidance trajectories and control. Model based reinforcement learning is proving to be an important tool in simplifying the highly complex process of generating optimal guidance trajectories and control. In this work, we present DreamLander, a state of the art model based reinforcement learning algorithm based on DreamerV3, to generate optimal guidance and control for a realistic 6 degree of freedom entry descent landing problem. The model based approach creates a sample efficient and highly generalizable model that can be applied to a variety of scenarios whilst maintaining a high degree of accuracy, landing within 2 meters of the designated landing zone, and with velocity norm less than 0.3 meters per second. DreamLander makes use of state sensor observations, making progress toward End-to-End solutions of the Entry Descent Landing problem.

</details>
